Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(rem)
Vol.3

Chapter 3745
In the 77th chaos era, Heavenly Lord Laojun, Heavenly Lord Yuanshi, and other human heroes had already made a judgment.
To fight against the End, it would take about nine million godly jun of strength.
That was a conclusion under limited conditions.
The result proved that the original number was so naive.
In the following chaos eras, many dominators wanted to complete the Path of the End, and the numbers they obtained varied.
Among them, the Tianzhi Civilization made an accurate calculation and obtained a number far greater than the human race, 160 million godly jun of strength … but this result was still not comprehensive.
According to the information provided by Elder Xue, it would take about ten million godly jun of strength to fight against the End.
After all, restarting a chaos into a new reincarnation cycle was to provide energy for the Four Pillars, and the restarting process would not consume too much energy.
However, the chaos world was different from all other subworlds.
The Snake Spirit Gate had modified this world, and it would take an astronomical number to complete the Path of the End. According to Elder Xue, there was an incomprehensibly powerful existence behind the Snake Spirit King, so the power needed to complete the Path of the End was about infinite … so halfway through Elder Xue's dream, Taishang Laojun already understood that the only way to stabilize the chaos world was to get rid of the Snake Spirit King.
This was an answer that bordered on despair.
Even the Human Spirit King, Monkey Spirit King, and Bird Spirit King had lost to the Snake Spirit King. Let the humans or other races of the subworld fight against the Snake Spirit King?
Those dominators who wanted to complete the Path of the End did not know how powerful the opponent they were facing was. If they knew, they would not have any thoughts of fighting back! However, after Taishang Laojun finished the dream, he knew that there was a chance.
Elder Xue had a way to carry the Origin God Blood.
After the disaster in the main realm, all living beings were wiped out. None of them, including the Snake Spirit Gate, could return to the main realm! However, the immortal painting scroll was a smooth passage.
Elder Xue gave this difficult task to Taishang Laojun, asking him to choose a Nirvana Realm expert to obtain the Origin God Blood … Taishang Laojun had already completed this seemingly impossible task.
Even facing the Snake Spirit King, Taishang Laojun was calm. He had already done his best! Faced with Taishang Laojun's reprimand, the snake masters were all stunned. Some of them even stole a glance at the bified-headed serpent.
Was this old man telling the truth?
The bifid serpent maintained its posture and looked down at the human elder.
After a while, the Spirit Snake King's voice sounded in the Biheaded Serpent's mind.
"Kill him."
The two-headed serpent also said, "Get rid of them!"
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh swoosh …" The snake masters had been prepared for a long time. Once the bified-headed serpent gave the order, they charged madly towards Taishang Laojun.
Perhaps they were still confused and doubtful.
However, the King was not to be doubted. The confusion, doubts, and even uneasiness in their hearts could only be suppressed at the bottom of their hearts.
Taishang Laojun narrowed his eyes and raised his hand.
"Rise!"
"Rumble …" The thick and heavy Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda flew up from the ground.
"Suppress!"
Boom!
The few Snake Sovereigns who were charging at the front were directly smashed into pieces by the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda.
"Light of Annihilation …" "Hum …" The bright white light enveloped the torn Treacherous Bodies. Combined with the killing power of the Black Yellow Pagoda, several Snake Masters were killed.
"Child's play, die!"
Bone spikes grew out of the bifid serpent's body as it joined the fray.
In the Divine Clock City.
Yu Shenfeng was sitting cross-legged under the huge bell.
He was trying to break through to the twentieth level of the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda.
The bell had already rung, and Yu Shenfeng had already entered the Nirvana Realm.
As soon as he entered, he realized that he had already reached the twentieth level. He was secretly delighted.
However, at this moment, the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda shook violently. Yu Shenfeng even saw four people gathered at the top of the pagoda! The news of Hua Tianming meeting the "Pagoda Master" had already spread like wildfire. Yu Shenfeng naturally knew about it too.
He wanted to try his luck in the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda. If he met the Pagoda Master, perhaps he could ask for something good?
He did not expect to meet the so-called "Pagoda Master" but four young people instead! "You …" Yu Shenfeng had just opened his mouth when Du Zhen turned to look at him and pointed at him coldly. "Get out!"
An irresistible force was exerted on Yu Shenfeng. Yu Shenfeng only felt his vision blur before he was directly sent out of the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda! The bell was still ringing. Only half the time had passed. When the other Nirvana Realm cultivators saw Yu Shenfeng come out so quickly, they thought he had already chosen the Nirvana token.
"Shenfeng, what's wrong?"
"Junior Brother, did you get the 20th step Nirvana token?"
Gu Bei and Jing Wuhuan came over.
Yu Shenfeng still looked confused. He shook his head. "I didn't get it."
"Why?
You didn't get to the 20th step? "
Gu Bei asked curiously.
Logically speaking, with Yu Shenfeng's strength, climbing to the 20th step should not be difficult. It should be very safe.
"I saw four people …" Yu Shenfeng said again.
"Four!"
Jing Wuhuan was slightly shocked.
"But one of them chased me out!"
Yu Shenfeng looked aggrieved. He did not even have time to speak! Kubei furrowed his brows, "Other than those four people, did you see any other movements?"
"The Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda shook violently. I don't know anything else." Yu Shenfeng shook his head.
The Ultimate Graveyard had many guesses about the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda. One of them had already been confirmed by Hua Tianming. The Master of the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda was most likely a human. At the very least, he would be on the human's side.
This could be seen from the special treatment Gu Bei received when he entered the Ultimate Graveyard, and the changes after the divine realm entered the graveyard! "According to Li Luoshui, the human race is not considered a strong force in the Nirvana Realm. They will definitely encounter strong enemies. I hope they can get through this safely," Gu Bei said worriedly.
The Nirvana Realm cultivators in the Ultimate Graveyard relied on the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda. If this pagoda was gone, everyone's cultivation would not improve.
"Let me go in and try," Jing Wuhuan volunteered.
He suspected that his junior brother spoke too carelessly and offended the people in the pagoda … Then, Jing Wuhuan took out his fishing rod and swung it violently. He was about to ring the bell.
But before he could touch the bell, it started ringing on its own.
"Dong." The bell let out an unprecedented roar. Even the region outside the Divine Bell City could hear it clearly.
But when the bell reached the end, the sound became loud and sharp. Even the Nirvana Realm cultivators could not help but cover their ears.
"Crack!"
The sound stopped abruptly.
A crack appeared in the middle of the bell, and the face of the bell curled up.
If not for the fact that so many Nirvana Realm cultivators saw it with their own eyes, they would have thought that the bell had been destroyed by someone.

Chapter 3746
When the Divine Clock City's Nirvana Realm cultivators saw the broken bell, they were all stunned.
They stared blankly at the bell, unable to believe what they were seeing.
The surroundings were extremely quiet.
Jing Wuhuan was the first to react. He quickly said, "It wasn't me … I didn't ring the bell!"
This bell was the lifeblood of many Nirvana Realm cultivators!
If Jing Wuhuan took the blame for breaking the bell, he would be wronged.
"I saw it. It definitely wasn't you who broke it. Senior Brother, you don't have the ability …" Yu Shenfeng said.
"But why did the bell break?"
"Isn't this cutting off our cultivation path?"
"I still want to advance to the third stage …"
When the Nirvana Realm cultivators realized this, they all went crazy. This was their only way to advance.
Kubei walked forward and reached out his hand to caress the big bell. His expression became abnormally serious.
When Yu Shenfeng entered, he already realized that there was something wrong with the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda. Then, the bell was destroyed. The Mysterious Yellow Pagoda was probably doomed!
"Let me try!"
A Nirvana Realm cultivator walked to the broken bell.
This Nirvana Realm cultivator came from the Jiang Yun Clan, which was also a large clan in the Ultimate Cemetery.
The destruction of the bell would not only affect the human race. The other Nirvana Realm cultivators would also feel terrible.
The Jiang Yun Clan member walked to the bell. His hands hung down and his head was raised. This was their cultivation method.
"Buzz …"
Green light appeared on top of his head. As he silently chanted the True Meaning of Dao, he struck the bell.
"Crack!"
The bell let out a sound like a broken gong.
"Buzz …"
The green light on top of the Jiang Yun Clan member's head dimmed. He shook his head at Kubei. "We can't go in …"
"Is it really broken?"
"What should we do!"
"Wuwuwu …"
The Nirvana Realm cultivators' voices were already sobbing.
After the Jiang Yun Clan member, a few groups of people rushed to the bell to try. Without exception, they all failed.
Even so, the living beings in the Ultimate Cemetery did not give up.
The few powerful human beings gathered together, trying to find a way to fix the bell.
The simplest and most direct method was to repair the bell. The human race mobilized dozens of skilled craftsmen from the God Realm to straighten the curled edges of the bell and carefully fill in the gaps with metal …
After three days and three nights, the bell returned to its original state, and they tried to ring it again.
In order to prevent the bell from being broken again, a First Heaven of the Nirvana Realm rang the bell.
"Ka, ka, ka …"
The big bell was obviously fine, but no matter how he struck it, he couldn't make the same sound as before, nor could he return to the Black Yellow Pagoda of Heaven and Earth.
It was only then that their hopes of repairing the bell were dashed. They realized that the bell would never ring again …
…
In Clear Jade Sky, inside Sky City.
The Spirit Snake King's long tail extended in front of it, and the black light embedded in the tail constantly flickered.
"Why don't you let him finish?"
Snake Spirit King asked.
"His words are meaningless," the voice came from the Snake Spirit King's mouth as the black light flashed.
"Is this world really irreversible?"
Snake Spirit King asked again.
"I've already shown you. How can you believe the words of a mere human?"
Black Light replied.
"If it's reversible, why do we have to wait for so long? More than a thousand chaos eras, even if it's calculated according to the time of the Main World, it's still a long time. We … still have to wait, or even wait until the next chaos era?"
Snake Spirit King asked again.
Before this, Snake Spirit King had never dared to talk to Black Light like this, and it didn't even dare to ask about Black Light's origin.
After all, everything it had now was given to it by Black Light, and Black Light could also take away everything it had.
According to the original plan, Snake Spirit King still had to wait for a long time.
However, a series of troublesome things had happened recently, especially Black Light's refusal to let it kill the Spirit Bird King, which made Snake Spirit King doubt and waver in its heart.
It realized that it couldn't delay any longer.
Black Light was silent for a while, and then said, "In that case, you go and activate the Land of Extinction, and I'll light the black lamp to upgrade it."
The crescent-shaped golden light in Snake Spirit King's eyes flashed fiercely. It had been waiting for Black Light to say this.
According to the original plan, Snake Spirit King would let the Yuan Ling civilization support You Xiong, Shennong, and the other races to completely control the mother world. After completing the path of order, it would wait leisurely, as long as the chaos world was under its control.
However, now that the Evil Deities had unified the path and the mother world was under its control, the situation had become extremely complicated!
"I'll activate the Land of Extinction now …" Snake Spirit King said without hesitation.
…
There were two famous Danger Zones in the chaos. One was the Ultimate Cemetery, and the other was the Land of Extinction.
No matter how powerful an existence was, as long as they fell into these two Danger Zones, there would be no way out …
Thankfully, the Sinkhole Graveyard and the Deathless Burial Grounds floated aimlessly in the depths of the chaos. Most living beings would never leave their homeworlds. Aside from a few races living in the chaos who would accidentally wander into the Deathless Burial Grounds, most people would never come into contact with them in their entire lives.
"Whoosh …"
Looking at the Land of Extinction from afar, it looked like a perfect triangle. Its surface was greenish-black, and it floated silently in the chaos.
The Land of Extinction was very wide inside, but it was very crowded for the creatures inside. This was because there were thousands of wild races here.
"Chirp chirp chirp chirp …"
A flock of red-eyed black birds flew across the black sky. They'd just finished a hunt, and their claws were still stained with the thick scent of blood. They planned to return to their nests after a full meal.
However, thin claws suddenly extended out from the ground. These claws wrapped around the Red-eyed Black Bird at lightning speed. They squeezed, tore, and strangled the Red-eyed Black Bird, squeezing out the blood from its body.
"Whoosh …"
Waves of blood rained down from the sky.
Large mouths dozens of meters wide grew on the ground. These mouths opened with all their might, feasting on the blood rain that fell from the sky. At the same time, they let out excited cries …
This was a demonic world.
Even in the endless chaos of the Land of Extinction, there were still rules.
Although these wild races fought and killed each other, they didn't dare to provoke the existence. It was a serpentine statue that stood in the center of the Land of Extinction.
Any existence that dared to approach the statue would be instantly killed. There had never been an exception in countless years.
Over time, that place became a forbidden area. Most of these wild races were immersed in killing each other. Who would pay attention to the origins of this statue?
However, the statue suddenly moved today.
The black snake coiled on top of it moved, and slithered down as if it were alive.
"Hiss …"
"Ever since I created this Land of Origin, I've never interfered with it. Now it's so chaotic …"
The Spirit Snake King could no longer bear to watch after smelling the thick scent of blood in the Land of Extinction.
It controlled the black snake to fly up. A crimson light shot out from the snake's eyes, and a powerful will spread out along with the red light, descending upon the Land of Extinction.

Chapter 3747
The thousands of barbaric races in the Extinction Ground had no idea what was going on.
These barbaric races were not afraid of anything, but they began to tremble under this terrifying will.
"Rumble …"
A thunderous voice reverberated through the Extinct Burial Grounds.
The barbaric races saw three white lightning bolts appear at the edge of the world. Any creature that was in the middle of these three white lightning bolts turned into ashes immediately. They did not even have time to scream.
At the same time, the voice of the Serpent King was heard in this world.
"Do you want power?"
The Serpent King asked.
Although these black barbaric creatures were terrified, they were filled with admiration after sensing the power of the Serpent King.
"If only I had such power …"
"We can control everything!"
"Invincible power can make me stronger!"
Now that they heard the Serpent King's words, they were all excited. Every barbaric race was filled with fighting spirit.
"I want such power!"
"Master Serpent, will you give it to us?"
"I will pursue such power …"
The Serpent King was no exception to these barbaric races.
They had been living in a cruel world without morals or order. As long as they had power, they would pay any price.
"Pipa!"
The three white lightning bolts intersected to form a huge snake-shaped totem. The size of this totem was so dazzling that the entire Extinction Grounds could see it clearly.
"Go to the totem!"
"There, you'll obtain unprecedented power …"
After the snake totem was set up, the huge Extinction Ground began to change shape.
From each of the three sides of the triangle, there was a crack with intersecting canine teeth. It was as if a three-dimensional triangle had three monster mouths.
After doing all this, the Serpent King's consciousness returned to the Jade Clear Sky and said to the Black Light, "The Extinction Ground is ready."
"Then let's begin. Let the blood lamia activate the black lamp," the Black Light replied.
"Lamia …" The Serpent King's eyes flashed coldly.
After a few breaths.
On the wall of the Chaotic World, the woman embedded in the wall suddenly opened her eyes. She was the blood lamia that the Black Light had mentioned.
The two crescent moons in her beautiful eyes glowed. "Father, is that you …"
"Activate the Ominous Orb," the Serpent King's cold voice sounded in her ears.
"You're still so stubborn?"
Lamia asked.
"Hahaha …" The Snake Spirit King let out a series of laughter. "The biggest reason I let you live is to let my daughter see who is right. Believe me, you will live to see that day!"
The Lamia remained silent for a moment and did not argue further.
Over a thousand chaos epochs ago, she had tried to fight back. Because of that, she had been imprisoned until now.
At this moment, she was actually looking forward to seeing her father's face filled with despair and helplessness …
"My father, I will open the ominous bead as you wish!"
"Buzz …"
As soon as the Lamia finished her words, the ominous bead on top of her head started to spin. Streaks of bloody light started to gather.
"Huff huff huff …"
An unprecedented amount of energy surrounded the ominous bead. The surging energy formed tide-like waves, pushing away the surrounding chaotic power wave after wave.
One thousand Shen Jun …
Ten thousand Shen Jun …
One million Shen Jun …
Ten million Jun …
The power contained in the ominous bead increased exponentially.
Among the ten saint items of the Serpent Spirit Sect's Holy Master, only this ominous bead could obtain unlimited energy from the Holy Pillar.
The terrifying energy was compressed in this tiny bead. If this energy were to explode, even the Mother World would not be able to withstand it. It was enough to smash a huge hole in the Mother World and destroy thousands of continents!
"Go, light up the black lamp …" The Lamia sneered.
"Pop!"
The ominous bead released a streak of bloody light and flew through the boundless chaotic world.
The terrifying energy was completely compressed into a finger-thick streak of bloody light and shot upward at an incredible speed.
"Whoosh —"
At this moment, Li Shan was still flying through the chaotic world at high speed.
That extremely powerful streak of bloody light shot upward like a meteor and swept across thirty thousand kilometers on one side of Li Shan.
If Li Shan's speed was even a bit slower, he might have been pierced by this thin streak of bloody light.
Because the energy contained within the bloody light was too terrifying, it alarmed all the major powers on Li Shan even though they were thirty thousand kilometers away.
Nuwa, Fuxi, and Luo Zheng — the three Primal Chaos Elder Gods — all looked toward the side of Li Shan. They could not see the streak of bloody light, but they could sense an irresistible energy flying past.
"What's that?"
"How could there be such energy in the chaotic world?"
"It's too fast, I can't see it clearly …"
This power appeared abruptly and disappeared in an instant.
After a few breaths of time, the bloody light shot toward the top of the chaotic world and struck the thick ceiling.
"Crack!"
The ceiling was smashed open by the bloody light, revealing a small lamp.
It was a small black oil lamp.
The oil lamp was simple and unadorned. The four wooden legs were engraved with complicated patterns. Inside was a glazed cup with a thin wick floating in the center.
Without the small oil lamp, this streak of bloody light's energy would have been enough to pierce through the ceiling, but the small oil lamp had actually endured all of the bloody light's energy.
To be precise, the small oil lamp had completely absorbed the bloody light's energy.
"Bzzz …"
The bloody light revolved around the small oil lamp, finally converging at the wick.
"Fuse!"
A crimson flame lit up.
If the experts of the Four Spirit Gates saw this small oil lamp light up, they would definitely feel an indescribable sense of terror.
In the past, the Snake Spirit Gate had lit up this small oil lamp and destroyed everything in the master realm …
For the sake of survival, the Human Spirit Gate and Bird Spirit Gate had been forced into the chaotic world by this small oil lamp.
Now, this lamp had been lit up once more.
Soon after, countless crystals filled with the profound power of space were released from the small oil lamp, spreading down from the top of the chaotic world.
When the crystals touched the chaotic clouds, the chaotic clouds began to grow restless.
These massive clouds silently floated within the chaotic world. Unless they were absorbed, their form would never change.
But now, after the crystals merged with the chaotic clouds, they began to rapidly absorb chaotic energy.
The chaotic clouds, which were originally a trillion kilometers in size, were split into tens of thousands of pieces by the crystals, and they quickly condensed into different shapes!
These shapes were the heads of enormous, nameless beasts.
Their faces were similar, and they all had crazed expressions on their faces.
As the crystal expanded in the Chaotic World, the number of strange beast heads formed after absorbing the Chaotic Energy was innumerable.
They floated quietly in the Chaos, their eyes closed and motionless.

Chapter 3748
In the boundless Chaos, there were some unique Chaos creatures. The Chaos Winged Beasts were one of them.
These Chaos Winged Beasts were ferocious and lived in groups. Anything that approached their nests would be attacked fiercely.
Their nests were like giant cages, drifting in the Primal Chaos.
These nests were also living creatures. They had extremely special and sensitive senses that allowed them to sense other Chaos creatures from a long distance away.
When they sensed other Chaos creatures, the nests would emit a faint stench. This stench would guide the Chaos Winged Beasts to find their target.
If the nests sensed a powerful existence, they would emit a strong stench. This was a warning to the Chaos Winged Beasts to avoid powerful creatures that lived in the Chaos.
"Phew …"
When those crystals fell and absorbed them, they turned into huge heads. The interior of the nests was filled with a strong stench. It was like the stench of a corpse that had been rotting for days.
The Chaos Winged Beasts that stayed in the nests broke down when they smelled this stench. They all came out of the nests.
This stench reminded the Chaos Winged Beasts that there was an extremely terrifying existence around them …
When the Chaos Winged Beasts flapped their wings and rushed out of the nests, they were all stunned.
They saw numerous gigantic heads floating in the air. Although these heads were motionless, they still felt a strong sense of danger!
"Wu wu …"
"Wu wu wu …"
The Chaos Winged Beasts made sounds to communicate.
Every living creature had their own way of survival. Otherwise, they would have gone extinct.
The Chaos Winged Beasts did not attack these heads rashly. Instead, they sent dozens of Chaos Winged Beasts to observe.
These Chaos Winged Beasts carefully approached the heads and looked around them.
The heads did not emit any aura of life. They closed their eyes tightly and were like huge rocks floating in the air. They were dead creatures.
"Wu, wu …"
One of the Chaos Winged Beasts cried out for a while and got closer to the head. It reached out its sharp claws and touched the head gently.
Unexpectedly, the moment it touched the head, the head opened its eyes.
Those huge eyes emitted rays of sunlight. Then, the head opened its mouth and tried to swallow the Chaos Winged Beasts.
The speed of the Chaos Winged Beasts was not slow. When they sensed danger, they flapped their wings and transformed into streaks of black light to escape. But the big mouth sucked. The Chaos Winged Beasts were unable to resist. They were all sucked into the big mouth.
To the awakened head, these Winged Beasts of Chaos were not even qualified to fill the gaps between its teeth. Its gaze quickly locked onto the nest of the Winged Beasts of Chaos. These nests were quite heavy when they were joined together. The head did not hesitate and swallowed them in one gulp.
However, the head still felt "hungry". Its motive for appearing was to devour everything that could be devoured, and then continue to strengthen itself. What in this empty bastard world was worth devouring?
Soon, it set its sights on its own kind.
"Phew …"
It flew toward the other head and took a big bite out of the other head. The head that was bitten also woke up, so the two heads started to tear at each other, trying to devour each other.
After tearing at each other for a while, one of the heads was completely devoured by the other, and the size of the surviving head immediately doubled.
However, the head still felt hungry. Soon, it locked onto the next head. With the advantage of its size, it was easier for it to devour other heads. Thus, it began its journey of devouring.
The heads that were spread throughout the chaos woke up in various forms. The same scene kept repeating as they devoured each other in a frenzy.
The heads that had awakened first stood out from the billions of heads and snowballed into a snowball to strengthen themselves.
However, the ones that grew the fastest in the chaos world were not these heads. Instead, it was the one located in a corner of the chaos.
"The Land of Annihilation."
From the moment the heads were born, the three "mouths" at the edge of the Land of Annihilation began to devour them. Every time the Land of Annihilation swallowed a head, the Land of Annihilation would grow.
When the Land of Annihilation swallowed all the heads around it, its body had expanded by more than ten times!
As the Land of Annihilation continued to grow, the speed at which it devoured the heads also increased. The three mouths opened wide and devoured the heads like a whale swallowing a whale!
However, the Land of Annihilation was still too small. Although it was a place of origin, the Serpent King had never paid much attention to it. Even though it had expanded by more than ten times, it was still not as big as a continent in the Mother World.
…
When the crystals descended around the Mother World, they turned the chaos clouds around the Mother World into giant heads.
When these heads woke up, they did not make their companions their first target. That was because there was a huge Mother World next to them. This was the perfect target for the heads. They swarmed toward the Mother World without hesitation.
The outer walls of the Mother World were several hundred miles thick. Although the heads were devouring at a fast speed, it would still take a while for them to enter the Mother World.
Inside the Mother World, Evil Deities floated at the top of a mountain. In front of him was a ball of Daofuse energy. Although the Daofuse energy was only the size of a fist, it was actually in a highly condensed state. If it exploded completely, it would be enough to destroy several large continents.
After Li Shan successfully escaped, Evil Deities created clones of himself in all the large continents in the Mother World. He paid a visit to the various major factions in the Mother World and announced that he was the ruler of the Mother World.
As early as when the Evil God released the evil intent that permeated the entire motherworld, the various superpowers and major forces had already speculated that the motherworld might already have a master, and the appearance of the Evil God had confirmed their speculations.
These major factions did not have the ability to separate a large continent from the Mother World like Li Shan did. Faced with Evil Deities who came knocking on their door, they had no other choice but to submit.
However, other than making them submit, Evil Deities did not do anything else. To him, these existences in the Mother World were no different from ants. Even if there were a few Immortal Realm experts from the major factions, they were just slightly stronger ants.
When Li Shan left the Mother World, the giant ape that suddenly appeared had been lingering in his mind. For this reason, he asked the old captain to ask the Prime of Tongtian about the origin of the giant ape.
However, he did not know what happened on the other side. The Prime of Tongtian and the others seemed to have disappeared and did not give him a reply.
He could still rely on himself …
After realizing that there was such a terrifying person in this world, Evil Deities became even more obsessed with the pursuit of power.
On one hand, he wanted to further deepen his control over the Mother World. On the other hand, he needed to comprehend the Power of Truth from the Daofuse energy.
His control over the Mother World was gradually increasing. It was not difficult for Evil Deities to completely control everything in the Mother World. However, Evil Deities encountered some unsolvable problems when comprehending the Daofuse energy.
In a short period of time, he had already comprehended the embryonic form of the Power of Truth from the Daofuse energy.
The six embryonic forms corresponded to the six Chaos Ancient Gods. However, other than the Power of Truth, Evil Deities caught a trace of something unusual in the Daofuse energy.
Because of this, Evil Deities pondered for several days.
Not far away, Xiuzhu walked down from the flying ship and put a black cloth robe on Evil Deities. She had sewn it herself.
Evil Deities looked at the Daofuse energy in front of him and murmured.
"The power of the Daofuse energy is not just the Six Great Truths. It should be stronger, thousands of times stronger than it is now. Why is it so weak in my hands? It shouldn't be …"
Looking at her husband's expression, Xiuzhu could not help but shake her head.
Her husband was already the ruler of the Mother World. Even if the leaders of those superpowers had already submitted, he was still obsessed with power. She did not know whether this was a blessing or a curse.
Just as Evil Deities was concentrating on comprehending the Daofuse energy, he suddenly felt a trace of uncomfortable movement from the Mother World. Evil Deities frowned. His pupils reflected the crazily gnawing heads on the outer wall, and his face suddenly revealed a puzzled expression.
"What is this …"

Chapter 3749
The Tianwu Continent was located at the top of the Mother World. It was the largest exit from the Mother World to the Primal Chaos World.
In the past, the Tianwu Continent's exit was very prosperous. All the flying ships of different races used this exit to trade. After all, traveling through the Primal Chaos World was much faster than passing through the crystal walls.
However, after the Evil Deities took control of the Mother World, they issued a ban. No race was allowed to leave the Mother World without permission. Violators would be killed without mercy.
Most of the trade in the Mother World had stopped. Even if they wanted to trade, they could only use the Mother World's waterway.
At the huge exit of the Tianwu Continent, there were tens of thousands of flying ships.
The people in charge of these flying ships were very depressed. Every day they stayed here was another day of losses. They could only watch as those shining white god crystals slipped away.
"I wonder how long the ban will last!"
"Who knows? Our patriarch said that the Mother World is now under the control of that human called Evil Deities. Whenever he says …"
"Why! Are all the Immortal Realm experts in the Mother World dead?
How can a human junior be so arrogant? "
Some people were indignant.
In their opinion, the Immortal Realm expert was the peak expert in the Mother World. The latecomers would at most be at the Saint Soul Realm. How could they control the Mother World?
"Shh!"
An alien at the side made a hand gesture. "Don't talk nonsense. I heard that Evil Deities are everywhere …"

"Hum …"
The enchantment on the surface of Mount Li disappeared.

These bamboo shoots flew up from all directions and drilled into the bottom of Mount Li before firmly binding Mount Li to the ground.
The Land of Origin would continue to expand and might even encounter some unpredictable dangers. To prevent Mount Li from rolling around inside, the Origin Divine Blood came up with this countermeasure and firmly fixed Mount Li.
"Luo Zheng, what's your situation now?"
Fuxi approached Luo Zheng and asked.
"Me?
What's my situation? "
Luo Zheng was somewhat puzzled.
"You are now in your own inner world. Are you an inner body incarnation?"
Fuxi asked with a smile.
Nuwa, Donghuang, and the rest were also curious about this question. They all looked at Luo Zheng.
In theory, this was Luo Zheng's inner world, but Luo Zheng was now in his own inner world …
It was like a big cup containing a small gourd, and the small gourd contained the big cup. This created a logical paradox. Was it the cup that contained the gourd, or was it the gourd that contained the cup?
Luo Zheng was facing the same situation.
"This is not an inner body incarnation …" Luo Zheng shook his head. "This is!"
With a slight thought, an inner body incarnation identical to Luo Zheng condensed. The aura of this inner body incarnation was exactly the same as Luo Zheng's.
Then, a second, third, and fourth inner body incarnations appeared one after another …
In a short while, there were hundreds of inner body incarnations standing side by side with Luo Zheng. Each inner body incarnation had extraordinary strength.
"Wow," Fuxi exclaimed.
Even though Fuxi's prowess had increased tremendously, it was still difficult for him to deal with just one incarnation, let alone the fact that Luo Zheng could easily summon over a hundred incarnations. Without using any skills, they could easily crush Fuxi to death.
"It seems that we are destined to become members of Luo Zheng's inner world …" Donghuang sighed from the side.
Nuwa, on the other hand, had an indifferent look on her face as she said, "The Chaos World has reached this stage, and the battles within it have already far exceeded our imaginations. For Li Mountain to have the protection of this world is already a blessing."
"Li Mountain is lucky to have this world as its protection."
"It is lucky to have this world as its protection."
"It is lucky to have this world as its protection."
Luo Zheng stood to the side, listening attentively to Nuwa's words.
But all of a sudden, he felt a strange fluctuation, and Nuwa's words were repeated several times in his ears.
"Luo Zheng, what's wrong?"
Nuwa could see that Luo Zheng was a little uncomfortable.
Luo Zheng shook his head. "Nothing …"
Then, he waved his hand lightly, and all the inner body incarnations dispersed.
With Luo Zheng's current state, it was impossible for him to be hearing things. Perhaps it was because he had summoned too many inner body incarnations?
But just as he dispersed the inner body incarnations and looked up, he saw afterimages all over the sky.
There were dozens of Nuwa standing beside Nuwa.
There were dozens of Li Mountains standing beside Li Mountain …
The entire world was filled with afterimages!

Chapter 3754
These afterimages were very real and didn't seem to be illusions.

Fuxi, Nuwa, Donghuang, and the others gathered around, looking concerned.

Compared to Fuxi, Nuwa trusted Luo Zheng more.
If Luo Zheng said that it wasn't an illusion, then it definitely wasn't an illusion …

"Indefinite signs, Luo Zheng, what you said might be indefinite signs …" At this moment, Ji's voice suddenly floated over.

Ji, who controlled the truth of life, had his own definition of life.

According to the legends passed down in the Mother World, the Sealing Stone was a stone that fell from the Hourglass of Time. By relying on the Sealing Stone, one could calculate the lifespan of the Primal Chaos World.
When Luo Zheng first came to the Mother World, he had once fought for a Sealing Stone to fuse into his body. But after inheriting the Origin God Blood and rebuilding his fleshy body, both the Sealing Stone and the Spirit of Chaotic Origin had disappeared.
"It might be possible that another Sealing Stone appeared in the Mother World. And your current attainments in the Dao of Space are comparable to Kong. You can sense the ripples in the time sequence and see those Indefinite Images," Ji said.
This explanation was more reasonable.
Luo Zheng had grasped the Origin God Blood. His attainments in the Dao of Space were not any weaker than the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods. When the Sealing Stone descended, he saw the Indefinite Images.
But what exactly were the Indefinite Images? Kong had no answer. It was even more impossible for Luo Zheng to find the answer now.
"Compared to these Indefinite Images, I am more concerned about the End Ruins Graveyard," Nuwa suddenly said.
The End Ruins Graveyard was also a world floating within the Primal Chaos. This world would also be attacked by the heads.

Luo Zheng clapped his hands. His eyes were filled with worry.
According to their previous plan, the moment Li Shan found the inner world, they would change their direction and search for the End Ruins Graveyard!
By relying on that Mother Butterfly, Luo Zheng could easily sense the location of the Land of Origin and the End Ruins Graveyard.
If it was before, Luo Zheng would not be in a rush. He had sufficient time to find the End Ruins Graveyard.
But now, the End Ruins Graveyard was facing the attacks of those heads. He did not know if they could survive this calamity …
The expressions of Fuxi and the others turned grim.
Li Shan had three great Primal Chaos Ancient Gods and several Sempiternal realm experts. It would not be easy for him to deal with those heads.
The End Ruins Graveyard did not even have any Sempiternal realm experts. How could they deal with the attacks of those heads?

Northwest of the Primal Chaos.
The End Ruins Graveyard floated there quietly.

Chapter 3756

Chapter 3757

Outside the Ultimate Cemetery …

The human heart was very subtle.

On one hand, they were suspicious of each other, and even of the human race. On the other hand, they had all made plans to escape.
Once the Sinkhole Graveyard was completely breached, they would gather their remaining forces and flee.

The Sinkhole Graveyard was indeed protected by a mighty figure!

Powerful human beings and non-humankind beings all moved out in full force.
As long as a head approached, it would be killed before it could sprout to prevent it from devouring and growing stronger.

But on the fourth day, the situation changed.
The number of heads had increased …
Among the heads that invaded the Sinkhole Graveyard, there were many heads that were ten or twenty times the size.
Although the clansmen attacked with all their might, there were still some that escaped, and many of them even died in the mouths of the heads.
Once these heads entered the Sinkhole Graveyard, they could grow like snowballs through devouring. This was also why they were so terrifying.
On the fifth day, hundreds of heads that were a hundred times the size had appeared in the Sinkhole Graveyard, and there were even two heads that were a thousand times the size …
The Nirvana Realm powerhouses of the various races fell one after another, and many of the Saints in the divine territory also died!
If the two heads that were a thousand times the size were allowed to continue devouring, they would grow to be even bigger than the Sinkhole Graveyard in a day at most!
But even Hua Tianming was powerless against such a colossus. With the help of dozens of Nirvana Realm powerhouses, Hua Tianming attacked one of the heads that was a thousand times the size, but the result was not satisfactory.
Although he heavily injured the head that was a thousand times the size, almost half of the Nirvana Realm powerhouses died!
The situation in the Sinkhole Graveyard fell into danger again …
Hua Tianming stood in the void and stared at the colossal head in the distance. He held his lone sword in front of him and asked, "Senior, is there really no other way for the Sinkhole Graveyard?"
Taishang Laojun did not respond.
The answer was obvious …

That colossal head could be seen from every corner of the graveyard. The old, young, women, and children hiding in the depths of the graveyard looked at that colossal creature and began to panic.
The divine territory that was originally quietly docked in the graveyard began to activate.
After Luo Zheng left, the divine territory ship was controlled by the reincarnated Yuan Chengdao.
Yuan Chengdao was also paying close attention to this calamity in the Sinkhole Graveyard. Seeing that the Sinkhole Graveyard was about to be devoured, he naturally would not sit still and wait for death.
Once the Sinkhole Graveyard was broken, he would drive the divine territory and rush out immediately …
Another two hours passed.
The colossal tombstone was devoured, and the head that was ten thousand times the size grew again, becoming twenty thousand times the size.
Despair spread in the hearts of every living creature.
"Senior, if I burn my lone sword, can I cut off that head?"
Hua Tianming asked.
Burning the Nirvana token could unleash a great power.
If the lone sword was burned, it would be even more unimaginable.
However, it would attract the punishment of truth …

Taishang Laojun still did not respond.
If it was time to burn the Nirvana token, it would mean that the Sinkhole Graveyard was completely hopeless.

If it was time to sacrifice himself, he would not hesitate in the slightest.

In the depths of the chaos, he could see something the size of a soybean.

Chapter 3758

Inside the sun in the Divine Dominion.

Mu Xuerong, who was not far away, looked like she had been struck by lightning.

If Luo Zheng returned one day, she hoped that she would be able to surpass him …

Chapter 3760

Taking control of the Land of Origin was indeed an unexpected gain, and it was a success.
Evil Deities frowned, "Let me correct you, it's not you who took control of the Mother World, it's me, this is my world."
Han Ge glanced at Evil Deities.
Before it descended, the Prime of Tongtian had advised it to be careful.
Evil Deities was very rebellious, not to try to completely control it, and to give Evil Deities more patience and respect.
Han Ge was the kind of existence that could take things and let them go.
Even if it was the high and mighty Bird Lord, in front of a lowly human, it could still condescend and let go of the arrogance in its heart. Han Ge smiled faintly, "That's true, now the Wu Kong Clan and even us need to rely on you."
Evil Deities was a little surprised by the Bird Lord's attitude. Evil Deities asked, "What kind of existence are you? You don't seem to be living creatures, but you have extremely powerful strength, especially that giant ape …"
The scene of the giant ape slapping Li Shan away was still replaying in Evil Deities' mind.
"We are existences that are one level higher than you, and we are also the creators of the Chaotic World. After we die, we will become what we are now. You can see us as souls, but in the Nirvana Realm, we are called Eerie," Han Ge said.

"What about you?
You have a king too? "
Evil Deities asked again.

Chapter 3766

"I once defeated a little black snake in the consciousness of the mother world, did that come from the Snake Spirit Sect?"
Evil Deities asked.

In theory, only Evil Deities could enter that place. What kind of ability did this little black snake have to be able to force its way in?

Evil Deities had always regarded Luo Zheng as his ultimate opponent. Now, this ultimate opponent had suddenly become the so-called Snake Spirit King. This made him feel a little regretful.

Tens of thousands of Evil Deities thought at the same time and quickly came up with a new plan.
Three huge rifts were opened at the edge of the Mother World. Each rift spanned hundreds of continents. At the same time, a huge array was set up in the continents within these rifts.

The plan implemented by Evil Deities was exactly the same as the Snake Spirit King's plan!
This plan was progressing very smoothly, but during this time, Evil Deities had been depressed.

Evil Deities glanced at him faintly, "What do you know?"

Chapter 3767
In the depths of the Chaos.
A huge triangular cube was spinning at a slow speed.
The area behind the triangular cube was empty. The heads that were scattered there had been swallowed up.
Now, it had entered a new world.
After clearing up the surrounding heads, three beams of light extended from the three big mouths!
The energy contained in the beams of light was weak, but very stable. It could extend for billions of miles in the Chaos.
Although the heads were stupid, they had an extraordinary sensitivity to energy. Even if the beams of light passed by them, the heads were like sharks that smelled blood, crazily moving after the beams of light.
When the number of heads following the beams of light was enough, the distance between the beams of light would rapidly shrink. The heads would also move forward with the shrinking beams of light, and finally enter the "big mouth" of the triangular cube. In this way, the heads within billions of miles would be cleared in one go!
The triangular cube maintained an extremely high efficiency in swallowing the heads. In a short time, it had expanded by a thousand times.
Although it was still very small compared to the Mother World, the speed of its growth was astonishing.
On the other side of the Chaos, Luo Zheng's method of using the Land of Beginnings was almost the same.
Previously, Luo Zheng asked Nuwa to spread Nuwa's Shadow out and use the energy beam of Nuwa's Shadow to attract the heads. This method was very effective at first.
As the Land of Beginnings continued to expand, Nuwa's Shadow was not very useful.
In theory, Nuwa's Shadow could extend almost infinitely. Given enough time, Nuwa could pull Nuwa's Shadow from one end of the Chaos to the other.
The problem was that Nuwa's Shadow extended too slowly …
According to the Origin God Blood, there would be a round of competition after the descent began. The bigger one would have a great advantage.
If they wanted to absorb more heads faster, they had to think of a better way.
After Nuwa's Shadow's plan was abandoned, Luo Zheng gathered everyone on the Jade Platform to brainstorm.
They needed to find something that could spread fast and far.
"Light," Ji quickly gave the answer.
Not only did light contain energy, but it could also spread rapidly in the Chaos!
Luo Zheng entrusted the Origin God Blood to transform the Land of Beginnings again.
Soon after, a huge six-sided array appeared on the surface of the Land of Beginnings. This six-sided array could not be used to defend against enemies. It could only release strong light.
"Buzz …"
After the powerful energy was injected into the six-sided formation, it scattered in all directions in the form of strong light.
It was as if there was a huge sun in the chaos, and the blazing white light could affect hundreds of millions of miles away. When the heads in this range felt the energy in the strong light, they rushed to the place of origin without stopping …
The three Lands of Genesis in the Chaos had all groped their way out of the chaos and began to grow in unique ways.
Although the speed at which they absorbed the skulls wasn't slow, and the speed at which they grew was even more exaggerated.
But how big was the Chaos World?
Given their size, it would take a long time for them to grow to the size of Chaos.
…
One month later.
Several humans appeared at the entrance of the Night Vision Island, which was located in the Shang Qing Sky.
The person in the lead was wearing a large and thick fur coat. His face was handsome, and there was a trace of heroic spirit in his eyes.
Behind him were three women and two men.
Just as the group approached the pool in the cave, two monkey lords emerged from the pool.
"Stop right there."

The entrance of the Night Vision Island was of great importance. Under normal circumstances, no living beings were allowed to approach it.

The reason why they restrained themselves was because this group was of the human race.
After all, the monkey lords had interacted with the human race many times over the years. In addition, Luo Zheng had saved the Monkey Spirit King, so the monkey lords had changed their view of the human race.

Tongtian?

The coordinates were Shifu's life.
This person came with Luan Gu's life!

When he had just climbed to the second level of Three Worlds, Tongtian knew that he was extraordinary. He also knew that this kid could change the situation, but he did not expect that he could affect the balance between Shifu and Shifu!

Tongtian's eyes could not help but twitch.

Chapter 3768

Tongtian's face immediately darkened. It would be fine if you said this to me, but you said it to the Luan Fox!

Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes!"
The Luan Fox gritted its teeth and said.

However, this brat could easily catch its wing slash?

Chapter 3769
"Looking at your Excellency Luan Fox's attitude, you don't seem to want to discuss the alliance?"

"I wonder if your Excellency Luan Fox is interested in my strength?"
Luo Zheng asked coldly.
Both the Luan Fox and Tongtian were stunned.
It did not expect this human to be able to hurt it.
Of course, this kind of damage was not substantial to Shift.
The Luan Fox's body trembled slightly, and the pieces of Shift that fell to the ground jumped up one after another. They quickly stuck together and returned to their previous state.
"You surprised me." The arrogance in the Luan Fox's eyes completely disappeared.
Luo Zheng's attack was like a bucket of water splashed into its heart, and it quickly woke up the Luan Fox.
Whether it was the Spirit Monkey King or the Great Ape King, they were not truly stupid. There was definitely a reason why they supported Luo Zheng so much!
Luo Zheng's expression was normal. "Let's continue to talk about the alliance."

The Luan Fox was surprised.

When it asked this question, the Grandmaster of Heaven sighed silently in his heart.
Although the Bird Lord Luan Fox was powerful, it did not have any negotiation skills.
They treated the humans with arrogance, but Luo Zheng found out that the Spirit Bird King was in a bad situation. Even if they acted recklessly, they didn't gain any advantage …
If he asked Luo Zheng now, Luo Zheng would definitely ask for an exorbitant price.
"The condition of the alliance is that the entire Bird Soul Sect must obey my orders," Luo Zheng replied.
The anger that the Luan Fox had been suppressing surged up again.
This human really infuriated it …

The luan fox's resounding voice contained a trace of grief and indignation.
Its words were meant for the great ape king.
However, Da Gu didn't say much. Instead, he acted as if it was a matter of course. "There's nothing wrong with listening to him. That's what King said …"
The monkey spirits weren't good at strategizing and basically left this aspect to this group of humans.
When the Luan Fox heard this, he wanted to vomit blood. Was this really the once invincible Monkey Spirit Sect?
Depressed, he really wanted to turn around and leave. However, reality told him that he couldn't just leave like this.
The Bird Lords couldn't bear the current situation. They had to win over the strong support of the Monkey Spirit Sect.
After thinking carefully, he gritted his teeth and said, "We can obey your orders. All of our Bird Lords are at your disposal, but there are two exceptions!"

The Bird Spirit Sect would never put their King in danger. After all, Shou's strength was far inferior to the Snake Spirit King when he wasn't awakened.
The Mother World and Evil Deities were the other major foundation for the Bird Spirit Sect to turn the tables. They had already attracted the attention of Han Ge and the others. The Luan Fox also had some understanding of the conflict and relationship between the Evil Deities and Luo Zheng.
"Okay, but you have to give me the coordinates of the top ten Bird Lords," Luo Zheng said.
Luo Zheng didn't have any trust in the Bird Lords. Moreover, because of the Evil Deities, the Bird Spirit Sect could turn friends into enemies at any time. He had to hold these strange coordinates in his hands.

"Next time, I will bring the coordinates of the other Bird Lords …"
Then, a Light of Destruction burst out from the Luan Fox's neck. Countless tiny thunderbolts wrapped around its Treacherous Body. In the end, only the coordinates in the Prime of Tongtian's hand remained.
