"But there's something strange." Nuwa frowned. "Since the Bird Spirit Sect is completely weak, why didn't the Snake Spirit Sect eliminate them along with the Bird Spirit King?
Could there be some change in this? "
Even the bird owners couldn't figure out why the Spirit Snake King would let the Spirit Bird King go that day, let alone the people present …
After the Luan Fox returned to its nest in Clear Jade Sky, it told the other Bird Lords about Luo Zheng's request.
Han Ge, Ling Lou, and the other Bird Lords were furious.
Handing their lives over to a mere human?
Wishful thinking!
However, this excitement only lasted for a short while. After the Luan Fox analyzed the stakes and the fate of the Bird Spirit Sect, the Bird Lords fell silent.
Yes, they had no choice.
After these Bird Lords compromised, they all bowed in front of the Bird Spirit King.
Of course, they didn't dare to tell this to the Bird Spirit King, Shou. Although Shou hadn't awakened, once he heard this, he would definitely turn the world upside down.
And this scene was naturally seen clearly by the Snake Spirit King who was far away in Sky City using the limited omniscient stone.
"Why didn't you stop them …" the Snake Spirit King asked unwillingly.
"Are you afraid of more Bird Lords?"
Black Light asked.
"Of course I'm not afraid of mere Bird Lords," the Snake Spirit King said disdainfully.
"That's right. The addition of the Bird Lords will allow them to enter Clear Jade Sky faster," Black Light said.
"What do you want to do?"
The Snake Spirit King asked.
Black Light did not answer. It only let out a burst of laughter that it could not understand.

Chapter 3770
Several days later, Tongtian returned to Night Vision Island.
This time, he handed the bird lords' coordinates to Luo Zheng.
Placing one's life in the hands of others was foolish in most cases.
However, the bird lords had nowhere to go. On the other hand, they were prepared to sacrifice themselves for the safety of the Bird Spirit King's "hunt." This was not inconsistent with their usual loyalty.
Most importantly, the bird lords trusted the Monkey Spirit King.
The relationship between the Bird Spirit Sect and the Monkey Spirit Sect was much deeper than that of Luo Zheng's group.
On the day of the alliance, although Da Gu was only squatting at the side, he had heard every word of their conversation.
Both the Monkey Spirit King and the Great Ape King were rash and impulsive, but they were not stupid.
They trusted Luo Zheng and the Primeval Lord of Heaven because these humans had the same goal as them. They were worthy of their trust.
If the Monkey Spirit King could trust someone, the bird lords could also trust them.
However, on the day the coordinates were delivered to Luo Zheng, Da Gu personally came to take the coordinates away. The reason was that it was not safe to leave them in Luo Zheng's hands. The Monkey Spirit King would protect these coordinates.
As for whether the Monkey Spirit King really wanted to protect these coordinates or was worried about leaving them in Luo Zheng's hands, no one knew.
The addition of the Bird Spirit Sect gave the heroes of the human race even more confidence.
After all, they, who were originally isolated and helpless, suddenly gained the support of the Bird Spirit Sect and the Monkey Spirit Sect. This was something they did not even dare to imagine.
The matter of entering Jade Clear Heaven was put on the agenda. However, before that, they still needed to make careful preparations to find a better opportunity.
… …
In the Chaotic World, the Land of Beginnings was still devouring thousands of heads in an orderly manner.
Previously, the Land of Beginnings was only twice the size of Mount Li. Mount Li barely touched the top of the Land of Beginnings.
Now, standing at the top of Mount Li, one could see a boundless blood-red sky. This was already a huge world … …
The "continental plate" that was originally floating in the sky had been moved down by Luo Zheng. It lay between Mount Li and the Ultimate Graveyard, acting as a bridge between the two.
On the other side of Mount Li, three other huge mountains were born.
Each mountain was only one-third the size of Li Mountain, and their shapes were all different. These were the lairs that Luo Zheng had specially built for the three Primal Chaos Ancient Gods.
Previously, the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods had been curled up inside Blackfoal Mountain. Now that space was no longer a problem, there was naturally no need for them to suffer.
"Boom, boom …"
Thunder rumbled in the blood-red sky.
Be it Li Mountain, the continental plate, Yang, or the people in the Sinkhole graveyard, all of them looked up.
"Mother, there's thunder …"
In the immortal residence, Luo Yunxin covered her ears, a trace of fear in her big black eyes.
"Don't be afraid, Yunxin. That's not thunder. Your father is cultivating …" Xi Youqin hugged Luo Yunxin and comforted her.
"Where is Father?" Luo Yunxin asked.
"Look over there, that figure … That's your father," Xi Youqin said as she pointed upwards.
There was indeed a tiny figure floating in the middle of the blood-red sky.
In the past few days, Luo Zheng's inner world had undergone great changes.
Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin, Xun, and the other girls also saw this. After Luo Zheng explained, they learned that Li Mountain had been placed in the Place of Beginnings …
What surprised them even more was that not long after, the Sinkhole graveyard and the Divine Realm were also placed in!
Thus, the inner world became lively …
Su Lingyun, Ning Yudie, and Xun wanted to return to the Great Evolution Universe.
Mu Ning and Han Liusu couldn't wait to return to the Divine Realm to reunite with their loved ones.
In the blood mist …
Luo Zheng's body floated like a piece of rootless duckweed.
Every half an hour, the blood-red lightning would whip his body like vines.
This kind of whip didn't bring pain, but a sense of power. Endless power was fusing into Luo Zheng's body.
"Boom, boom …"
After dozens of blood-red lightning struck Luo Zheng's body, cracks suddenly appeared on his body, like a porcelain that was about to shatter.
"It's about to shatter, my body can't take it …" Luo Zheng shouted in the lightning.
The blood-red lightning quickly disappeared, and the sky regained its tranquility.
Immediately after, bright green raindrops fell on Luo Zheng's body. These raindrops were like small insects with intelligence, crawling into the cracks on Luo Zheng's body. Then, the cracks were repaired at a speed visible to the naked eye.
After Luo Zheng's body had been tempered a hundred times, it was absorbed by the Origin Divine Blood, which was equivalent to reconstructing a nearly perfect body for Luo Zheng.
Now, this body began to be tempered again. Before this, he had already undergone the baptism of thirteen rounds of blood-red lightning.
"How long will it take to reach the limit?" Luo Zheng asked.
"I'm not sure. In my memory, there is no upper limit to the strength of the body, because I once strengthened a host to a terrifying level," the Origin Divine Blood said as it recalled.
Its memory had always been very chaotic, and some key memories had directly disappeared. However, some details were very clear.
"Terrifying … how terrifying?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows trembled slightly.
To be described by the Origin Divine Blood like this, it was probably extraordinary.
"His body was not destroyed by a full blow of ninety percent of the energy in the Starry World. It's probably like this …" the Origin Divine Blood said.
The Starry World was the fifth grid world, which was the deep space of the Gate of the Four Spirits.
If it was an independent sub-world like the Chaotic World, it would be fine, but that was an entire grid world!
What kind of terrifying power was that, and what kind of powerful body was that …
"It's a pity that I forgot the appearance of that host, and even his name." The Origin Divine Blood's voice sounded a little disappointed. "In the battle with the Descenders, I lost every time …"
The Origin Divine Blood did indeed possess great power, but its opponents were always ahead of it.
"This time is different," Luo Zheng suddenly said.
"What's different …" the Origin Divine Blood asked.
"Because your host this time is me," Luo Zheng said confidently.
The Origin Divine Blood was silent for a while. It probably did not want to discourage Luo Zheng, so it said, "Begin the fourteenth round of the blood-red lightning baptism. After the fiftieth round of baptism, I will fill your body with Void Node spores …"
"That's also a method to strengthen the body?"
"Yes, using the power of space to strengthen the body …"
"This is the last method to strengthen the body?"
"No, there are several more …"
Then, a rumbling sound came from the blood-red sky again. Blood-red lightning as thick as a water tank swam in the sky, rushing toward Luo Zheng.
At this moment, countless afterimages suddenly appeared in Luo Zheng's eyes …
The mountains in the distance had afterimages, the lightning also had afterimages, and so did he …
Indefinite images!
Another sealing stone had descended …
It had only been a few months since the last sealing stone had appeared, but the frequency of its appearance seemed to be increasing. At the same time, a square black stone floating more than a thousand feet above the central part of the Blood Sea State quickly attracted everyone's attention.

Chapter 3771
The sealing stone was an excellent material for refining weapons. Every time it appeared in the mother world, it would cause a fight.
This time was no exception …
Although the Blood Sea State was relatively desolate, it was once the territory of the You Xiong Clan.
Therefore, as soon as the sealing stone appeared, dozens of Nirvana Realm cultivators surrounded it.
"It's only been two months, and another sealing stone has appeared in the mother world …"
"Who will take this sealing stone?"
"Give it to the Ji Clan?"
Although Ji Xuanyuan was killed and the You Xiong Clan was destroyed in name, the Ji Clan still existed. After all, the Evil Deities did not destroy the clan.
At this moment, one of the Nirvana Realm cultivators began to tremble.
The expressions of the other Nirvana Realm cultivators changed drastically when they saw this. The Evil God had once descended upon the Blood Sea Continent and started a massacre, occupying the bodies of many people. A few of these Nirvana Realm cultivators had been occupied by the Evil God before, so they naturally recognized this scene.
In the blink of an eye, that person had turned into Evil Deities.
"Give it to the Ji Clan?" Evil Deities glanced at that person calmly.
That person seemed to have fallen into an ice cellar as his entire body began to tremble. "No, no, no, of course it's to Lord Evil Deities …"
Evil Deities snorted and looked at the sealing stone. He seemed to be in deep thought.
He seemed to be unable to figure it out, so he looked at that person. That person trembled and wanted to speak, but his body began to twitch as well. He turned into the second Evil Deities.
"It's said that the sealing stone is used to measure time. Does this mean that the frequency is near?" Evil Deities asked.
"It should be …" The second Evil Deities answered.
"But the Chaos World has changed drastically. What form will the final appear in?"
"No one can predict …"
"Let the main body ask the Bird Lords. Maybe they will know."
"Yes, but I'm not concerned about the final. Since the Serpent Spirit King wants to complete the descent, the final must be within its consideration. I'm curious about the afterimage I saw just now."
"Didn't we talk about these afterimages last time? The sealing stone itself is a timer of chaos. When it appears, it will cause a slight time disorder, and we will see the afterimage. "
"I don't think so. If time is really out of order, then the number of afterimages should be the same for each of us. However, the number of afterimages of each of our doppelgangers, including the main body, is very different. These afterimages are probably other versions of us!"
Evil Deities had a big disagreement about the strange afterimages.
One side believed that it was just a simple temporal disorder, while the other side believed that there really were many 'selves', but these 'selves' were usually hidden in the body and could only be observed when the temporal disorder occurred.
"So what if it's another one of us? They have always existed in sync with us. Normally, we wouldn't notice them at all. This is meaningless … "
"There must be a meaning to its existence …"
This question also troubled the main body of Evil Deities.
He leaned on the side of the hall and listened to the debate between the Evil Deities. He was also confused by these afterimages.
"Hmm?"
At this time, the main body of Evil Deities suddenly felt that one of the doppelgangers was extremely excited. Then, an Evil Deity who was squatting in the corner of the square suddenly laughed, attracting the attention of the other Evil Deities.
"What are you laughing at?"
"If you have something to say, say it quickly!"
"Stop pretending …"
The Evil Deities sneered. "Those afterimages have always been in our bodies. They can only be seen when the time fluctuates. Why do you think?"
"Why?" Some Evil Deities were puzzled.
"Because you are all idiots," the Evil Deities scolded.
The other Evil Deities were not angry. Instead, they retorted, "We are all the same. Are you scolding yourself?"
They came from the same person and had the same personality, the same emotions, and the same wisdom.
On the other hand, the main body of Evil Deities in the hall was a little depressed. Why did they suddenly start scolding? The Evil Deities who scolded continued, "These afterimages are so vivid. We can see them when the time is chaotic, but we can't see them when the time is stable. That is because our cultivation in space-time is not enough. If our cultivation is enough, we can naturally distinguish them.
。”
These words sounded reasonable, but they were undoubtedly nonsense …
The current cultivation of the Evil Deities in the Chaotic World was already at the peak.
Not only did they unify the 3,001 Divine Paths, but they also comprehended the six Divine Paths of Truth. They even used the power of the six Divine Paths of Truth to create the even more powerful "Six True Fusion Explosion".
Putting aside the Nirvana Token and the bloodline of the Immortal Realm, the Evil Deities were simply unprecedented.
"But the fusion of Dao energy that we cultivate has reached the limit."
"Yes, this is common knowledge …"
"There is even less possibility of strengthening the Spatial Truth!"
The Evil Deities said one after another. "Didn't that birdman called Han Ge tell us that the Chaos World was descended from the Master Realm? The energy that exists in the Chaos World is also descended from the Master Realm. Although we haven't been there before, we've never been there.
Although we have not gone to the Immortal Painting Scroll, how is the bloodline power obtained from it different from the Divine Path? "
The Evil Deities were silent, but a light seemed to have been lit in their hearts.
They were originally the same person, and their hearts were connected. In an instant, they all understood what he was trying to say.
"The bloodline power of the Main Realm is clearly the same thing as the fusion of Dao energy!" The Evil Deities who scolded said.
"The difference in power is huge …" The Evil Deities reminded.
In the battle of the Blood Sea Prefecture, the two Immortal Realm experts, Ji Xuanyuan and Shinno, had displayed their strongest abilities. This had already exceeded the Evil Deities' expectations. If not for the Evil Deities being able to clone themselves at will, the outcome would have been uncertain.
"Of course there is a difference, but since the two are the same, we have a way to restore them," The Evil Deities who scolded continued.
"Restore the fusion of Dao energy into bloodline power …"
"That's a crazy idea!"
"Impossible!"
"Idiots, how do you know it won't work if you don't try? Before this, who had the complete fusion of Dao energy? "The Evil Deities scolded again. At this time, the Evil Deities' main body's voice rang out in the hall. "That's a good idea, but you've overlooked one point. The birdmen came from the Main Realm, and they were born with the bloodline power of the Main Realm. However, I've asked Shen Tu, and they don't know what the afterimage is.
What's going on? "
"If you want to see the afterimage clearly, you'll need a higher level of power than the Main Realm. Perhaps we can continue to restore it …" The Evil Deities speculated. "In the end, all the powers can be restored to one type!"
"Isn't that the fusion of Dao energy?" The Evil Deities roared with laughter.
However, the Evil Deities' main body didn't laugh. Instead, he wore an unprecedentedly serious expression.
Han Ge had once told him about the history of the Four Spirit Gates, and he had also mentioned a higher level world called the "Deep Space". This point coincided with the Evil Deities' speculation.
Restoring the fusion of Dao energy might be an option …
"You, come into the hall," the Evil Deities' main body said to the Evil Deities.
"What for?" The Evil Deities who scolded them didn't understand. "I'll give you a reward."

Chapter 3772
Less than a month after the second sealing stone, the third sealing stone appeared.
At this time, both the people in the mother world and the people in Li Mountain began to worry.
There was only one possibility for the sealing stones to appear so frequently — the End was coming.
However, this Chaos Epoch seemed to have reached an even worse state than before.
At the end of previous eras, there was only one place of origin like the mother world, and there was only one dominator-level civilization in this place of origin. When this dominator-level civilization faced the End, they would gather the strength of the entire civilization to resist.
。
Of course, not every era would have a dominator-level civilization. There might be three to five superpowers, but these superpowers would join forces to resist the End. Anyway, the chaos was about to be destroyed, and all the hatred of the past was gone.
It was meaningful.
Not only was there no dominator-level civilization in this Chaos Era, but even the superpowers were like a sheet of loose sand.
Even Li Mountain, who valued the Path of the End the most, could not even take care of himself. He hid in Luo Zheng's place of origin.
At this point, no one could care about the End anymore.
More than twenty days later, the fourth sealing stone appeared in the mother world again. There was one less grain of sand in the hourglass.
At the same time, in the blood-colored sky above the place of origin, a blood cocoon cracked open, and Luo Zheng climbed out.
"The blood cocoon is the last strengthening method?" Luo Zheng asked.
"For now," the Origin Divine Blood replied.
"It's time to go …"
Luo Zheng's figure shook slightly, and like a meteor, he fell straight down and landed on the Jade Platform of Li Mountain in the blink of an eye.
During this time, the Primeval Lord of Heaven had come to the Jade Platform several times. He wanted to send news to Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng was always in seclusion, so he did not stay for long.
Today, the Primeval Lord of Heaven had been waiting here, and Luo Zheng had really come.
"The Grandmaster of Heaven has come to Night Vision Island three times," the Primeval Lord of Heaven said directly.
"Why?" Luo Zheng was a little puzzled.
"The bird masters are anxious for us to go to Jade Clear Sky," the Primeval Lord of Heaven said.
Jade Clear Sky had also regained its peace during this period of time. The long war between the Bird Spirit Gate and the Snake Spirit Gate had finally stopped for a few days. However, the Bird Spirit Gate was constantly in fear. After all, the Snake Spirit Gate was a fierce tiger that could pounce at any time.
"Even if we go to Jade Clear Sky, we won't immediately attack the Snake Spirit Sect," Luo Zheng said after thinking for a while.
His goal had always been the Earth Altar in Yu Qingtian.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven smiled calmly. "If we bring the Monkey Spirit Sect into the Jade Clear Sky, we will naturally intimidate the Snake Spirit Sect. The pressure on the Bird Masters will naturally lessen."
Luo Zheng looked at the Land of Beginnings that was getting bigger and bigger, and said, "It's time to go!"
…
North of Shang Qing Heaven.
Thousands of cranes crouched on the side of the mountain.
The appearance of these crane-beak gulls looked a little funny. Their big beaks were like crane-beak hoes. They were not big in size, but they were notorious among the Nirvana Realm living creatures in Shang Qing Heaven. They were smart and cruel, and there were many of them.
The prey that they set their eyes on always ended up in a miserable state.
They had been entrenched here for a few days and nights. Their purpose was to capture the Bone Worms that crossed the border.
At this moment, they suddenly felt a trace of life aura coming from the side of the mountain.
The leader crane-beak gulls poked its head out to take a look. It saw people coming out of a cellar in the distance one after another.
"Coo coo …"
"Coo coo coo …"
These crane-beak gulls began to talk.
In their memory, the human race was a relatively weak target. They were also a kind of excellent food, but there were too few of them.
They had never encountered a situation where dozens of humans appeared at once.
The crane-beak gulls would be a little restless before they hunted. However, in order to be sure, they had to calm down. When the humans came close, they would swarm them. Within a few breaths, these humans would become food in their stomachs.
Just as they quieted down, they suddenly felt a shadow above their heads. It was as if a mass of dark clouds had floated past.
It never rained in Shang Qing Heaven. What they saw was a giant foot.
"Boom!"
This giant foot stepped down. Along with the crane-beak gulls and the mountain, all that was left was a big and deep footprint. The entire Shang Qing Heaven trembled.
"Boom, boom, boom …"
The Spirit Monkey King strode forward.
The Nirvana Realm creatures retreated when they saw such a huge creature.
Even the Shang Qing Heaven couldn't deal with the Hatred Level. What level should the Spirit Monkey King be at?
After Luo Zheng and the heroes of the human race climbed out of the cellar, they climbed onto the eagle back of the Wing King.
The Wing King flapped its wings and followed closely behind the Spirit Monkey King. It was like a green leaf floating in the air.
After the Wing King entered the Night Vision Island, the Primeval Lord of Heaven helped it ask for a secret blood control technique. Now, the Wing King's control of speed and space had increased by a big realm.
The distance from the cellar to the Hundred Thousand Pagoda Mountains was not far.
About four hours later, the Spirit Monkey King stopped in front of the Hundred Thousand Pagoda Mountains.
"How do we open the entrance to the Jade Clear Heaven?" the Spirit Monkey King asked.
Da Gu and the other monkey lords were standing on the Spirit Monkey King's shoulders. They told the Spirit Monkey King how to open the entrance to the Jade Clear Heaven.
"Third row from the left, 120 seats …"
"Upper right, 66th row …"
"First row …"
For the Spirit Monkey King, the Hundred Thousand Pagoda Mountains were like a chessboard.
Under Da Gu's instructions, the Spirit Monkey King strolled among them. It casually reached out and pushed the Pagoda Mountains to the ground.
When the Spirit Monkey King pushed down about 14 Pagoda Mountains, the ground suddenly shook. Then, there was the sound of a mechanism locking together.
"Ka, ka, ka, ka …"
After the underground mechanism was completely locked together, a magic array was activated. In the center of the Hundred Thousand Pagoda Mountains, a blue halo suddenly appeared. Then, the blue halo quickly expanded and formed a space passage.
"This is the passage to the Jade Clear Heaven," the Primeval Lord of Heaven said excitedly.
In the past, the heroes of the human race had paid an unimaginable price to find this passage. In the end, they still failed.
"Actually, there are several passages to the Jade Clear Heaven. They are all located in different directions. The passage opened by the Spirit Monkey King is the largest one," Taishang Laojun said. This time, he was going to the Jade Clear Heaven with his only remaining soul.
"I'll go in first. You guys follow behind," the Spirit Monkey King turned around and said.
Then, he led the other monkey lords and lightly jumped. The moment they touched the passage, they were sucked in.
After the monkey lords entered, the Wings Monarch also lightly flapped his wings and flew into the passage.
"Whoosh …"
The space passage was pitch-black.
Rather than calling it a passage, it was more like a static space.
Everyone was stunned. It was as if they were trapped in a closed world.
After waiting for about the time it took for an incense stick to burn, the pitch-black world suddenly lit up. Everyone appeared in a new world.

Chapter 3773
Every level of the Nirvana Realm had tremendous pressure. Normally, when a Nirvana Realm cultivator ascended to a new level, he or she would feel severe discomfort.
Even someone like Luo Zheng could feel it clearly.
However, after being in there for a long time and getting used to the pressure, he or she could not feel it.
As the core area of the top level of the Nirvana Realm, Luo Zheng thought that the pressure here would be unprecedented.
However, after arriving here, Luo Zheng did not feel the slightest bit of pressure.
What appeared in front of him was a green grassland. There was a small lake in the distance. A faint breeze blew past, causing the lake to sparkle.
This was not the Nirvana Realm. It was clearly an ordinary world.
However, Luo Zheng soon realized that something was wrong.
Beside him, the Primeval Lord of Heaven, Fuxi, and the others were all in a state of rigidity. It was as if they had been immobilized. Not only them, but the Monkey King and the Monkey Masters were also in the same state.
…
"Senior Primeval Lord of Heaven?"
"Senior Fuxi …"
"What's going on?"
Luo Zheng's face was filled with astonishment.
No matter how powerful the spatial technique was, it was impossible to immobilize everyone. Moreover, the Monkey Masters and the Monkey King were there!
"Crack, crack, crack …"
At this moment, black cracks appeared in the distance in Luo Zheng's vision.
These black cracks were like countless crawling insects. They approached Luo Zheng from afar.
Everything along the way was completely eroded by the cracks. Whether it was the lake or the grassland, they all disappeared quickly like background scenery.
"Be careful, Luo Zheng. This is the Swallowing Sky Technique!" Taishang Laojun's voice sounded in Luo Zheng's mind.
"This Swallowing Sky Technique can trap all of them?" Luo Zheng asked.
"No, you are the one who is trapped," Taishang Laojun said. "What you see is all an illusion. This Swallowing Sky Technique will tear you and these illusions to pieces …"
"Crack, crack, crack …"
The black cracks approached from afar. They were getting faster and faster. In a short while, they had spread to the human heroes.
Just as Taishang Laojun had said, the Monkey King, the Monkey King, and all the other human heroes were just nothingness. They were directly eroded into pieces by the black cracks and disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight.
The others might be an illusion, but Luo Zheng himself was the real deal.
Seeing the black crack closing in on him, he had nowhere to run.
"Crack, crack …"
When the first black crack spread on Luo Zheng's body, an extremely powerful tearing force came.
"Ka, ka …"
With the help of the Divine Blood of Origin, Luo Zheng's physical body was far stronger than before. In the face of this powerful Swallowing Sky Technique, his body was as solid as a rock. Only the edges of his body were slightly warped, and then the black cracks lost their power to move forward.
…
"Cha, beep …"
"Crack …"
Then came the second one, the third one …
Hundreds of thousands of black cracks surrounded Luo Zheng and tore him apart.
"This Swallowing Sky Skill is not as terrible as I imagined …" Luo Zheng said.
Taishang Laojun was speechless. This round of strengthening was indeed extraordinary, so he said, "It's your body that's too strong."
This attack lasted for an entire incense stick's worth of time before the black cracks gradually faded away and the surrounding space gradually cleared up. However, the scene that unfolded before Luo Zheng's eyes shocked him greatly.
Not far away, the Fire God Zhurong had turned into a human-shaped flame. The flame was divided into hundreds of parts. A large part was extinguished, and another large part was ignited. However, after each extinguishment, he could clearly feel that the flame was weakened by a bit!
Nuwa's physical body had completely disappeared. She had turned into a pure shadow of Nuwa, but her shadow was constantly being cut apart …
Fuxi, Gonggong, East Emperor, and the others were all using their own methods to resist.
"They were also affected by the Swallowing Sky Technique?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes." Taishang Laojun nodded.
Even among human heroes, there were differences in strength.
For example, Nuwa, Gonggong, and Zhurong could still resist the black cracks by activating their bloodline to the extreme and turning it into an energy body after practicing the blood control secret method. However, not all bloodline power could be turned into an energy body.
On the other side, Di Jun's body was shining with blue light. His whole body was fluttering like a feather, avoiding the black cracks.
However, the black cracks would eventually converge on the target. No matter how ingenious Di Jun's spatial magic was, he would eventually be forced into a desperate situation …
"Puff! Puff! Puff … "
Horrifying cracks appeared on Di Jun's body as blood gushed out from his wounds.
"Senior Di Jun!"
Luo Zheng took a step forward and flashed behind Di Jun.
"Buzz …"
A green life force emerged from Luo Zheng's place of origin. This was the ability of the Origin Blood, which could heal even the most serious injuries.
However, when this life force rushed out, it brushed past Di Jun!
"What's going on?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
"He's not here," the Origin Blood replied.
In theory, when the Space-Swallowing Technique covered this area, everyone would be isolated.
"Then find him …"
Even though the Space-Swallowing Technique was very powerful, it was still a magic of the fourth grid world. It could not escape the Origin Blood's space-time quadrant.
However, before he could finish speaking, Di Jun seemed to have reached his limit. Hundreds of cracks appeared on his body at the same time.
"Puff!"
In the next moment, his body was torn to shreds, and blood splattered in all directions.
"Senior, Senior Di Jun …"
Luo Zheng's expression changed drastically.
"Puff, puff, puff …"
Not far away, more blood mist exploded. At the same time, human heroes were dying continuously.
The Space-Swallowing Technique descended without any warning and rapidly took away everyone's lives!
"Sigh …"
Taishang Laojun sighed in his mind.
Compared to the heavy injuries suffered by the human heroes, the monkey lords were in a much better condition.
The Treacherous Body could withstand endless division. Although the Space-Swallowing Technique tore them into pieces, they could be restored in an instant.
As for the Monkey Spirit King, he stood in place and looked straight ahead. No one knew what he was looking at …
After more than ten breaths of time, this miserable scene finally came to an end.
"Buzz …"
The human figure and Nuwa's figure flickered. The originally blurry boundary slowly became clear, and Nuwa's body was restored.
In order to deal with the Space-Swallowing Technique, Nuwa's original body had squeezed her bloodline power to the limit. At this moment, her face was as white as paper. When she saw the puddles of blood on the ground, her expression was extremely ugly.
"Whoosh!"
Just beside Nuwa, a black and white Eight Trigrams was still slowly rotating. Then, Fuxi's figure appeared from it. Facing the Space-Swallowing Technique, Fuxi's original body seemed very relaxed. However, when he saw the miserable state of the human heroes, his expression changed.
It was extremely ugly …
Then, Zhurong, Gonggong, Eastern Emperor's original body, and the others gradually recovered their original appearances.
"Da, da …"

Chapter 3774

Inside Sky City.

Chapter 3777

Cross Bone Club.

Chapter 3778

Chapter 3779
This Earth Altar was not only huge in size, but also very beautiful in appearance.

When he reached the bottom of the stairs, Luo Zheng saw a stone door in front of him.
The stone door looked very simple, as if it had been casually cut out with a stone knife or an axe, and its surface was covered with messy carvings.

Luo Zheng, on the other hand, was unaffected. The blood energy in his body circulated slightly, and the white frost dissipated. When he looked inside, he saw a snake-shaped path made of solid ice. Countless white dots of light floated on both sides of the path.

In the upper levels of the Yu Sheng-tian, Luo Zheng had seen many Cold Prisons, but these Cold Prisons were very small in size. But the Cold Prison in front of him was different. Except for the ice path in the middle, the rest of the Cold Prison was a world formed by the Cold Prison. If one didn't look carefully, the dots of light in the sky would be mistaken for fluttering snowflakes.

Chapter 3780

The cold air went straight to Luo Zheng's Dantian. Large amounts of white cold air drilled out from the top of the blood-colored sky. If it was not stopped, the entire Land of Origin would turn into a world of ice and snow, and ninety-nine percent of the creatures living there would be frozen to death.
Of course, this did not happen …

The sun in the Land of Origin sent blazing energy into Luo Zheng's body, which was enough to contend with this low temperature.
So Luo Zheng stepped into the Cold Prison's range with ease.
"Phew …"
The low temperature of the Cold Prison and the high temperature of Luo Zheng's body competed with each other. The two contended with each other, offset each other, and finally formed a balanced situation. A thin layer of white fog appeared on the surface of Luo Zheng's body, which made Luo Zheng look like a white bear.
Bear, but at least he could move freely now.

After exiting the Cold Prison,
Luo Zheng moved forward along the snake-shaped path.
After walking for a distance of more than a hundred feet, the Human Spirits in golden armor lay on both sides of the snake-shaped path. After a distance of more than a hundred feet, the Human Spirits lying on the ground wore armor that turned from gold to cyan.
These Human Spirits in cyan armor should be stronger than the Human Spirits in golden armor. After walking for a distance, the Human Spirits in the Cold Prison on both sides were no longer wearing armor, but all kinds of luxurious clothes. Among them, there were also some beautiful women. Luo Zheng entered the Cold Prison to investigate again. Regardless of whether it was a man or a woman, his mind was the same.
There was no "soul" in the old one.
So Luo Zheng did not stay any longer and went straight to the end of the snake-shaped path.
The snake-shaped path became wider and wider as he walked, and finally, there was a three-way fork. The left and right fork could be seen at a glance, and the middle fork was an ice sculpture gate.
Luo Zheng was not in a hurry to enter the ice sculpture gate in the middle. Instead, he walked to the left fork.
At the end of the fork in the road was a throne made of ice and frost. On the throne sat a tall and sturdy man.
When he saw this man, Luo Zheng felt a sense of familiarity. He seemed to have seen him somewhere, but he could not remember.
After exiting the fork, Luo Zheng entered the right fork. At the end of the fork, there was also a frost throne, and on this frost throne sat another woman.
This woman looked very young. She reclined on the throne, showing off her slender and elegant figure. Her slender legs hung on the armrest of the throne. When Luo Zheng saw the woman's appearance, his eyebrows suddenly jumped, and he blurted out, "This is … Lingshuang?"

Chapter 3781
Although her eyes were slightly closed, her appearance was exactly the same as Ling Shuang's.

This man was wearing heavy black armor, and even his face was covered. It was unknown what material this armor was made of, but it exuded a strong deterrent force.
The most eye-catching thing was the heavy sword beside the man.
The sword was ten feet long, almost as tall as the burly man. There was a faint red light flashing in the irregular blade.
Was this the Human Spirit King Bitter Tree?
Luo Zheng carefully looked at the black armor.
A solemn voice came from the black armor.
"Who are you? Tell me your name."
Hearing the black armor speak, Luo Zheng was slightly happy.
He was most afraid that the other party would attack without talking to him. With the Human Spirit King's reputation, he did not want to fight him.
The other party's voice was not mechanical, which meant that he was still rational. This was the best situation!
Luo Zheng took two two two steps forward and clasped his fists........ a human.. Luo Zheng....
Humans and human spirits were almost the same in terms of appearance. It could even be said that humans were the continuation of human spirits in the third grid world.
.................... he...
..........
.....
The irregular heavy sword flew up horizontally, breaking through space and slashing towards Luo Zheng's head.
"This …"

He leaned back reflexively, and the irregular heavy sword almost grazed his nose as it slashed past.
. Boom. Boom. Boom.
.. The wall behind Luo Zheng exploded with a series of dark red shockwaves.

The Origin God Blood quickly analyzed the power of the heavy sword. Although it was not as domineering as the Monkey Spirit King, it seemed to be more powerful!
After analyzing the power of this slash, the heavy sword disappeared again.
When the heavy sword reappeared, it was not one, but seven. These seven heavy swords seemed unreal as they constantly flickered between each other.
"Time Quadrant, 1st, 3rd, 33rd …"
Luo Zheng analyzed the power of the heavy sword in his mind again.
"It's just a sword, but it uses an ingenious space divine ability. This bloodline power does not seem to come from the Human Spirit King, but directly from the heavy sword itself. Can a sword also inherit the power of the Origin God Blood?"
Seeing the seven heavy swords coming at him again, Luo Zheng's figure flashed and floated out like a willow leaf. The seemingly light movement technique also contained a mysterious space divine ability, which was bestowed by the Origin God Blood.
. Whoosh! Whoosh!
Seven slashes!
All of them missed!
In the next moment, Luo Zheng appeared in front of the black armor.
"Senior, this junior has no intention of offending you. This junior just wants to know …"
Before Luo Zheng could finish speaking, the black armor stretched out a huge black hand and grabbed Luo Zheng's neck. The other hand opened, and the huge irregular sword reappeared in his hand. With a "whoosh", it slashed horizontally, wanting to cut Luo Zheng in half.
Luo Zheng was still like a slippery loach, slipping away from the giant black hand and avoiding the slash. There was a trace of anger in his eyes. "It seems that Senior will only be able to speak properly after a victor is determined!"
After saying this, Luo Zheng's figure flickered continuously, wandering around the entire stone hall. The irregular heavy sword followed closely behind like a maggot. The stone hall was filled with the shadows of Luo Zheng and the irregular heavy sword.
The irregular heavy sword was astonishingly fast, but Luo Zheng was even faster.
With the support of the Origin God's Blood, Luo Zheng controlled all the abilities of the entire space-time quadrant. He could even figure out the possible position of the irregular heavy sword and throw a punch at it.
"Boom!"
With this punch, all the phantoms of the irregular heavy sword in the stone hall swayed a few times.
The Origin God's Blood not only had unparalleled abilities, but it had also strengthened Luo Zheng's body repeatedly. Now, it was as if he had been reborn. The power of this punch was not to be underestimated!
After throwing a punch, Luo Zheng's figure flickered again. After arriving at a new position, he threw another punch high up in the air.
"Boom!"
The irregular heavy sword was smashed again.
In this contest of space and speed, Luo Zheng had the advantage.
"Boom! Boom! Boom!
Boom! "Following this, the irregular heavy sword could only passively take a beating, punch after punch, strike after strike.
After taking dozens of punches, cracks finally appeared on the surface of the sword.
"Break for me …"
Now, Luo Zheng was chasing after the heavy sword and smashing it!
But at this moment, a strong green light erupted from the cracks of the heavy sword, and the rich life force quickly repaired the cracks.
"Life quadrant, 36th ability, 40th ability …"
The ability of the Origin God's Blood analyst's irregular heavy sword.

Luo Zheng was somewhat speechless. A sword actually had the ability of several quadrants at the same time, and could even heal itself!

The voice of the Origin God's Blood came from the Inner World.

Chapter 3783

Fortunately, the Origin Divine Blood was also repairing Luo Zheng's body. Several green lightning flashed across the bloody sky in the inner world and poured into Luo Zheng's body. That feeling of tiredness was swept away.

Inside the black heavy armor, a pair of starry eyes revealed a trace of hope and a trace of sorrow.
The Human Spirit King Ku Shu was also in a state of not awakening, but he had a vague perception of the outside world like the old man outside the door.

He was not as smart as Sin, not as fast as Shou, and not as strong as Yin Chu.

He had all the characteristics of the other three Spirit Kings.

The Serpent Spirit King had been in there before and had even tried more than once, but it had all ended in failure.
He had already calculated that unless the Spirit Snake King used the power behind it, it would not be able to break this trap.

Why aren't they here yet?
Had Sin completely sealed off this place?
Or did something happen to them?
Even though he knew from the beginning that the possibility of breaking out of this situation was very small, hope was still like a candle flame in the middle of winter, giving him some warmth and supporting his belief.

A human had actually barged in!
These humans were just creatures created by the creators of this world. Although they were very similar to human spirits, the difference between them and human spirits was too great.
If humankind was a painting created by a master, then human beings were just the scribbles of a child!
This human being named Luo Zheng was not to his liking, so he had to kill him.

How could there be such a powerful being in the Chaotic World?
His speed was in the Sword of Life, his power was also in the Sword of Life, and even his understanding and use of space was in the Sword of Life?
How could this be possible?

This human was trying to shake the Sword of Life bit by bit, and he was trying to consume the power of the Sword of Life bit by bit.
He could defeat him …
A ridiculous idea suddenly popped up in Ku Shu's head.
It was this idea that gave him hope. Maybe the whole deadlock could be turned around because of this human being?
But this hope was too small.
When Ku Shu had set up this trap, he had combined the wisdom of the entire Spirit Gate. Only after Ku Shu had awakened could he break out of this trap. It was impossible to break in and defeat him. This was not looking down on Luo Zheng.
The Sword of Life was just the first part of the deadlock. Once the Sword of Life was defeated, it would be transformed into the Sword of Death. This young man named Luo Zheng would definitely have no way to survive.
Alas, why did he set up this place so strictly?
The Spirit Snake King was a little annoyed.
But if he hadn't made thorough preparations at that time, the Spirit Snake King would have taken him down. This was really contradictory!

Under their relentless pursuit, nearly seventy percent of the snake spirits were wiped out.

After struggling for a while, the Biheaded Serpent was kneaded into a ball by the Spirit Monkey King along with the Holy Stones. As the Holy Stones erupted with the Light of Annihilation, the Biheaded Serpent melted in the Spirit Monkey King's hands …
…
Inside the stone hall.
Luo Zheng's battle with the irregular heavy sword was still ongoing.
He already couldn't remember how many times the irregular heavy sword had been smashed by him. Each time the irregular heavy sword was repaired, it took longer than the last time.
When Luo Zheng smashed the irregular heavy sword again, the green light emitted from the inside of the sword seemed to be somewhat dim. The time it took to repair was getting longer and longer.
"Crack!"
This time, the irregular heavy sword was once again smashed by Luo Zheng while it was being repaired.
The crack wasn't completely repaired. Instead, it widened.
Luo Zheng took advantage of the situation and bombarded it with dozens of punches. Countless cracks continuously extended from the sword's hilt. Finally, with a "pop" sound, it shattered into dozens of pieces.
Patter patter …
Those irregular pieces were scattered all over the ground.
"It's finally over …"
Luo Zheng also felt that it had taken too long. He walked directly towards the black heavy armor.
As soon as he took two steps, the pieces on the ground floated in the air and pieced together, forming a new sword.
"You still want to struggle?!"
Luo Zheng's face darkened. His figure flashed, and he arrived in front of the irregular heavy sword and smashed a punch.
But when this punch hit the sword, the irregular heavy sword suddenly became erratic. Luo Zheng's punch passed through the afterimage, and the irregular heavy sword flashed back to the black heavy armor's hand.
Sizzle sizzle sizzle …
Wisps of black fog escaped from the sword, which seemed to be able to erode the light. After spreading to the entire stone hall, everything fell into darkness.
Inside the black heavy armor, Ku Shu was still immersed in shock.
This human had actually defeated him!
What strange things had happened in the Main World?
Why had such a powerful being been born in the Chaotic World?
On one hand, he was surprised and puzzled. On the other hand, he felt pity and pity.
Breaking my Sword of Life would be the thing you would regret the most. Ku Shu knew very well how powerful the Sword of Death was.
He had spent a long time with this heavy sword. In countless battles, Ku Shu had only used the Sword of Death three times. Once the Sword of Death was activated, even Ku Shu would not be able to control it.
Both Ku Shu and the sword would fall into a terrible state …
The darkness lasted for three breaths of time, and then many horizontal and vertical green lines lit up the darkness.
These lines extended an infinite distance, outlining many huge squares in this space.
"The space has changed?
This is no longer the stone hall? "
Luo Zheng could not help but say.
"No, it's not just the space that has changed …" The voice of the Origin Blood came, "This sword has changed all the rules. This is another world, a world with new rules."
"A world with changed rules …" Luo Zheng said in surprise.
"Correct …"
Ku Shu, inside the black heavy armor, answered silently.
Then, Ku Shu could only watch helplessly as he charged toward Luo Zheng with his heavy sword in hand …
"This speed …"
Luo Zheng's pupils slightly widened.
Before he had time to dodge, the heavy sword had already smashed into his body.
Bang!
Luo Zheng was directly sent flying, his body passing through countless huge square spaces.
If he had been in the stone hall, he would have crashed into the walls of the stone hall within 500 feet.
But now Luo Zheng was sent flying tens of miles away, and there was still nothing behind him. This was a world with infinite length and width.

Chapter 3787
"Luo Zheng, are you all right?"
Old Lord Taishang asked worriedly.
No matter how strong Luo Zheng's body was, it couldn't compare to the Treacherous Body. It would be terrible to be hit by the irregular heavy sword.
"I'm all right," Luo Zheng answered.
What he cared about was not the heavy sword itself, but this world that changed the rules.
He was no stranger to changing the rules.
The universe, the Realm of Gods, the mother world, and the other side all had different rules. The rules of these places were set at the beginning of creation, but with enough power, the rules could still be changed.
The simplest way was to change the rules of the inner world. For example, Luo Zheng had the Land of Origin. It was not difficult for him to change the rules.
The irregular heavy sword was indeed fast, but Luo Zheng could dodge it with his supernatural power.
But the rules of this infinitely vast world changed. Luo Zheng's supernatural power was not so easy to use. The space supernatural power he used lost its effectiveness.

From the surface of the black heavy armor, runes lit up one after another.
The originally lifeless heavy armor became full of vitality …

Now, the black heavy armor seemed to be inspired by the irregular heavy sword and gradually came to life!
Two blood-red light spots lit up in the helmet of the black heavy armor.

Luo Zheng's face showed an unprecedentedly dignified expression.
The black heavy armor gave him the feeling of a living person, an unprecedented peerless master.

I don't want to kill you …
But you must die by my hands.

If only Shift could change her obsession at will. Otherwise, she wouldn't be facing such an awkward situation now.

"Da!"
The black-armored man took a step in the air and disappeared from his original position. His irregular heavy sword chopped down at Luo Zheng's head.
The other party's speed wasn't that fast.
However, Luo Zheng's speed had decreased too much. He had no way to dodge this sword and could only take it head-on.
"Crack!"
Luo Zheng's sword slapped his arms and sent him flying again.
While still flying, the black heavy armor appeared above Luo Zheng at an extremely fast speed. "Boom!"
This sword heavily smashed Luo Zheng's back, and Luo Zheng began to fall vertically.
"Phew …"
The black heavy armor disappeared again. This time, it actually appeared below Luo Zheng, raising its irregular heavy sword and thrusting upwards.
If he was stabbed by this irregular heavy sword, his body would probably be directly pierced through.

At the critical moment, the Origin Divine Blood once again merged with the ability of the Space Quadrant.
It couldn't change the basic rules of this space, but it could adapt to such rules!
After the ability in Luo Zheng's dantian emerged, he suddenly disappeared and then reappeared in a space dozens of miles away.
After using the Space Shift, Luo Zheng didn't stop. Instead, he continued to shift …
Ten miles, a hundred miles, a thousand miles …
In a dozen breaths' time, Luo Zheng had shifted thousands of miles away, and this space didn't seem to have an end at all.
The black heavy armor didn't seem to be in a hurry to pursue. It merely floated in the air and waited.
When Luo Zheng was tens of thousands of miles away from it, it took another step forward.
Even though it was tens of thousands of miles away, it still appeared precisely beside Luo Zheng. It had precise control of this space!
Luo Zheng retreated reflexively, wanting to continue increasing the distance between them.
However, a strange scene occurred. Although Luo Zheng had clearly chosen to retreat, he still crashed into the irregular heavy sword.
"Dong!"
Luo Zheng's forehead crashed into the irregular heavy sword, and an inch-deep dent appeared on his forehead.
"Luo Zheng, what's wrong with you?"
Taishang Laojun asked worriedly.
"I …"
Before Luo Zheng could answer, the black heavy armor had already circled around to his left. Luo Zheng habitually shifted to the right. "Cha …"
This time, he crashed heavily into the irregular heavy sword, and another thumb-sized wound appeared on his shoulder.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng's bones were extremely strong, and his body was unimaginably tough. After the wound was cut, there was no blood at all.
"The basic rules of space have been reversed. Front, back, left, right, up, and down are all opposite to you," the Origin Divine Blood reminded him.
"How should I deal with this …" Luo Zheng asked.
"Let me change your perception!"
A life force rose from the Land of Origin, and this life force directly poured into Luo Zheng's mind.
Luo Zheng's eyes began to shake up and down. Under a wave of dizziness, the world suddenly turned upside down!
"In the opposite direction …"
Seeing the black heavy armor slashing at him again, Luo Zheng's reaction was extremely accurate this time, and he easily dodged the slash.
Within the treacherous body, Ku Shu had already sunk into deep thought.
The Death Sword could design the basic rules …
And the basic rules were the rules that all living creatures relied on to survive.
The young man could not resist it!
But after such a long time, this guy was still alive and kicking?
Where did this monster come from?
This was completely beyond Ku Shu's imagination. As smart as he was, he could not figure it out even if he cracked his head.
"The Death Sword has modified the rules twice, and you can adapt to it. This ability is not much worse than mine in the past. However, if it continuously modifies the basic rules, you will be in danger …"
Everyone's ability was limited and fixed. Perhaps Luo Zheng's bloodline power could resist the two modified basic rules, but that was luck and coincidence. However, if the Death Sword continuously modifies the basic rules, Luo Zheng would still die.
Ku Shu really wanted to tell this to Luo Zheng, but unfortunately, he could not do it.
Just as Ku Shu said, the irregular heavy sword modified the basic rules again. Luo Zheng's body suddenly became incomparably heavy, as if it was filled with lead. The weight of everything in this world was increased by ten thousand times.
If it was before, Luo Zheng would have been unable to avoid being slashed by the sword.
But now, the Origin God Blood had adapted. Whenever the basic rules were changed, the Origin God Blood would offer a new ability for Luo Zheng to adapt.
Therefore, the third modification of the rules only delayed Luo Zheng for a moment.
The black heavy armor did not expect Luo Zheng to adapt in such a short time. As usual, it attacked Luo Zheng.
But this time, Luo Zheng began to counterattack.

Chapter 3788
When the black heavy armor attacked again with the irregular heavy sword, Luo Zheng's slightly sluggish body suddenly became swift.

On the other side, the irregular heavy sword obviously did not expect Luo Zheng to be able to counterattack so smoothly. Luo Zheng's palm touched the sword, and the palm print was accurately imprinted on the sword.
"Bang, bang, bang!"
Another three muffled sounds that were not very powerful came.
The irregular heavy sword that had recovered its original shape was pieced together again, and now the surface actually cracked again! "Wha, what …" Even though he was restrained inside by the Treacherous Body, Ku Shu could still feel the pain of the irregular heavy sword.
Facing the Death Sword, Luo Zheng not only adapted to the rules, but after adapting to the rules, he could also heavily injure the Death Sword! He had a chance of winning! This was the first time Ku Shu had such a thought.
This time, he no longer felt that it was absurd, but became very excited.
For him who had not awakened, the Treacherous Body was not his strong reliance, but a prison.

Luo Zheng might not be able to awaken him, but at least he could bring him out of the Earth Altar.

As a Human Spirit King, he more or less looked down on the human race. This kind of contempt came from a higher level world looking down on a lower level world. It was similar to how humans looked down on insects crawling on the ground.
Previously, Luo Zheng's "insect" was somewhat special and attracted Ku Shu's attention, but it was only a powerful insect.
But now, Ku Shu realized that he had made a mistake … After all, he hadn't awakened yet, so he couldn't observe the outside world directly. He could only infer through some vague things.
The strength of this human was probably beyond his imagination. Hope gushed out from the dried spring like water.
At this moment, the basic rules of this world became abnormally complicated.
With every breath of time, the basic rules would change.
If an ordinary Sempiternal realm expert was in this space, even if the irregular heavy sword didn't attack, they would still be killed by the rapidly changing fundamental laws! Unfortunately, the irregular heavy sword was facing Luo Zheng, who had the Origin God Blood.
No matter how the basic rules were changed, Luo Zheng could instantly use the power of his bloodline to deal with it.
The means of the two seemed to have merged together many years ago. You know everything I know, but you can restrain everything I know.
This contest lasted for as long as the time it takes to burn a stick of incense.
Both of them were frozen in place and didn't move.
In the end, the irregular heavy sword couldn't suppress Luo Zheng's rules.
It lost its patience.
After a burst of green light appeared on the sword, this infinite space was filled with the power of life, as if it was a cradle for life everywhere.
The horizontal and vertical green lines in the space transformed into balls. Abundant power of life gathered in these balls. After a few breaths of time, these balls broke open, and monsters with two legs and two pincers crawled out of them.
These monsters had just been born, but they emitted a powerful aura.
There was no emotion in their eyes, only confusion in the depths of their pupils. However, all of their gazes were locked on Luo Zheng. They were waiting for an order to swarm him.
"You want to win with numbers?"
Luo Zheng showed a smile.
With a slight movement of his mind, Inner Avatars were constantly formed in the Land of Beginning.
After these Inner Avatars appeared, they flew straight to the center of the Land of Beginning. After rushing out of the dome, these Inner Avatars appeared beside Luo Zheng.
One, two, three … After a while, hundreds of Inner Avatars stood neatly beside Luo Zheng, and the number was still increasing.
This time, Luo Zheng didn't use the power of the Origin Blood, but the power of the Land of Beginning! Seeing the number of Luo Zheng's "Inner Avatars" skyrocketing, the monsters attacked Luo Zheng.

With this order, Luo Zheng's Inner Avatars were also endowed with spirituality at this moment, and began to fight back against the Human Spirits.
This was no longer a one-on-one battle, but thousands of killings. A chaotic war had begun.
"Boom! Hua! Swish … "Luo Zheng and the irregular heavy sword did not join in.

Outside the Altar of Earth.

Chapter 3789
Luan Fox, Han Ge, and the other bird lords, as well as Shen Chengang and the other human spirits, wanted to take the initiative to attack.

In the blink of an eye, it was already near the Earth Altar. What entered everyone's eyes was a round ball, and within this round ball, there seemed to be something flickering with golden light.

Countless silver spikes rapidly grew from the Snake Spirit King's body. As soon as these silver spikes grew, they pierced into the Monkey King's body.

In the sphere that protected the Snake Spirit King's coordinates, a huge Holy Stone suddenly appeared.

This was a battle of mutual destruction.

Once, or twice? "
The Snake Spirit King sneered.
"But you can't kill me even once …"
As the Monkey King spoke, the 22 wolf fangs on the True Tooth Necklace flickered violently.
The bloodline power in the wolf fangs flowed and gathered in its body. A wild aura bloomed from its body.
The Monkey King's power had always been domineering.
The bloodline power in the 13 wolf fangs was originally a fusion bloodline.
In different situations to deal with different enemies, it was reasonable to use different fusion bloodlines. In most cases, this was the case.
However, once the Monkey King entered a state of rage, it would not care about anything. It would pour out all the fusion bloodlines it had mastered.
The 13 fusion bloodlines were not compatible with each other. They would even conflict and repel each other.
However, the Monkey King forcibly fused them together again, unexpectedly possessing a terrifying destructive power. In the past, it had used this punch to kill 13 elders of the Ancient Eater Clan, shocking the entire Origin World.
The Great Conjecture Institute of the Human Clan had tried to analyze the principle and even tried to recreate this power, but to no avail. At that time, Elder Xue of the Great Conjecture Institute had even lamented that the Monkey King's stupidity had its own luck.
If it had been anyone else who had fused with these bloodlines, the only outcome would be self-destruction.
Now, the Monkey King had done the same thing and directly used the 22 fusion bloodlines.
These fusion bloodlines entangled in the Monkey King's body. Sometimes they released fire aura, sometimes they leaked life force, and sometimes thousands of lightning bolts exploded in its body. Even the time and space around it were faintly chaotic.
"What … kind of power is this?"
The Snake Spirit King was confused.
"The power to send you to your death." The Monkey King smirked.
A ball of light bloomed from his body like a rapidly growing sun. It engulfed the Spirit Monkey King, the Spirit Snake King, and everything within a radius of a few miles. Of course, this included the ball protecting the Spirit Snake King's coordinates in the sky.

Chapter 3790
That terrifying aura shook the hearts of everyone present.

The Spirit Snake King died just like that? "

The Spirit Monkey King stood motionless in the same place, looking tired.
Cracks began to spread across the originally tough Treacherous Body, and clumps of Treacherous Matter began to peel off from its body.

Without waiting for Da Gu to get close, the Spirit Monkey King's Treacherous Body had completely peeled off, turning into broken pieces that scattered on the ground. These broken pieces gradually dissipated …
When the Spirit Monkey King released that terrifying energy, the light of destruction shone on him and the Spirit Snake King. Under the combined effect of the two, not only did they kill the Spirit Snake King, but the Spirit Monkey King also couldn't escape.

On the altar, the Spirit Monkey King's coordinates glowed with a golden light. A huge foot emerged from the coordinates, followed by another foot, then the legs, the torso, the hands, and the head.
"My King!"
"My King!"
"My King!"
The Monkey Lords roared again.
This time, not only did the Spirit Monkey King win, it won completely!
After killing the Spirit Snake King, there was nothing in the Clear Jade Heavens that could threaten them!
Humans and Spirit Birds also showed relaxed expressions on their faces.
"The Spirit Snake King's coordinates have been destroyed. There shouldn't be a chance for it to be reborn …"
"We won just like that?"
"Of course we won. Our King is invincible, hahaha …"
Just as the Monkey Lords were celebrating, they realized that something was wrong with the Spirit Monkey King.
Black cracks once again appeared on the Spirit Monkey King's Treacherous Body. The broken pieces of Treacherous Matter quickly peeled off like cotton mats, enveloping everyone on the altar.

The Spirit Monkey King, who had just been reborn, died. The Monkey Lords were all dumbfounded.

Xia Gu asked with a grave expression.

The Primeval Lord of Heaven, Nuwa, and the others also came over.
They had encountered many Treacherous Matter over the years, but they had never seen such a strange thing.
"Buzz …"
The Spirit Monkey King's coordinates flashed again.
Then, its huge body drilled out of the coordinates again.
The Spirit Monkey King, who had just been reborn, appeared to be in great pain. It opened its mouth as if it wanted to say something. However, before it could speak, cracks began to spread from its head. Its huge body quickly turned into broken pieces and covered the altar. Then, it disappeared …
Although the coordinates could be reborn infinitely, the speed of rebirth would become slower and slower.
"Buzz!"
The Spirit Monkey King was reborn from the coordinates again.
The third time it was reborn, it was no different from before. It turned into a pile of broken pieces without being able to say anything.
The fourth time …
The fifth time …
After the Spirit Monkey King had been reborn five times in a row, the golden light on the coordinates dimmed.
Even someone as powerful as the Spirit Monkey King could only be reborn five times in a row. The coordinates needed a period of time to accumulate energy before it could be reborn again.
"Why?!"
"Why did it turn out like this?!"
Da Gu and Kong Gu were as anxious as ants on a hot pan. The other Monkey Lords were also hugging their heads helplessly. Asking the Monkey Lords to deal with such a simple matter was a little beyond their capabilities.
"The situation … is not good." Nuwa looked into the distance.
The Spirit Snakes and Snake Lords had been reborn one after another. Now, they had gathered again.
"Without the Spirit Snake King, these guys are nothing to be afraid of," said the Primeval Lord of Heaven.
After all, they still had the Bird Lord, the Monkey Lord, and the Human and Spirit Palaces by their side.
Da Gu, Kong Gu, Xia Gu, and the other Monkey Lords looked at the Spirit Snakes. Although they didn't know what was going on with the Spirit Monkey King, they still had to settle the score with the Spirit Snake Sect!
"I'm not talking about them …"
Nuwa shook her head and pointed her slender finger at the sky.
Unknowingly, a ball of black light had appeared in the sky. It was like a shadow floating in the air.
The position of this ball of black light was exactly where the ball had been floating before.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven was also stunned when he saw this black light. Then, his expression became more and more frightened.
The black light gradually expanded, and a sphere slowly emerged from the black light. The huge coordinates in the sphere were still intact!
"Hiss …"
The thick snake tongue was the first to be reborn from the coordinates, followed by the Spirit Snake King's head and long snake body.
Seeing the Spirit Snake King reborn, everyone on the altar felt as if they had fallen into an ice cave.

Chapter 3791
"How dangerous …"
The Spirit Snake King's voice came from above.
Yin Chu's attack was indeed beyond Sin's expectations. It was actually able to destroy its guidance barrier!
The guidance barrier was the sphere that protected the coordinates. This sphere was the strongest ability of the guidance spatial stone.

The Spirit Snake King said it casually, but this curse was not simple.
No matter how strong the curse was, it could only affect the current state. This curse directly affected the coordinates!

Anger filled Da Gu's chest, and his fear of the Spirit Snake King was thrown to the back of his mind. He stomped his feet on the ground, and the ground around him flipped. Da Gu was like a meteor as he flew towards the Spirit Snake King.

The Spirit Snake King did not move much.
There was now an invisible spatial wall between it and Da Gu.

This cube was still shrinking …
Da Gu's sturdy Treacherous Body slowly shrank, slowly filling up the gaps in the spatial wall. Finally, Da Gu turned into a white cube. How could one still see its shape?
This was also because the Treacherous Body was a very special shape. If it was his physical body that was squeezed into a cube, he would have died long ago.
"Gu, gu, gu …"
This spatial body was still shrinking. Da Gu's treacherous body that was tens of feet tall finally turned into a three feet tall and three feet wide square.

They couldn't see through the sphere that was floating in the air, but they could still fight against the space that was binding Da Gu.

But what kind of existence was the Snake Spirit King?
With the existence of the Guiding Space Stone and the Limited Omniscient Stone, there was no one in this area that could go against them.
Even though it had almost died to the Spirit Monkey King, that was because it had been careless. Otherwise, no matter how powerful the Spirit Monkey King was, it wouldn't have been able to threaten it.

…
In the infinite space.

The number of "Luo Zheng" floating in the void was increasing.
Although the irregular epee was still constantly creating monsters, the speed of creation was getting slower and slower. Moreover, the quality of these monsters was getting worse and worse. They simply weren't enough for Luo Zheng's replica to kill.
It was like a lamp that had run out of oil.
"The war is over. You've lost." Luo Zheng had a smile on his face.
Even if the irregular epee had turned into the Sword of Death, its energy was still limited.
Luo Zheng relied on the Land of Origin to wear out the enemy. Eventually, he was in an invincible position. Choosing a war of attrition was a mistake for the irregular epee.
When the last monster was killed by the "Luo Zheng" army, Luo Zheng's thoughts moved slightly. Those replicas flew backward toward Luo Zheng, turning into small light spots that merged into Luo Zheng's Dantian.
The black heavy armor was still silently holding the Sword of Death …
The irregular epee had emotions. These emotions were not perceived by outsiders, only its owner knew.
Ku Shu could clearly feel that the irregular epee was a little depressed!
To force my sword to such an extent …
Ku Shu had never experienced this kind of emotion from the irregular epee.
Whenever he held this sword, he could feel the abundant fighting spirit and the excitement of a spiritual connection.
Even after he became a Treacherous Body, the irregular epee still loyally guarded Ku Shu.
During these years when he couldn't move, this sword was still cultivating by itself …
Although Ku Shu wanted to fight against a common enemy with the irregular epee, at this moment, he hoped that Luo Zheng would win. When this depressing emotion came, Ku Shu was somewhat happy, although this happiness was somewhat contradictory.
But this depressing emotion only lasted for a moment. Then Ku Shu sensed that the irregular epee was transmitting a great determination.
"You, you … what do you want to do?" Ku Shu was shocked.
The Sword of Death was the final form of the irregular epee. If even this form could not defeat the opponent, the situation was a foregone conclusion!
"Buzz …"
The originally unfolded Infinite World suddenly shrank and disappeared. Luo Zheng returned to the stone hall.
Then the black heavy armor raised the irregular epee towards Luo Zheng. White light spots and a gloomy and cold aura floated out of the sword.
"Still want to fight?"
Luo Zheng frowned.
The irregular epee might be strong, but Luo Zheng could not find a way to deal with it. It seemed that this sword still had a trump card?
But to Luo Zheng's surprise, the irregular epee did not attack.
The white light spots and the gloomy and cold aura actually created a cold prison. Then the white light spots hit the heavy armor.
Layers of white frost appeared and condensed into blocks of ice, sealing the sword and the heavy armor inside.
"What is the meaning of this?"
Luo Zheng felt incredulous.
"This sword has a spirit. It knows that it is not your opponent and cannot protect Ku Shu in the black heavy armor. It can only seal itself in ice," Taishang Laojun said.
At this time, it was still protecting its master. This sword was indeed loyal.

Chapter 3792
Luo Zheng stepped forward and knocked on the layer of ice on the black heavy armor. A touch of coldness condensed into white frost on his hand.

Moving Ku Shu out first and then making a decision, perhaps he could think of a way to awaken Ku Shu.

This sword's ability to constantly modify the Laws was indeed very admirable to Luo Zheng.

After saying this, Luo Zheng placed his palm on the surface of the ice and lightly patted it.
"Pop!"
A muffled sound spread out.
Honeycomb-Like cracks appeared on the originally clear ice.
Countless finger-sized ice pieces shattered on the ground …
If these finger-sized ice pieces were thrown into any large province of the Origin World, it would turn into a world of ice and snow that would last for several years, and after it melted, it would form an ocean.
The irregular epee was obviously unwilling to give up.
Although it didn't attack Luo Zheng again, the moment the ice broke, it once again released white light spots, which quickly sealed it and the black heavy armor in ice.

"Buzz …"

At the same time, the irregular heavy sword was communicating with the bitter tree silently.

This sword and armor made of life iron were painstakingly found by the bitter tree and Shou Shou in the deep space.
When Ku Shu met it for the first time, he had to use all kinds of methods to tame it.
After so many years of companionship, he and the sword were like comrades.

Ku Shu advised for a while.
In fact, Ku Shu might not be able to trust Luo Zheng.

Luo Zheng held the hilt of the sword with one hand, and gently pulled the sword upwards.

Red light was emitted from the cracks on the sword body, and a desire was transmitted to Luo Zheng.
It was very thirsty. It needed energy …
Before Luo Zheng knew how to respond, the Origin divine blood had already sensed it. Energy as vast as the sea rushed into Luo Zheng's arm and converged into the irregular heavy sword.
The irregular heavy sword didn't reject the energy given by the Origin divine blood at all!

The Origin divine blood said, "I don't know, but I must have a relationship with it."
After the irregular heavy sword absorbed enough energy, it made a buzzing sound, and the black heavy armor disintegrated by itself.
"Crash!"
Helmet, breastplate, back armor, shoulder pads …
The disassembled pieces of the black heavy armor swarmed towards Luo Zheng. In a breath, it was already neatly put on.
"This armor …"
While Luo Zheng was still surprised, he saw a strange body standing motionless. This strange body was tall and sturdy, with a handsome face, and an unrestrained and agile feeling between his eyebrows. This was the Human Spirit King Ku Shu inside the black heavy armor.
On Ku Shu's chest, there were two golden crosses floating slowly, one above and one below. These were his coordinates.

Taishang Laojun thought for a moment and replied, "It belongs to neither the slaughter type nor the good type. The quiet and motionless type of strange body belongs to the sleeping type."
Ku Shu himself didn't attack Luo Zheng. What attacked Luo Zheng was his sword.
"Then I …"
Luo Zheng was about to say how to deal with the strange body, when suddenly there was a rumbling sound above his head, and the whole Earth Altar began to shake!
Can't hold on?
Luo Zheng's eyebrows slightly raised, and he suddenly felt anxious.
He knew that the Snake Spirit King would definitely attack the Earth Altar, but with the combined power of the Human Spirit, the Monkey Spirit, and the Bird Spirit, they could hold on for a while.
So Luo Zheng took out the Sumeru Space, directly threw the Human Spirit King's strange body and the coordinates inside, and then left the stone hall.
When Luo Zheng opened the door, the old man guarding the ice hall suddenly raised his head.
The old man's obsession was all on Ku Shu, so naturally he was very concerned about everything in the stone hall.
Seeing the black heavy armor open the door, the old man stayed in place for a while, and his dull eyes suddenly became clear. The old man thought that Ku Shu had awakened, and his obsession was satisfied in an instant, so he directly awakened!
"Master Ku Shu!"
The old man strode to Luo Zheng and knelt on one knee in front of him.

"No, his strange body has been put into the Sumeru Space," Luo Zheng replied.

His voice fell, Luo Zheng didn't stop and went straight to the gate of the ice hall.

Chapter 3793

The Bifid Serpent didn't place the monkey lords in its eyes. After all, this was a battle it was certain to win. It didn't expect that Xia Gu and Ancient Inch Stone would be caught off guard. Right now, it was very difficult for it to counterattack.

There were fewer and fewer Human Lords, Bird Lords, and Monkey Lords …

Although the human heroes had faced many desperate situations in the past, they still had the strength to fight back.

They had never been discouraged, but such an opponent was so strong that they could not understand it. How could they fight back?

Next were the Bird Lords who had nowhere to run …
Next were the Human Lords.

The Spirit Snake King looked down at the few people in front of it. There was a faint smile in the depths of its snake eyes.
"Fight it to the death!"
Zhurong widened his eyes, and a surging heat spread in all directions.

Whoosh!

Chapter 3794

Why don't we see him? "

Gong Gong, who had been silent the whole time, abruptly said, "Don't forget about Luo Zheng's power. He has inherited the powerful original divine blood. You're too pessimistic."
Cang Jiao shook his head and said, "There are many experts in our Human Spirit Sect, and all of them have inherited the power of the Origin Divine Blood. But in the end, they still ended up like this."
"Senior Cang Jiao, Luo Zheng didn't inherit the power of the divine blood of origin. He inherited the complete divine blood of origin," Gong Gong corrected.
