He inherited the Origin Divine Blood?
The complete Origin Divine Blood? "
"Yes." The Primeval Lord of Heaven nodded.
"Are you sure you're not mistaken?"
Cang Jiao still didn't believe it.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven said with certainty, "As Immortal Realm experts, we naturally know the difference. Luo Zheng can use the power of the four quadrants at will. That's what the Origin Divine Blood can give him."
Cang Jiao's eyes lit up …
"No wonder! No wonder he could defeat Lord Ku Shu. That sword … with that sword, maybe he can win! "
Cang Jiao was so excited that he couldn't speak coherently.
"Phew …"
At this moment, the irregular heavy sword in Luo Zheng's hand emitted an angry emotion.
Although this sword couldn't speak, it could convey emotions. It could feel sadness and anger.
Facing an opponent who had once killed a Human Spirit King, the irregular heavy sword was full of fighting spirit!
"You want to kill it?"
Luo Zheng asked.
Immediately, Luo Zheng received a response. The irregular heavy sword responded to Luo Zheng with an emotion that was close to joy.
"I have the same thoughts as you." Luo Zheng smiled.
When he raised his head to look at the Spirit Snake King again, his figure had already disappeared.
Thousands of feet of space flashed by, and the irregular heavy sword was already in front of the Spirit Snake King's forehead.
"Let me see your power …" Luo Zheng said.
"Hum!"
The surface of the irregular heavy sword suddenly cracked.
This sword itself was not a whole, but a sword made of 15 pieces of life iron.
When it was brought back from the deep space by the Ku Shu, it had never fully used its power.
There was no other reason. It needed to absorb the power of the bloodline, but even the powerful Ku Shu couldn't support this sword.
But now, it could continuously absorb Luo Zheng's bloodline power. The power of the Origin Divine Blood was almost unlimited, making it unprecedentedly powerful!
"Hum …"
A vortex of energy appeared in the center of the 15 pieces of life iron. With a swoosh, the huge body of the Spirit Snake King was sucked in.
"Twist!"
From the moment Luo Zheng approached the Spirit Snake King, the irregular heavy sword unfolded, and the vortex of energy appeared in an instant!
But the Spirit Snake King's reaction was not slow.
The limited omniscient stones were all concentrated on Luo Zheng.
Every movement of Luo Zheng's body, from a small hair fluttering in the wind to a big heart beating faster, all these information were constantly poured into the mind of the Spirit Snake King.
The guiding space stone began to flash with blue light.
"Unending and Unending Spatial Manipulation."
Layers of space like a thousand-layer pancake constantly appeared, enveloping the huge body of the Spirit Snake King.

Since he had obtained this sword, he had only communicated with the irregular heavy sword on an emotional level. Although the irregular heavy sword accepted Luo Zheng, it still recognized the bitter tree as its true master.
Luo Zheng also thought so. After all, this was a borrowed sword, and he would eventually have to return it to the Human Spirit King.
But now, Luo Zheng realized that if he wanted to defeat the Snake Spirit King and the existence behind the Snake Spirit King, he needed to understand this sword better and control it! Just like what Cang Jiao had said, he wanted to use this sword as an ancient cultivation technique to comprehend it …
Luo Zheng's divine sense poured into the red light spot in the middle. When it came into contact with the red light spot, a feeling of resistance quickly attacked him.
The irregular heavy sword was unwilling to accept him.
"I know you miss your former master."
"But only I can bring you to the extreme. You should know what kind of power I have."
"If you want the bitter tree to come back to life, there is only one way, and that is to defeat the opponent in front of you!"
"Accept me!"
Luo Zheng kept persuading it through his divine sense.
Gradually, the feeling of resistance became weaker and weaker.
Finally, his divine sense smoothly merged into the red light spot.
"Boom!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's mind felt as if it had been hit by a heavy hammer. A huge amount of information gathered. It was an almost infinite arrangement of 140 light spots.
At the same time … Luo Zheng quickly understood the energy used by the Snake Spirit King and the irregular heavy sword. He even understood a strange name from it.
"Probability Extreme State!"
"Swoosh …" Then, traces of black energy flowed from the irregular heavy sword into Luo Zheng's body.

Chapter 3799
The Spirit Snake King was also confused about Luo Zheng's current state.
Its opponent had changed from Luo Zheng to this sword, and Luo Zheng had become a burden.
This was good news for the Spirit Snake King, as it meant that Luo Zheng was no match for it.
However, this irregular heavy sword was so fierce that it could keep up with its speed and neutralize its attacks.
The Spirit Snake King was getting impatient after a long time of failure.
When it saw Luo Zheng's body turning black, its eyes turned serious.
If a sword was so difficult to deal with, wouldn't it be more troublesome if the man and the sword came together?
With this thought, its attacks became even fiercer! The two black blades slashed out tens of thousands of times in a breath.
In other people's eyes, the Spirit Snake King didn't exist, nor did the black blades or the irregular heavy sword. Even Luo Zheng's hand holding the irregular heavy sword was faintly discernible.
The tens of thousands of slashes were blocked by the irregular heavy sword, and the sound was even louder and more ear-piercing, as if someone was sharpening a sword on marble.
"Hiss …" The weakest people present were all in the Immortal Realm. If they heard this sound, they would probably go crazy.
Under such fierce attacks, the irregular heavy sword finally couldn't withstand all the attacks of the Spirit Snake King.
"Whoosh …" One of the ten thousand slashes missed.
The black blade slashed hard on Luo Zheng's black heavy armor.
"Crack!"
A thin crack appeared on the waist of the black heavy armor. The crack went straight through the inner layer and cut through Luo Zheng's waist, all the way to Luo Zheng's back! "Luo Zheng was hit!"
"That sword … can't hold on anymore?"
"Luo Zheng seems to be in some kind of state and can't wake up …" "It's useless even if he wakes up, he can't dodge this attack!"
Everyone on the Earth Altar was talking about it.
Especially the Spirits of the Black Mansion, Yue Mansion, and Guang Mansion were the most nervous.
They didn't expect that the final winner would be a human, and they, as Spirits of the Spirit Snake King, could only watch.
In the next breath, the Spirit Snake King slashed out tens of thousands of times again.
Blindly defending was tantamount to a slow death. The irregular heavy sword missed two of the ten thousand slashes! "Crack, crack!"
This time it was Luo Zheng's right shoulder.
The wound was one and a half feet long, almost splitting half of Luo Zheng's body.
Had it been any other Immortal Realm expert, they would have been sliced into two pieces. However, his body had been strengthened by the Origin Divine Blood and was extremely tough. Moreover, Luo Zheng felt almost no pain.
"Buzz …" The Origin God's Blood also began to move.
Green energy gushed out from the wound, crazily repairing Luo Zheng's body.
This kind of rich life force had an extremely strong restorative ability. It could even bring the dead back to life. However, it was a big project to restore Luo Zheng's strong body, so the restoration speed was naturally much slower.
In the third breath … another ten thousand slashes.
The irregular heavy sword withstood nine thousand nine hundred and ninety-eight slashes, but missed two more.
"Crack, crack!"
These two sabers slashed Luo Zheng's chest. After breaking through the black heavy armor, they directly cut through the ribs in front of his chest. Luo Zheng's heart was pierced through.
The fourth breath … Ten thousand slashes … The breathing of everyone on the altar quickened.
"What should we do? What to do! That sword can't defend against the Spirit Snake King's attacks! "
"Luo Zheng is going to be dismembered!"
"If he keeps getting slashed like this, he will definitely die!"
"Should we go and help?"
The General of Black suggested.
Shen Chengang shook his head and smiled bitterly. "How can we help?"
They couldn't even see the Spirit Snake King's figure … In theory, Luo Zheng should be fighting against an invisible being. However, the Spirit Snake King was too fast, and it had already become a 'non-existence'.
Who could attack an opponent that didn't exist?
Although the General of Black suggested this, Da Gu, Kong Gu, and the other monkey lords had already charged down.
However, before they could leave the altar, each monkey lord was hit by hundreds of slashes. Their Treacherous Body was sliced into half-inch thick slices that fluttered in the wind. The scene was quite spectacular … This was no longer a ghostly attack. This was an attack that they couldn't understand.
While the Spirit Snake King was slicing the monkey lords, most of its focus was still on Luo Zheng. It was distracted for a moment, so it wasn't able to break through the defense of the irregular heavy sword.
However, in the next breath, it was another ten thousand slashes … The black heavy armor was sliced into pieces. The wounds on Luo Zheng's body increased. Most of the wounds were ghastly and almost fatal.
While Luo Zheng was being cut, the black energy continued to crawl all over his body.
"Die for me!"
The speed of the Spirit Snake King's black blade increased again.
This time, the Spirit Snake King was almost pushing itself to its limit. The ten thousand slashes became extremely tricky.
"Bzzt bzzt bzzt …" The irregular heavy sword missed hundreds of slashes amidst the resounding and sharp sounds of metal clashing.
If these hundreds of slashes landed on Luo Zheng's body, it would be enough to slice him into pieces.
However, before this breath, Luo Zheng opened his eyes. He saw a cloud with a trace of confusion.
It was a large black cloud.
It covered an area of about twenty miles, including the Earth Altar and the area in front of it.
The moment Luo Zheng saw this cloud, he immediately understood.
He could appear in any area of this cloud … whether it was the front position or the back position, whether it was the edge of the black cloud or the center of the black cloud, he could appear directly, and he could appear at the same time! This was a very strange feeling.
There seemed to be no concept of space-time in this cloud, or it could be said that this space-time was very special.
He could appear in one place at the same time, or in many places at the same time. This kind of appearance didn't mean that there were more than a few copies. Each copy was himself … He could appear in many places, and he could also disappear directly! In the remaining half a breath, Luo Zheng clearly saw the ten positions of the Spirit Snake King, and the hundreds of slashes from these ten positions were missed by the irregular heavy sword.
Tricky, vicious, and every slash was fatal.
After Luo Zheng comprehended the probability extreme state, he could move in this cloud like the Spirit Snake King, so Luo Zheng suddenly disappeared from everyone's sight.
Then, his figure kept retreating.
Obviously, there was only one person, but he retreated more than ten times in different directions, and each retreat corresponded to an attack from the Spirit Snake King.
The hundreds of slashes from the black blade were all easily avoided by Luo Zheng! After pulling away, the two stopped for the first time, and their figures appeared in everyone's eyes.
Although Luo Zheng's body was still broken and the Origin Divine Blood was desperately repairing it, he still had a smile on his face. "You made this cloud?
It's very special. I seem to be able to move freely in it. "
The Spirit Snake King's face became ugly.

Chapter 3800
Without saying a word, the Spirit Snake King swam into the black cloud and attacked Luo Zheng again.
Luo Zheng was seriously injured, and his body was in tatters.
It wanted to kill Luo Zheng before he could recover! The moment the Spirit Snake King activated its extreme probability state, thousands of Spirit Snake Kings appeared in front of Luo Zheng. These Spirit Snake Kings were densely stacked together, but they didn't interfere with each other.
"Whoosh …" The overlapping Spirit Snake Kings waved their black blades and attacked Luo Zheng.
If it was before, Luo Zheng wouldn't have been able to see the Spirit Snake King's attack pattern, but at this moment, he could see it clearly.
Nearly 2,000 black blades slashed toward Luo Zheng, but before these blades touched Luo Zheng, he disappeared.
The next moment, Luo Zheng's figure appeared on the edge of the black cloud.
Similarly, there wasn't just one figure, but a thousand.
A thousand Luo Zheng were evenly distributed on the edge of the black cloud, forming a circle.
"The largest number in this black cloud is a thousand?"
"It seems that I can't break through … You can't either."
A thousand Luo Zheng said at the same time.
"Cut the crap!"
The Spirit Snake King flew into a rage out of humiliation.
It also spread out a thousand of itself, and each Spirit Snake King corresponded to a Luo Zheng.
The black blades slashed down … "Swoosh!"
Luo Zheng didn't take it head-on.
He disappeared again … In this black cloud, all movements had probabilistic characteristics and couldn't be predicted.
Although the Spirit Snake King also had the extreme probability state, it still couldn't predict Luo Zheng's movements. Thousands of Spirit Snake Kings slashed down at the same time, naturally, they missed.
The next moment, Luo Zheng appeared in the center of the black cloud again. He could now freely summon and release a thousand of himself and one of himself.
After activating the extreme probability state at the same time, the two once again fell into a tug-of-war.
The people on the Earth Altar and the Spirit Snakes under the Earth Altar were even more confused.
Although they couldn't see the Spirit Snake King before, the Spirit Snake King's storm-like attacks could still make ear-piercing sounds. This was a way to prove its strength in battle.
Now, Luo Zheng and the Snake Spirit King had disappeared without a sound. Their fighting style was beyond their comprehension.
"They … Could it be that they're fighting somewhere else?"
"Even his aura has disappeared. How strange!"
"Luo Zheng seems to have comprehended the same movement. Don't worry, I believe he can win!"
After both sides had lost their backbone, the atmosphere calmed down slightly.
Only then did Shen Chengang, the Black Manor General, and the other human spirits climb up the Earth Altar and come to Cang Jiao.
"I am Shen Chengang. Greetings, Elder Cang!"
"I am Black …" Although Cang Jiao was an old servant of Ku Shu, his position in the Human Spirit Sect was not low at all. Sometimes, he could even replace the Human Spirit King to take charge of the situation.
Previously, when the human spirits of the three mansions discovered Cang Jiao, they were all very excited. However, they were trapped in the space and could not come up to greet him.
Cang Jiao looked around at the human spirits and sighed. "You're the only human spirits left in this world?"
"Apart from us, there are two hundred world builders," Shen Chengang replied.
Although the world builders were the pioneers of the Human Spirit Sect, their positions were not high and they were not core figures.
"Is there no one else?"
Cang Jiao asked again.
"No," Shen Chengang said with certainty.
The three mansions had stayed in the Paramita Realm for a long time. Although they had always been under the orders of the Snake Spirit Sect, they had been secretly investigating in secret. At present, it seemed that there were no other human spirits.
"Sigh …" Cang Jiao sighed again.
Shen Chengang thought for a moment and asked, "Elder Cang, are you trying to find the elders of the Great Guessing Manor?"
"Yes." Cang Jiao nodded.
In the Human Spirit Sect, the Great Guessing Manor was on par with the Manor of Shifang, but its role was much more important.
The Manor of Shifang gathered the elites of the entire Human Spirit Sect. For example, the famous Manor Master of Shifang's strength was second only to Ku Shu. However, the reason why the elites of the Manor of Shifang were so strong was almost all thanks to the Great Guessing Manor.
The strongest Human Spirit Sect would enter the Manor of Shifang, but the strong and smart people would definitely enter the Great Guessing Manor.
Ku Shu was trapped, so Cang Jiao placed most of his hopes on the Great Guessing Manor. Only they had a slight chance of rescuing Lord Ku Shu.
However, the final outcome was beyond Cang Jiao's expectations.
The young man who came to rescue Ku Shu was not from the Great Guessing Manor, nor was he from the Manor of Shifang. He was not even a Human Spirit, but a human. He found it unbelievable.
"Sigh, according to our speculations, the Great Guessing Manor has probably been completely annihilated." Shen Chengang also sighed.
After all, they had not been able to find any trace of them for so many years.
Unless they were dead, how could they hide for so long?
"However, there is finally hope now. This human called Luo Zheng actually confronted the Snake Spirit King directly. If Lord Ku Shu knew about this, he would be very pleased." A smile appeared on Cang Jiao's face.
Although Luo Zheng did not defeat the Snake Spirit King, he did rescue the Human Spirit Sect from a hopeless situation.
"I hope he can win," Shen Chengang said.
This battle was too important for them.
After the Snake Spirit King and Luo Zheng disappeared for nearly half an incense stick's worth of time, a crisp "whoosh" sounded in everyone's ears.
"They're fighting again?"
Everyone shivered. Their eyes and Divine Sense swept around, and they found the two figures in the open space in front of the Earth Altar.
The Snake Spirit King's two black blades pressed down, while Luo Zheng held the irregular heavy sword above his head. He said with a smile, "After chasing for so long, it's my turn now?"
The Origin Divine Blood had completely repaired his physical body, and now it was time to fight back.
As soon as his voice fell … the man and the snake disappeared from everyone's sight again.
The continuous sound of metal rubbing against each other appeared again.
"Luo Zheng's injuries have healed!"
"Now he's fighting back?"
"Even with a serious injury, he can still avoid all the attacks of the Snake Spirit King. Luo Zheng's current state … is so strong!"
Everyone present was intelligent, and they could see any subtle changes in the situation at a glance.
In this black cloud … the figures of Luo Zheng and the Snake Spirit King were constantly flashing.
The speed of the two was neither fast nor slow. The speed in the eyes of ordinary people didn't exist in this cloud.
Swathes of "Luo Zheng" approached swathes of "Spirit Snake King".
Each of Luo Zheng's movements was different. The irregular heavy sword chopped, stabbed, and turned … The Snake Spirit King's two blades blocked the sword, defended, and counterattacked crazily … In a breath's time, they fought back and forth tens of thousands of times! "So what if you've comprehended the probability extreme state?
In the end, it's only a draw. What can you do to me? "
The Snake Spirit King said.
Luo Zheng's face was calm with a faint smile. "The one behind you only gave you the probability extreme state?"
"What do you mean?"
The Snake Spirit King didn't know why Luo Zheng asked this question, but he had a bad feeling in his heart.
"The supernatural power from the fifth grid world is extraordinary, but it's a pity that there's only one probability extreme state …" Luo Zheng said as he held the hilt with one hand and the blade with the other. Energy rushed out from the irregular heavy sword again.

Chapter 3801
Wisps of light green flames escaped from the irregular heavy sword.
These green flames were like a viscous liquid, slowly climbing up Luo Zheng's arm and forming a green head.
The head looked terrifying, like an evil spirit crawling out of hell. It had horns on its head and fire coming out of its mouth! "Hua!"
The evil spirit's head opened its mouth and spat out a green flame, igniting the irregular heavy sword.
Luo Zheng looked at the Snake Spirit King through the green flame and said, "In theory, the extreme probabilistic state is unsolvable in the clouds … If we continue fighting like this, neither of us will be able to do anything to the other, because no one can kill an opponent with unlimited speed!
Of course, maintaining the extreme probabilistic state requires energy. I can exhaust you bit by bit in this cloud, but I have a better choice. "
Luo Zheng disappeared as soon as he finished speaking.
He disappeared and reappeared at the same time.
Under unlimited speed, space and distance lost their meaning.
Therefore, although he and the Snake Spirit King were separated by some distance, they could be seen as overlapping each other! "Hua!"
The irregular heavy sword chopped down at the Snake Spirit King's head.
But the Snake Spirit King's figure disappeared, so this attack was naturally useless.
"Although I don't know what this flame is for, it can't burn me no matter how powerful it is. You, you …" The Snake Spirit King stopped halfway through its words.
It saw a hole with a diameter of several hundred feet appear in the black cloud.
This green flame directly burned a part of the black cloud.
Luo Zheng turned to look at the Snake Spirit King, still with a faint smile on his face.
"Although the extreme probabilistic state is strong, there is a way to restrain it.
The way to restrain it is called … "" The extreme probabilistic state. "
The irregular heavy sword could only communicate with emotions, and the meaning it expressed could be understood but not expressed in words.
But when Luo Zheng obtained this ability, he naturally interpreted these words.
"Hua!"
Luo Zheng began to wave the irregular heavy sword in the black cloud.
The green flame spread everywhere.
The black cloud was like a huge curtain, and when burned by the green flame, a big hole would appear.
How fast was Luo Zheng's speed?
In an instant, he could create thousands of bodies and wave thousands of swords.
Thousands of green flames drew a big hole tens of thousands of feet wide in the black cloud! "Stop!"
The Snake Spirit King roared hysterically.
Then, it pounced on Luo Zheng again, trying to stop Luo Zheng from burning the black cloud.
But in terms of speed, Luo Zheng was comparable to the Spirit Snake King. As long as Luo Zheng kept dodging, the Spirit Snake King could do nothing to him.
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …" The irregular heavy sword in Luo Zheng's hand was now like a poker, fanning the flames everywhere.
A huge hole began to expand from the center of the black cloud at an extraordinarily fast speed.
After about an incense stick worth of time, this hole had already occupied two-thirds of the black cloud. After another half an incense stick worth of time, the black cloud that was twenty miles long and wide had basically disappeared, leaving only a few corners.
The black clouds at the corners were not connected to each other, but the Spirit Snake King could still move back and forth among them.
Now, the Spirit Snake King was as anxious as an ant on a hot pan, but what was the use of being anxious?
Soon, the black clouds at the corners were only about ten feet wide, and the Spirit Snake King was trapped in them.
At this time, Luo Zheng had already escaped from the black cloud, and the people on the altar could naturally see Luo Zheng's figure.
"Why is there only Luo Zheng?"
"Where's the Spirit Snake King?"
"What does he want to do?"
"The flame on the sword is so strange, it flows like liquid …" They watched as Luo Zheng floated in one direction, raised the irregular heavy sword, and swung it hard.
"Whoosh!"
The green flames burned the last part of the black cloud that the Spirit Snake King was hiding in.
The Spirit Snake King stumbled out of the black cloud.
"Hiss —" The Spirit Snake King spat out its tongue, looking angry.
The two black blades created a series of black flowers, slashing toward Luo Zheng's head.
After losing the black cloud, the probability extreme state lost its meaning, and the Spirit Snake King's speed returned to normal.
"Clang, clang, clang …" Luo Zheng easily blocked it.
Every time he blocked, the green flames would spread a little toward the black blade.
By the time the Spirit Snake King's attack was over, the green flames had quietly spread all over its body.
The flames released by the extreme state of Xin Huo didn't cause any burning pain, at least the Spirit Snake King couldn't feel it. So when the attack ended, it found itself shrouded in flames.
Luo Zheng, on the other hand, stood in the distance with a faint smile on his face.
"Damn it …" Just as the Spirit Snake King was about to recklessly rush up again, the green flames burst out and burned fiercely.
The flames didn't have any heat, but they had amazing destructive power.
Even the Spirit Snake King's body was burned little by little … "Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …" The Spirit Snake King's body was like a ball of ignited kerosene, and as the flames continued to burn, its body began to collapse and shrink.
In less than ten breaths of time, the Spirit Snake King was burned alive.
So far, there were only two ways for the Spirit Snake King to die in the Nirvana Realm. The first was to be killed by the Light of Destruction, the second was to be completely eroded by the Void Dust. The Spirit Snake King's death now belonged to the third way, which was unprecedented.
"The Spirit Snake King lost again!"
"It was burned alive!"
"Luo Zheng's flames can burn a Spirit Snake to death?"
At this moment, all the Spirits Snake King, Bird King, and Monkey King felt a chill run down their spines.
After all, as Spirits, they had the privilege of being immortal. Suddenly, there was another way to break this privilege, and they instinctively felt a sense of crisis in their hearts.
"Is it over?"
Someone asked.
"No, the Spirit Snake King's coordinates are still there …" someone replied.
Everyone's eyes converged on the sky again.
The Spirit Snake King's pitch-black coordinates were still fixed there.
Luo Zheng's figure flashed, and he was already high in the sky, holding the irregular heavy sword and slashing at the black coordinates without hesitation.
Without the protection of the Ethereal Sphere, the black coordinates naturally could not avoid Luo Zheng's attack.
But just as Luo Zheng swung his sword, a mass of black light suddenly lit up from the coordinates. This black light spread like a black hole, and at the same time, a pitch-black fist stretched out from the black hole and slammed into the irregular heavy sword.
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng felt an irresistible force transmitted from the irregular heavy sword, and then his whole body flew backward at a terrifying speed.
"Buzz! Buzz! Buzz … "In the process of flying backward, Luo Zheng kept using Space Moving. He repeatedly shuttled back and forth within a distance of 200 feet, and only stopped after shuttling back and forth hundreds of times.
In the face of the fist that suddenly appeared, Luo Zheng was not surprised. He looked at the black hole with a calm gaze. "The Spirit Snake King's power was given by you. Are you the real culprit behind the destruction of the Four Spirits Sect?"

Chapter 3802
A black fist slowly came out of the black hole.
It was followed by a head, shoulders, torso, and legs … This was something that looked like a human. Its head was cylindrical, and there were six pairs of hollowed out depressions that emitted a faint light. For the time being, they could be considered eyes.
Its hands and feet were longer than a human's, and they looked like sticks.
When it came out of the black hole, an invisible pressure was released.
This pressure was not soul pressure, but everyone present felt that it was extremely dangerous.
When Nuwa, Yuanshi Tianzun, and the others saw this black existence, their eyelids twitched, and even the blood in their bodies froze.
In fact, when they saw the black light in the Ethereal Sphere, they guessed that the Spirit Snake King had a backup plan.
However, when this "backup plan" appeared so openly, they could not hide their surprise.
When they heard Luo Zheng's question, they were even more shocked.
The invincible Four Spirit Gates had been destroyed by this guy?
The human spirits were even more excited.
They missed the past when the Four Spirit Gates were prosperous, but at the same time, they had been hiding deep doubts in their hearts.
Facing so many powerful existences and natural enemies of the Main Realm, the Four Spirit Gates had survived, so why had they been defeated in the end?
Even if the Spirit Snake King had done everything, it was impossible for it to counter the other three Spirit Gates … Now, they understood that there was something else behind the Spirit Snake King, and this thing's strength was probably far beyond their expectations.
"Then who is it … Why did it destroy our Four Spirit Gates!"
"What good would it do to destroy the Four Spirit Gates and the Main Realm …" Cang Jiao's body trembled slightly. He really could not understand why the Four Spirit Gates had to be destroyed.
Now, it was not only the monkey master, the bird master, and the human spirits who were curious.
Even the snake masters who were eagerly waiting were also puzzled.
Why had such a thing suddenly appeared?
Where was their king?
These snake masters were not stupid.
The Spirit Snake King's power must have come from this humanoid.
Its goal was to destroy the Four Spirit Gates?
Didn't the Spirit Snake King want them to dominate the Main Realm?
"Hehehehe …" The black humanoid let out a strange laugh.
The six pairs of eyes on the face stared at Luo Zheng and the irregular heavy sword in Luo Zheng's hand.
"Answer me!"
Luo Zheng's voice grew louder, steady and full of explosive power.
"Since you asked so sincerely, I can only be merciful and tell you," the humanoid said slowly. "Yes."
"You, you, why did you do this?!"
A voice rang out.
The one who spoke was a woman from the Music House.
The woman was too angry and couldn't help but ask.
She asked the question in everyone's mind.
The humanoid lowered its head and looked down. One of the six pairs of compound eyes flickered slightly.
Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched. He lifted the irregular heavy sword and plunged downwards. It seemed as if he wanted to slay something, but he was still a bit too slow and missed.
Then, the woman's screams could be heard from below.
"Ahhhhh …" Pain was a way for living creatures to protect themselves.
The Treacherous Body was tough and powerful. It was almost impossible to inflict severe pain on it. Even if the Treacherous Body was destroyed by the Light of Destruction, it wouldn't have let out such a shrill scream.
The woman's body began to boil, as if some kind of gas was spreading from her body. White bubbles kept bursting out of her body. After a few breaths, she was no longer in shape and finally turned into a ball of white mud … "Wu Xin!"
"What happened to you …" "Why did this happen?!"
The human spirits were shocked.
They couldn't understand why she would die in such pain! "Crack …" Then, everyone heard an explosion behind them.
A set of coordinates in the center of the Earth Altar burst into a dazzling golden light. This set of coordinates was burning fiercely and rapidly disintegrating! "Wu Xin's coordinates!"
Everyone's expressions turned even uglier. The disintegration of the coordinates meant that she was completely dead.
"Such a weak existence doesn't deserve an answer." The humanoid's voice rang out in the sky again. "I want to destroy you. What does it have to do with you?"
In the humanoid's eyes, the Four Spirits Sect was no different from ants.
This was a contempt that came from a higher level of existence.
Luo Zheng's face was gloomy, and he asked again, "You … are a demoter?"
After the Origin Divine Blood recovered from its muddle-headed state, the first thing it wanted to do was to find a demoter. Luo Zheng was helping it confirm this.
"Yes," the humanoid answered honestly.
"You really did lie to the Snake Spirit King. The so-called promotion and restoration of the entire Main Realm is a lie, right?"
Luo Zheng asked.
"Hahaha … Of course," the humanoid continued.
Hearing the humanoid's words, the Snake Spirit Kings froze on the spot.
The Snake Spirit King had always vowed to bring them back to the Main Realm and restore them to their former state. After all, the fourth grid world was the home of the Snake Spirit Kings. It was a world where they could live like fish in water.
The third grid world was too narrow and monotonous for them. It was like a normal person who had lost their eyes and lived in a world of darkness.
The Snake Spirit Kings were just confused, but now they understood that they had been led to a path of no return by the Snake Spirit King.
"Hiss …" "Black light! How dare you lie to me! "
The Snake Spirit King, who was hiding in the black coordinates, suddenly poked its head out and angrily swung its two black blades at the humanoid.
When it killed Taishang Laojun, the Snake Spirit King already had doubts.
However, it had no choice. It could only follow this path to the end and silently pray that this was not the truth.
However, when Black Light said it personally, the Snake Spirit King was still dominated by anger.
It could not wait to tear this liar into pieces! Before the Snake Spirit King's blades could get close to the humanoid, the pair of black blades had already shattered into pieces. Even the Snake Spirit King's black snake body was broken into pieces and returned to the coordinates … "In the Starry Realm, I destroyed the Yang and Dan Clans, who were a hundred times stronger than you …" "In the Infinite Realm, I deceived the insufferably arrogant Sheng Ming, who was an existence ten thousand times stronger than you …" "In the Infinite Realm of Gods, those infinite spirits who did not obey me were banished into time and decayed one by one. They only need a millionth of their power to turn you into Xu You …" "In the Infinite Realm of Minds, I am an existence that you can't even imagine for thousands of years …" The humanoid's six pairs of compound eyes glowed faintly.
That feeling of contempt filled almost everyone's heart.
It was a feeling of being high and mighty. It was not looking down on all living beings, because all living beings had never entered its eyes.
"What do you think the Four Spirit Gates are in my eyes?"
The humanoid asked.

Chapter 3803
The Bird Masters, the Monkey Masters, and the Human Spirits all stared at the humanoid in the sky with stunned expressions.
When these words were said by the humanoid's powerful aura, they were indeed extremely shocked.
What were the Dan Clan and the Yang Clan?
Could they be a hundred times stronger than the Four Spirit Sects?
What was the Shengming?
Ten thousand times stronger than them?
And there were even stronger existences above the Shengming?
What were the realms that the humanoid was talking about?
Cang Jiao had the most peculiar expression among the crowd.

The Lower Extreme Realm is at the level of the Chaotic Realm … After hearing the humanoid's words, Luo Zheng's eyes lit up slightly. He seemed to be a little tempted.

The humanoid asked.

Do you have the strength to kill me? "

Luo Zheng gently lifted the irregular sword. The energy in the cracks on the sword continued to flow.

The black hand did not hit Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng groaned. He was hit by a violent impact. He fell to the ground like a falling meteor.
"Boom!"
At the same time that Luo Zheng hit the ground, the humanoid opened its palm toward the ground.
"Whoosh …" A black cloud that only the humanoid and Luo Zheng could see shrouded them.
This was the free space belonging to the Probability Extreme State.
Luo Zheng's Probability Extreme State and Torch Extreme State had not been removed by the irregular heavy sword.
Since he was shrouded by the black cloud, Luo Zheng quickly split into thousands of humanoid figures. These thousands of humanoid figures jumped up at the same time. The irregular heavy sword in their hands spun, creating tornado-like turquoise flames.

Chapter 3804

Hange fell silent.

After thinking it through, the Origin Divine Blood launched two or three soul attacks on Luo Zheng's mind.
These soul attacks were naturally blocked by the white flash and then extinguished with lightning speed.

Even though Luo Zheng was still stubbornly killing those humanoid figures and constantly absorbing the white flash, the speed of consumption still could not keep up with the speed of replenishment.
As the Origin Divine Blood's soul attacks became denser and denser, the white flash was consumed faster and faster!
The hostility on Luo Zheng's face weakened bit by bit. His originally bright red eyes also slowly regained a trace of clarity.

"You can actually break free from the extreme state of mind?

Chapter 3806

Chapter 3807

Ku Shu clapped his hands three times in a row.
Those who knew Ku Shu knew his habit. When he was very excited, he would clap his hands.
"I've always believed that fate plays tricks on people. The high and mighty deep space still gives us a chance. How can we not seize it?"
Ku Shu smiled.
The tunnel was peaceful, and there was even a reunion between father and daughter.

When the beings of the 33 Heavens on the other shore saw the golden pillar of light from top to bottom, all of them were left speechless.
What kind of existence could have such a supernatural power? It could directly penetrate the Nirvana Realm! Tongtian and the Yuan Ling Civilization were the ones who sensed the danger in advance.
They didn't participate in the battle in the Clear Jade Sky, but they knew about it. At the same time, they also knew that the Nirvana Realm would probably undergo earth-shaking changes.
From the perspective of the Yuan Ling Civilization, they definitely hoped that the Snake Spirit Sect would win.
Tongtian was thinking about how to negotiate with the Monkey Spirit Sect after the Bird Spirit Sect won. While he was waiting in the Shang Qing Sky, he noticed that the water of the Divine River began to flow backward. Since ancient times, the Divine River only flowed in one direction. Flowing backward meant that there would be great chaos in the Clear Jade Sky.
Then he saw circles of black silhouettes in the sky, and huge purple-black energy balls slowly fell! These purple-black energy balls were so powerful that almost nothing could stop them. After the energy balls eroded the Clear Jade Sky, they began to fall toward the Shang Qing Sky. If no one stopped them, these energy balls could swallow the Nirvana Realm! The humanoid didn't intend to stop them for the time being.
A purple streamer spread from afar. As the purple streamer spread, a huge bird shape appeared in front of the humanoid. It was the unawakened Bird Spirit King, Shou.
In fact, Shou could never be awakened. It had been controlled by the humanoid. Once the Snake Spirit King failed, the humanoid would activate the Bird Spirit King, which was its backup.
However, in the battle with Luo Zheng, the humanoid used a clever method to seize the sword, and the Bird Spirit King lost its chance to fight.
However, the humanoid didn't choose to kill it. Instead, he made it his mount.
Shou obediently lowered its head, and the humanoid stepped on the bird's back.
Because the Clear Jade Sky had been swallowed by the energy balls, the humanoid could directly look at the Shang Qing Sky below. Although it was far away, he still found the Sect Leader through the gaps of the energy balls.
The Sect Leader naturally saw the humanoid in the sky, and his heart suddenly tightened.
Although he was mentally prepared for the situation, it seemed to be a hundred times worse than he imagined.
The entire Clear Jade Sky was destroyed?
And the Bird Spirit King was enslaved by that weird guy?
Was there anyone who could tell him what had happened?
The gaze was like the claws of a monster, directly grabbing the Sect Leader's heart.
He hurriedly took out the Ring of Descension. As a smart person, saving his life was the most important thing now. After all, his biggest backer had met with misfortune.
"Ha …" The humanoid saw the Sect Leader's action of running for his life and just let out a faint laugh. He rode the Bird Spirit King and left.
The Sect Leader pulled the Ring of Descension and froze in place. As the leader of the Wu Kong Clan, he still had considerable weight in the Nirvana Realm. However, he was directly ignored by that guy. A sense of humiliation rose from the bottom of his heart, and he even had the impulse to fight with that guy.
However, looking at the slowly descending purple-black energy ball, reason prevailed in the end. After the guy used the Ring of Descension, he disappeared from the Shang Qing Sky.
Soon after, the Clear Jade Sky disappeared, and the purple-black energy ball appeared in the sky above the Tai Qing Sky.
There were many Nirvana Realm races in the Tai Qing Sky. They never thought that the Clear Jade Sky would be destroyed one day, so when they saw this scene, their expressions were extremely interesting.
Some of the Nirvana Realm creatures reacted quickly and began to escape to the thirty-second Ping Yi Sky, but most of them were stunned in place. When they reacted, there was no chance to escape.
They watched themselves, their companions, and their home that they had lived for hundreds of millions of years being destroyed.

However, the only person that Elder Ying was concerned about was Ling Shuang. However, ever since she entered that door, there was no news from Ling Shuang. During this period, Elder Ying was depressed and lived alone on Sword Comprehension Peak.
