"Where is she? Where is she?"
Elder Ying asked hurriedly.
Luo Zheng held the Ring of Descent in his hand and said before closing his eyes to operate the True Meaning of Tao, "You will see her soon."
"Phew …" The next moment, Luo Zheng appeared in the tunnel.
He gave the three Rings of Descent to Ling Shuang, Ku Shu, and Wen Dishi respectively. Then, the three of them began to descend.
"Buzz …" The three figures outlined below the three Rings of Descent.
Ling Shuang had just left the Nirvana Realm and was still not used to it. When she heard "Shuang 'er" calling her, she trembled and her eyes glistened with tears. "Grandma!"
Although Ling Shuang was the reincarnation of Ku Shu's daughter and had an indistinct bond with Ku Shu, her feelings for Elder Ying were the most sincere. After this exchange, she threw herself into Elder Ying's arms.
Fuxi, Nuwa, Donghuang, and the Chaos Ancient Gods focused their attention on Ku Shu and Wen Dishi.
This white-furred monster stood there by itself. Just the faintly discernible aura from it made them feel even more pressured.
Nuwa and Fuxi had seen Humankind before. For example, the Three Palaces of Human and Spirit who sought refuge in Yejian Island. However, even Lord Hei and Shen Chengang could not be compared to this Humankind.
"Luo Zheng, why don't you introduce these two?"
Nuwa said.
At this moment, Ling Shuang said, "Grandma, this is my father!"
"Your father?"
Elder Ying looked over with an incredulous expression.
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and said, "This is Lord Ku Shu, the Human Spirit King. This is Lord Wen Dishi, the Lord of Great Guessing."
The Human Spirit King was a well-known name. He was also the target of Luo Zheng's expedition to Jade Clear Sky.
Ku Shu nodded at Elder Ying and said, "Thank you for taking care of Ling Shuang in the Chaos World."
Elder Ying was even more shocked. Although she knew Ling Shuang's background was extraordinary, how could she expect her father to be a Human Spirit King?
Nuwa, Fuxi, and the others had similar expressions. After a short moment of shock, they bowed one by one.
"I'm Nuwa. Greetings to Lord Ku Shu …" "I'm Fuxi. Greetings to …" "I'm …" Ku Shu smiled and said, "No need to be so courteous. I, Ku Shu, am still alive because of your efforts. I should thank you!"
"It's also because of the Deep Space's protection. I didn't expect my Humankind to be saved by the Human Race …" Wen Dishi echoed.
Next, Luo Zhengcai recounted what had happened in the Three Pure Heavens.
Nuwa, Fuxi, and the others understood the ins and outs of the whole thing.
"Then, our true bodies … are all dead?"
Fuxi said with his eyes wide open. He didn't expect the situation to be so tragic.
On the contrary, Nuwa was as calm as usual. She pointed out the key point and asked, "As you said, if the sword fell on the person who fell, wouldn't we lose for sure?"
"So we came here to find a chance to reverse the situation," Ku Shu replied.
"There's such a chance in the Land of Origin?
Is it the Origin Divine Blood? "
Nuwa asked in confusion. The Origin Divine Blood could be used anywhere. It didn't need to come to the Chaos World.
"It's not the Origin Divine Blood." Luo Zheng pointed in the direction of the Ultimate Cemetery. "Our chance is there."

Chapter 3816
Nuwa, Fuxi, Donghuang, and the others were puzzled.
The opportunity to reverse the downfall was in the Sinkhole Graveyard?
Although the Sinkhole Graveyard was Grand Supreme Elderly Lord's painstaking effort, it could not even protect itself after the downfall of Chaos. How could there be the ability to reverse the downfall?
Coincidentally … Just as they were puzzled, a bright light suddenly rose from the Sinkhole Graveyard.
Whoosh!
This bright beam of light shot straight up into the sky from bottom to top.
"What is this!?"
"What terrifying energy … Who else in the Sinkhole Graveyard has such a divine ability?"
Fuxi, Donghuang, and the three Chaos Ancient Gods were dumbfounded.
Although Mount Li was adjacent to the Sinkhole Graveyard, there was still a considerable distance between the two massive objects. However, they could still clearly feel the exaggerated power from such a distance! The power of this beam of light far exceeded that of a Primal Chaos Elder God and Immortal Realm expert … Swish … If it wasn't stopped, this beam of light would most likely pierce through the blood-colored sky and hit the outside of the Land of Origin.
However, just as this beam of light approached the blood-red sky, pieces of green honeycomb-shaped energy blocks appeared. Although the beam of light was extremely powerful, it emitted a sharp tearing sound when it hit the energy blocks. The two of them canceled each other out and quickly disappeared into thin air.
"Did you guys do this?
Is this the opportunity you were talking about? "
Nuwa asked.
Luo Zheng was also dumbfounded. Fortunately, he knew everything about the Land of Origin. With a quick thought, he already knew the situation. With a smile on his face, he said, "We didn't do it, but the opportunity is indeed there. Everyone, follow me!"
Luo Zheng waved his hand, and everyone was enveloped by a layer of space. The next moment, they appeared in the Sinkhole Graveyard.
On a mountain wall in the Sinkhole Graveyard, Hua Tianming was still holding Solitary Sword in a daze.
Ever since Luo Nian removed the other end of the link to the Holy Pillar, Solitary Sword had been unresponsive like a piece of scrap metal. Hua Tianming did not mind. According to Luo Nian, he would seek greater power for Solitary Sword.
Today, Hua Tianming suddenly sensed Solitary Sword's abnormal behavior.
Silver yellow patterns suddenly grew on the surface of the originally plain and ordinary sword. These patterns spread from bottom to top, almost covering the entire sword. At the same time, he felt an astonishing energy being transmitted from the sword.
This power was completely different from the previous power. Although Gu Jian's power was strong, it was very violent and difficult to control. It did not match Hua Tianming's temperament.
Now, the energy contained in the Solitary Sword was as vast as the ocean, and it filled Hua Tianming's chest like flowing water.
"Shua!"
He couldn't help but swing his sword. Then, he was shocked by the power of the sword.
This power … was not something that he should be able to control! Luo Nian really did it! While Hua Tianming was deep in thought, Luo Zheng suddenly appeared with a group of people.
"Luo Zheng! Luo Zheng! Luo Nian did it. This sword, this Lone Sword has become so strong … "Hua Tianming was so excited that he wanted to share his joy with Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng nodded with a smile. "You've succeeded!"
If there were no Degraders, Luo Zheng would be as overjoyed as Hua Tianming. After all, Hua Tianming would become Luo Zheng's strongest assistant.
However, the Degraders they were facing now could not be defeated by the main world's power.
"Why is there such a strong power? Even a level 33 Nirvana token is not possible. How did Luo Nian do it?"
Mo asked in confusion.
At this moment, Ku Shu spoke. "Because this sword is connected to the Light of Broken Dawn. That was once my strongest Saint's item."
"So it's a Saint's item …" Hua Tianming murmured. He suddenly reacted and stared at Ku Shu with a strange gaze. "This was once your Saint's item. You are …" "This is the Human Spiritual King Ku Shu," Luo Zheng introduced. "Luo Nian must have climbed onto the Human Spiritual Sect's Saint Pillar."
Hua Tianming was stunned for a while. Suddenly, he took two steps forward and held the Solitary Sword horizontally in front of Ku Shu. "If so, this item should be returned to its rightful owner!"
With Hua Tianming's temperament, he naturally would not covet other people's items.
However, Ku Shu smiled faintly and shook his head. "That was something that once belonged to me. The sea has changed, and the mulberry fields have changed. As the 'original owner', I don't live up to my name. This is your sword, so it should belong to you."
Hua Tianming wanted to say something, but suddenly felt the Solitary Sword being fiddled with. It was Luo Nian pulling the link of the Saint's item. "Luo Nian seems to have something to say!"
"What did he say?"
Luo Zheng asked quickly.
The Solitary Sword itself was not that important. What was important was that it could connect to Luo Nian.
Hua Tianming held the Solitary Sword and patiently sensed it for a while. Then, he showed an expression of not knowing whether to laugh or cry. "He said 'Uncle Tianming'. This sword has become very powerful, right?
Are you satisfied? "
This kid was showing off on the top of the Saint Pillar.
"Powerful, very powerful. This is the strongest Nirvana Token!"
Hua Tianming responded.
Luo Nian had spent a lot of effort to embed the black cube on the top of the Saint Pillar. At this moment, he was very proud, especially when he found his father and the others beside Hua Tianming. He fiddled with the link again and said, "If you're not satisfied, I can change it for you. I still have a few places here!"
"That's not necessary. Your father has something to say to you," Hua Tianming said as he handed the Solitary Sword to Luo Zheng. He could see that Luo Zheng and the others were here for Luo Nian.
This kind of communication was very time-consuming. Luo Zheng did not beat around the bush and said directly, "Nian 'er, you've done well. Mastering this sword is enough to make Uncle Tianming the number one person in the Chaotic World. However, the enemy we face is even stronger. We need your help."
"Stronger?
How strong can it be? "
Luo Nian replied disapprovingly.
"Have you seen the deep space?"
Luo Zheng asked.
"You mean there's a lot of debris floating in the sky?
I've seen it. I can see it from a certain position on the Saint Pillar, "Luo Nian replied.
He could see it when he climbed up the Saint Pillar of the Snake Spirit Gate. He could also see it from the Saint Pillar of the Human Spirit Gate.
Luo Zheng continued, "The enemy we face comes from there. Therefore, we need you to go to the deep space to find a person, a person with the surname Xue."
There was another key point in the plan proposed by Ku Shu. Luo Nian could walk freely in the main world, but could he walk freely in the deep space?
Whether it was Elder Xue, Ku Shu, or Shou, their state in the deep space was the same as Luo Zheng's state when he entered the main world. Their vision was dark, and it was very difficult to walk because they did not belong to the fifth grid world.
Fortunately, the deep space was in a broken state. A considerable part of the space had been reduced to the main world, so the Four Spirit Gates had the opportunity to explore the deep space.
Luo Nian did not think about this. He did not care about the difficulty of the task at all. Instead, he was eager to prove his ability in front of his father. "The deep space is high above. How can I go there?"

Chapter 3817
The deep space and the main world were two completely different worlds. With Luo Nian's strength, it was impossible for him to cross over.
"This problem …" Luo Zheng looked at the Human Spirit King.
Luo Zheng didn't even know much about the main world, let alone the deep space.
The Human Spirit King immediately said, "It's in the Eternal Divine Court. There's a teleportation array at the top of the Eternal Divine Court. You can go there through that teleportation array."
The Four Spirit Sects attached great importance to the deep space. It was almost their religion.
Therefore, they spent a lot of time and manpower to build a teleportation array at the top of the Eternal Divine Court. This showed how much importance they attached to the deep space. The Four Spirit Sects monopolized all the resources in the deep space.
"The Eternal Divine Court … is that courtyard chained up?"
Luo Nian asked.
"Yes!"
Ku Shu said.
"I'm afraid it'll be very difficult to activate such a teleportation array …" Luo Zheng asked this time. After all, it was a teleportation array that connected two worlds.
"Don't worry. The teleportation array's energy is supplied by the Time Lock. As long as he can successfully reach the top of the Eternal Divine Court, he'll definitely be able to activate the teleportation array …" Ku Shu said confidently.
Many of the legacies left behind by the Four Spirit Sects were eternal. They could only be destroyed if the entire main world was degraded.
Ku Shu was very patient.
Although the other party was a human with insignificant strength, the hope of the entire world was tied to this human.
He explained his understanding of the deep space to Luo Nian in detail, as well as the possible situations he might encounter.
Hearing Ku Shu's description, Luo Zheng and the others felt very curious.
For example, when people from the three grid worlds saw the main world, they saw countless snow-white light spots. When people from the main world saw the deep space, they saw circles. It seemed that each grid world would create a cognitive barrier for the creatures in the next grid world.
It took the Human Spirit King four hours to finish. In the end, Luo Nian only replied with a "Got it" and didn't reply.
"I hope Luo Nian can make the trip this time," Nuwa said worriedly.
"He can definitely do it. The opportunity is on our side!"
Ku Shu had a confident look on his face. Whenever he was facing a crisis, he would always consider the possibility of failure. Only in this way could he maximize his potential.
Luo Zheng didn't say much. He felt guilty about letting Luo Nian take the risk, but now the whole Chaotic World could only count on him. There was no other choice … Ku Shu felt that everyone was a little depressed, so he smiled and said to Hua Tianming, "The Light of Broken Dawn is very powerful. I'll teach you how to use it, which can fully stimulate the potential of this holy tool …" "Lord Ku Shu, please enlighten me!"
Hua Tianming cupped his hands and said.
After that, Ku Shu took the Lone Sword from Hua Tianming's hands. He gripped it lightly, and the patterns on the surface of the sword became brighter and fluctuated, turning into endless energy flowers that bloomed continuously.
"It's not difficult to control the Light of Broken Dawn. When you use its energy, you have to separate it and release it. After releasing it, you have to return to one …" Ku Shu held the hilt of his sword and tapped it lightly.
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …" Bright rays of light shot out in the shape of a gourd. They fluctuated twice and then converged together.
"Buzz …" After converging, the sword rays seemed to become sticky, and their speed was reduced by half. However, the sword rays turned into a strange shape. When they shot into the sky, they divided into hundreds of sword rays. These sword rays contained special rules and flew continuously, dazzling people's eyes.
While Ku Shu was still teaching Hua Tianming, Luo Nian had already reached the edge of the Holy Pillar and crawled along the Chain of Time.
He had crawled back and forth on the chain several times and had already gotten used to the topography of the chain.
Two days later, Luo Nian finally crossed this section of the chain and once again climbed up to the Eternal Holy Court, which was located in the center of the Four Holy Pillars.
"There is a magic array on the roof of the Eternal Holy Court. I didn't find it before …" Previously, Luo Nian had been thinking about how to enter the Eternal Holy Court. After searching for a while in vain, he gave up exploring this courtyard. This time, he went straight to the roof of the upper level.
The main hall of the Eternal Holy Court was octagonal. There was a raised roof in the middle. Beyond the roof was a platform that was ten meters long and wide. At the four corners of the platform were four Time Locks.
Luo Nian observed for a while. The teleportation array that the Human Spirit King had mentioned was most likely on the platform.
After standing on the roof and observing for a while, Luo Zheng jumped onto the platform. The moment he stood on the platform, the four fixed Time Locks began to emit a faint blue light.
These lights were like water droplets. They began to flow along the grooves on the platform. The lights from the four directions constantly intertwined and finally converged into one.
"Whoosh —" Spots of blue light began to converge, forming a diamond-shaped magic array on the ground.
"This is the teleportation array?"
Luo Nian's eyebrows twitched.
He was filled with curiosity about everything. The deep space was also very attractive to him. He wanted to see what a higher level world looked like! Without any hesitation, Luo Nian stepped into the diamond-shaped magic array.
"Whoosh!"
The diamond-shaped magic array began to flicker.
Luo Nian saw beams of light rise from the diamond-shaped magic array. These beams of light drilled into Luo Nian's arm. A hole as thick as a pinky finger appeared on his arm, as if it had been eaten by worms. His flesh and bones as thick as a thumb disappeared! "How can this be!"
Luo Nian was shocked.
Fortunately, he didn't feel any pain. It was as if the hole wasn't growing on his body.
"Whoosh …" Several more beams of light rose. Two of them rose from the bottom of Luo Nian's feet. They pierced through his body like mutton skewers. If one were to lie on top of Luo Nian and look down, they would see a perfectly straight hole.
"This, this … this teleportation array is going to split me into countless parts?"
Luo Nian was indeed shocked. This was equivalent to opening a hole in his head, but he still didn't feel any pain.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …" More and more beams of light rose from Luo Nian's feet. Luo Nian's body was also filled with holes.
As more and more holes appeared, less and less parts of Luo Nian's body remained. In the end, only a small half of his fingers and half of his instep were left. When the remaining beams of light accurately shot at these "broken limbs," Luo Nian had completely disappeared from the Holy Pillar.
The beams of light shot upwards, and Luo Nian felt as if there was a force supporting his body upwards, and upwards … After an unknown amount of time, the beams of light finally reached their destination. Then, the beams of light transformed back into beams of 'flesh and blood.' These beams of flesh and blood pieced together seamlessly, and began to restore Luo Nian's body.
When the last beam of light hit Luo Nian's instep, his body was finally complete. Only then did he open his eyes and look into the distance.
Ku Shu had warned him repeatedly that if Luo Nian couldn't understand the Deep Space World, he had to keep going right.
The Deep Space Scripture had been reduced by more than half. There were many main worlds distributed within it. If he walked all the way to the right, he would reach the nearest main world.
But after looking into the distance, the corners of Luo Nian's mouth curled up high. His face revealed a proud expression.
He could still clearly see these "circles" that formed the Deep Space World!

Chapter 3818
Although it was the first time for Luo Nian to enter the deep space, he still had a hunch that he could understand this space.
Luo Nian had this confidence since he entered the Xuanliang World, so he wasn't too surprised when he saw the environment of the deep space.
He was in a circular secret chamber.
The walls of the secret chamber were carved with simple patterns. Although they looked like some kind of hieroglyph, he couldn't understand any of the shapes described by the characters.
"Should I go to the right?"
Luo Nian took a few steps and chose the passage on the right. Looking along the passage, he saw a broken path. The other end of the passage was broken, and the end had been reduced to a space of four spaces.
Ku Shu had prepared this path for Luo Nian because he was afraid that Luo Nian couldn't move freely in the deep space, so it was a good choice to walk with a part of the space of four spaces.
Now Luo Nian had no obstacles in the deep space, so he didn't need to choose this broken path.
Exiting the passage on the right, he walked toward a path in front of him.
This was a long and narrow passage. The passage was paved with transparent bricks, and each brick had countless small rings emitting light. It was these lights that illuminated the entire passage.
Measured according to the scale of the world of the third space, this passage was about three thousand feet long. He reached the end in a short while.
When he stepped out of the passage, a burst of dazzling light made him dizzy. After slowly adjusting to it, he looked up and was shocked by the wonderful scene in front of him.
There was a huge golden halo hanging in the sky, which projected endless light in all directions.
"Is this the sun in the deep space?"
Luo Nian was shocked.
Although Luo Nian's cultivation was not high, what he had seen over the years had greatly expanded his vision. His vision was not inferior to those powerful figures in the Chaotic World.
Only half of the ring-shaped sun was intact, while the other half was in a broken state. The golden fragments had turned into ribbons, still emitting heat and light.
"Half of the sun has been degraded, while the other half is intact. Was it because the person who degraded it was not powerful enough?"
Luo Nian murmured.
After looking away from the ring-shaped sun, Luo Nian immediately looked straight ahead. What appeared in the horizontal direction was the huge wheel-shaped building, and Luo Nian was at the edge of the wheel.
After communicating with the bitter tree, Luo Nian also had a general understanding of the deep space. This wheel-shaped building was called "Skywheel City" by the Four Spirits Sect. It was the most magnificent building in the deep space. Most of the excavation of the deep space by the Four Spirits Sect revolved around Skywheel City.
"We have never been close to the center of Skywheel City. The structure there is extremely complex. We have tried many times but failed to go there. I suggest you set that as your goal."
This was Ku Shu's suggestion to Luo Nian.
"I wonder if that Xue guy's soul is inside …" Luo Nian curled his lips.
Although he was overjoyed to accept this mission, the Human Spirit King had given him too few hints, and the Four Spirit Sect's understanding of the deep space was like a blind man groping for an elephant. Their efficiency was extremely poor, and he was afraid that they would not be as clear and real as Luo Nian's!
After being confused for a while, Luo Nian still walked towards the center of Skywheel City. No matter what, he was carrying the hope of the whole human race, the whole human spirit, and the whole chaotic world.
Thinking that he had such a difficult task on his shoulders, his heart burned with passion.
Now that he was so important, he was overjoyed …
Dozens of huge paths radiated from the center of Skywheel City, which were connected to the outer edge of the wheel-shaped building. Luo Nian walked on one of the paths.
At first, Luo Nian walked cautiously. He was afraid that some strange things would suddenly appear in Skywheel City.
It must be known that even the monsters in the chaotic world were enough to make Luo Nian suffer, let alone the things in the Xuanliang world. The monsters in the deep space could only be stronger and more terrible. Any one of them could take his life.
But as Luo Nian walked, he observed his surroundings. About an hour later, it was quiet all around. Especially since he was the only one on this large and wide path. The fear in his heart gradually dissipated, and his gait gradually became exaggerated.
"Senior Jiang Ziya can't even move an inch in the Xuanliang world. If I drag him into the main world, what will happen?
It will be worse than the Xuanliang world. I can't even move … "Luo Nian thought.
It was a pity that climbing the Holy Pillar took too long. Even without those statues blocking the way, it would still take more than two months. Otherwise, he would have really brought Jiang Ziya up.
"Rustle …"
While Luo Nian was walking and thinking, a strange sound suddenly came to his ears.
It was like the sound of sand sprinkling on a wooden board.
He, who was originally relaxed, suddenly stopped. With a suspicious look, he looked at the path in front and behind.
There was nothing …
Was it an illusion?
Luo Nian shook his head and continued to walk.
After a dozen steps, the rustling sound came to Luo Nian's ears again.
This time, he heard it very clearly. The sound came from the back of the path!
The path itself was suspended in the air. Luo Nian couldn't see what was on the other side. Now, he didn't dare to look. In a hurry, he directly ran.
"Sha sha sha sha sha …"
Luo Nian ran faster, and the frequency of the rustling sound also increased.
The thing on the other side of the path had been following him!
The fear of the unknown cast a shadow in his heart. He didn't think he could counter that thing. He had no choice but to speed up.
"Sha sha sha sha sha …"
As Luo Nian walked farther, the rustling sound on the other side became more and more intense. More and more things joined in the chase.
After running like this for several miles, the rustling sound got closer and closer. Luo Nian glanced at the two sides of the path, and his face turned pale with fright.
Black "sticks" as thick as fingers appeared on both sides of the path. The rustling sound was the sound of these black sticks rubbing against the ground.
And on the other side of these black sticks, there were pancake-shaped objects. Around these pancake-shaped objects, there was a circle of sharp teeth.
"Spiders …"
A bone-chilling feeling struck his heart.
These creatures in the deep space were very similar to spiders, but their legs were thinner and their bodies smaller. Their appearance was even more bizarre and terrifying!
"That Human Spiritual King is a liar!"
Luo Nian shouted in his heart.
Ku Shu had talked to him in detail for four hours, but he didn't mention these terrifying monsters!
In fact, Ku Shu couldn't be blamed for this.
The situation in the deep space was very similar to that in the Xuanliang World. After an incomplete degradation, the powerful beings that had once dominated the deep space were all dead. Even the weakest creatures were wiped out.
But time was the best antidote. After more than a thousand chaos epochs, the deep space that had been reduced to a forbidden land of life gradually gave birth to some strange creatures. These "spiders" were one of them.
"Rustle …"
One of the "spiders" was the first to approach Luo Nian. One of its black slender legs stabbed at Luo Nian like a javelin.
Luo Nian nimbly dodged this stab. But before he could land on the ground, he felt a pain in his chest. Another black slender leg pierced through his chest. Then, his feet left the ground and his body was directly lifted up.

Chapter 3819
Luo Nian did not have the slightest room to resist. After being picked up in the air for half a circle, he was thrown on the back of the "spider".
Although his chest was pierced, the wound was not fatal. Luo Nian wanted to roll over to break free from the "spider", but the fangs on the back of the "spider" suddenly grew and formed a cage to imprison him.
After capturing Luo Nian, the other spiders scattered back to the back of the passage.
The spider that caught Luo Nian ran forward with its long legs.
Sitting on top of the "spider", Luo Nian stopped the bleeding on his chest and stretched out his hands to grab the two fangs and tried to pry them open.
But as soon as his hands burst out with power, the two fangs suddenly turned red, like two pieces of red-hot iron.
Luo Nian quickly released his hands.
"I can't get away …" Luo Nian said helplessly.
In the Mysterious World, Luo Nian's luck had been relatively good, and he had not encountered any crisis.
Even the most troublesome bronze statues, he only needed to lead them down and let Jiang Ziya kill them.
Now, for the first time, Luo Nian faced these indescribable things. His face looked calm, but his heart was very flustered.
The only consolation was that the "spider" had only trapped him and did not swallow him. Moreover, the "spider" itself was moving toward the center of the "Skywheel City", and its speed was ten times faster than Luo Nian's.
"Sha sha sha sha …"
The passage of the Skywheel City did not seem to be far, but the actual length was amazing.
After ten hours, they had only walked less than half the distance. After a full day and night, the "spider" brought Luo Nian close to the center of the Skywheel City.
If Luo Nian were to walk on this road, it would take at least ten days. It could be regarded as an indirect help, but he was not happy at all.
After approaching the center of the Skywheel City, the "spider" carrying him entered a wide arched building.
As soon as he entered, Luo Nian saw many "spiders" slowly moving forward in a line, and these "spiders" also carried a variety of creatures on their backs.
On the back of the "spider" in front of Luo Nian, there was a round bug imprisoned. The bug kept trying to struggle, but it couldn't escape those sharp teeth. Occasionally, the bug's body would come into contact with the sharp teeth, and it would be scalded by the scalding sharp teeth.
A little monster with long arms, legs, and a tail was locked up on the back of a "spider" in front of them. The little monster sat quietly in the cage with sharp fangs. Its red eyes stared at Luo Nian, and there seemed to be a faint smile on its face.
Luo Nian frowned. He looked past the little monster and continued to observe the other trapped creatures.
The things in the deep space were mysterious and incomprehensible in the eyes of the creatures with four and three compartments. Even their forms were indescribable. However, in Luo Nian's eyes, they seemed somewhat bottomless.
Most of the creatures that were sent here were very primitive. The only one that seemed to have intelligence was that little monster …
The long line of "spiders" moved three steps with each step. Luo Nian felt uneasy. These spiders definitely didn't bring him here to watch a show. He just didn't know what kind of trouble he would encounter.
After moving a certain distance inside the arched building, a triangular platform appeared in front of them. At the bottom of the platform was a gap about a meter wide. The "spiders" were tightly wedged into the gap, and it was perfect for sending the prey in the cage into the triangular platform.
Luo Nian was concentrating on observing the triangular platform when he suddenly saw a flash of golden light and a strange cry. Then, the "spider" retreated from the triangular platform.
The prey on the back of the "spider" had disappeared, leaving only bits and pieces of carbide. The "spider" dumped the carbide on the ground and left.
"Dead, dead. This is going to be troublesome!"
Luo Nian stared blankly at this scene.
What were these spiders trying to do …
Did they capture these creatures from so far away just to burn them to death?
One by one, the "spiders" entered the triangular platform. After a flash of golden light, the prey on their backs had been burned to ashes!
Not long after, it was the turn of the little monster with long arms, legs, and a tail.
The little monster seemed to be quite calm. That distinctive face of his had a smile on it from beginning to end.
"You're about to die, and you're still smiling." Luo Nian bit his lip.
But after waiting for a while, the golden light in the triangular platform didn't light up. The "spider" dragged the little monster out unscathed. After the little monster came out, its smile became even more brilliant. It even stretched out its hands and made a cutting motion on its own neck, making a sound like a pig's howl.
Finally, he saw a living creature come out alive. Luo Nian was overjoyed. No matter what, there was a chance of survival in the triangular platform. At least he had a chance!
"Sha sha sha …"
Next was the round insect in front of Luo Nian. A few breaths after the insect was sent in, a flash of golden light lit up. By the time it was carried out by the "spider", it had been burned to ashes.
"It's my turn!"
Luo Nian took a deep breath and adjusted his mental state to 120%.
After being sent to the golden platform, a huge mirror appeared in front of him.
On the left side of the mirror, there were 15 small squares neatly arranged. On the right side, there was only one small square.
"This is … a test?"
Luo Nian was stunned for a moment. He quickly reached out and moved the small square into the 15 smaller squares, forming a complete large square.
After he finished this step, the scene on the mirror quickly changed again. This time, there were more than a dozen squares mixed together. What Luo Nian had to do was rearrange them …
"That's it?"
He completed three questions in an extremely short time and passed the test. The "spider" carried him out again.
These questions were easy for a three-year-old, so Luo Nian naturally completed them with ease. But after all, he had just walked on the edge of life and death. He felt relieved.
"These spiders catch prey to filter out intelligent creatures. Unintelligent creatures are directly burned to death, while intelligent creatures can live …"
The naked man's heart calmed down a lot.
No matter what the other party was planning, as long as it was a creature he could communicate with!
After the spider carried Luo Nian out of the arched building, it went down the stairs to a huge underground space. On both sides of the underground space were cages. These cages also held all kinds of creatures. These creatures had already been screened once. They were all intelligent creatures.
When Luo Nian was sent in, he attracted all kinds of attention. There were even creatures who greeted Luo Nian through the bars, letting out strange cries of excitement.
Since there was a language barrier, Luo Nian couldn't be bothered. When the "spider" found an empty cage, it stuffed Luo Nian into it.

Chapter 3820
The space inside the cage was limited, barely enough for one person to sit cross-legged.
Luo Nian grabbed the bars with both hands and looked around. His mind was in a mess. Who knew what he would face next?
There were probably thousands of creatures in this underground space. They came in all shapes and sizes, and even their languages were different. They were all chattering in different ways, as if they wanted to communicate with each other, but they couldn't understand each other.
"Although these creatures have some intelligence, most of them don't seem to be very intelligent …" Luo Nian frowned. "What the hell is this place? Why do they want to capture me …" Although Luo Nian was smart, he was far from his father's temperament. Although he could be complacent at times, he would show his true colors when faced with a setback.
"Coo coo coo …" Diagonally across from Luo Nian, a white bird was chirping and flapping its wings. It had been flapping for more than an hour.
"Stop it, you're so annoying!"
"Clang!"
Luo Nian roared and punched the bars.
The white bird sensed Luo Nian's anger and paused for a few breaths. Its eyes suddenly glowed dark green.
When Luo Nian looked into its eyes, he felt as if his head had been hammered. The pain was so great that he almost fainted.
"Coo coo coo coo …" After a warning, the white bird began flapping its wings again. Its chirping was even louder than before. Luo Nian hugged his head for a while before recovering. He said helplessly, "Although these guys have gained intelligence, they're still stupid …" The questions on the mirror were very simple. They must have taken into account the cultural barriers of different races. It was just a simple graphical test. How smart could these guys be?
Just as he was feeling depressed, Luo Nian suddenly saw a cage across from him. Inside the cage was the little monster he had seen before.
The little monster didn't make a fuss. Instead, it stared at him with its black eyes and a strange smile on its face.
"This is also a strange guy. Why does it keep staring at me?"
Luo Nian complained. He simply turned over and closed his eyes with his hands covering his head.
Although he didn't need to sleep at this level of cultivation, out of sight, out of mind, he could only isolate himself from the outside world.
A night passed in this state of boredom. The noise of the monsters also continued throughout the night.
When the door to the underground space opened again, a rustling sound came from the door.
Luo Nian got up and looked over from the corner of his eye. He saw a dense swarm of "spiders" pouring in.
When one of the "spiders" came in front of Luo Nian, he saw a pile of broken wooden planks on the back of the "spider". Next to the pile of planks were three square wooden frames of different sizes.
"This is a tangram?"
Luo Nian had played with this kind of gadget in the Cloud Hall before. Ning Yudie had hired a professional to make it. Every piece of wood could emit light.
After Luo Nian glanced at it, he reached out and picked out a few wooden planks and pieced them together. In the blink of an eye, he had filled the first square wooden frame with seven wooden planks.
Childish! Next, he used twelve wooden planks to fill the slightly larger square wooden frame, and then sixteen wooden planks to fill the largest square wooden frame.
"This test is a little more difficult. About the intelligence of an eight-year-old child should be able to do it." After Luo Nian finished, his gaze fell on the large white bird opposite him.
There was no doubt about the strength of the large white bird. Just a glare from it was enough to give Luo Nian a headache. At this time, the white bird stretched its head out of the fence and pecked the wooden planks into the square wooden frame like a chicken pecking rice. Its movements were fast, but the wooden planks were not pieced together properly.
"Da da da, da da da …" The wooden planks were either missing or missing. Not to mention twelve or fourteen, it couldn't even piece together the simplest tangram.
The white bird seemed to know the consequences of not piecing the wooden planks together. Its pecking movements became faster and faster, and its eyes revealed its anxiety. In the end, the more it pieced together, the more incomplete it became. The wooden planks flew everywhere, and a few of them even flew out. It was destined to fail.
"Gu gu gu gu gu …" The white bird stretched out its neck and cried anxiously to the sky.
Luo Nian sneered. "Serves you right. Let's see if you dare to glare at me again!"
This time, the "spiders" gave them more than enough time. After about an hour, the "spiders" left one by one with the unpieced wooden planks.
The originally noisy underground space suddenly became quiet. Most of the monsters knew their fate.
"Bo …" A muffled sound suddenly came from one of the cages. Then, a golden light flashed, and the monsters in the cages were burned to ashes.
"Bo bo bo …" The golden light lit up one after another, and the sound was endless. The monsters were burned to death in the cages one by one.
The white bird clearly knew what it was facing. It struggled madly in the cage, wanting to break out.
Its wings turned into a special form of energy, and it kept slashing at the fence. However, the fence was too strong. Even with the white bird's strength, it was unable to break the fence. It couldn't even leave a scratch on it.
"Bo!"
Following the golden light in the cage, the white bird was burned to ashes. Only a few white feathers floated out of the cage.
Luo Nian saw the white bird die tragically, but he didn't feel happy. Instead, he felt terrified.
Although he had completed the puzzle, he was still very nervous. If there was a mistake in the other party's record, or if his puzzle wasn't complete enough, dying here would be a waste.
When the bursts of golden light disappeared completely, Luo Nian saw that he was safe and sound, and he breathed a sigh of relief.
"Pass …" he said in a hoarse voice.
Just as he finished speaking, Luo Nian's gaze fell on the cage diagonally opposite him.
The little monster's tail was wagging nonstop, and it was still looking at him with a playful gaze.
Luo Nian couldn't be bothered with the little monster. His gaze swept across the cages in front of him, and he counted silently. Basically, one out of ten of the cages would survive. The elimination rate was 90%. The remaining monsters seemed to be relatively smart.
This kind of intelligence test would probably continue. He just didn't know how far it would end.
But what was the point of this test?
If it was a selection of intelligent creatures, they could have just sent someone over … What made Luo Nian the most uncertain was that the first and second rounds of tests were still relatively simple. He could easily deal with them. If the difficulty increased bit by bit, it would be hard to say.
However, he had no choice now. He could only grit his teeth and go through the tests one by one.
Twenty-four hours later, the "spiders" appeared in the underground space again. This time, they presented six unique iron blocks of different sizes.
After seeing these iron blocks, Luo Nian's expression relaxed, and a smile appeared at the corner of his mouth.
"Eight Trigrams Lock …" These iron blocks were locked together in a mortise and tenon structure. Even an adult would have to spend a lot of effort to open them.
Luo Nian had liked to open Eight Trigrams Locks since he was young. He had already memorized the method to open them.
Just as he was about to do it, the little monster suddenly grabbed one of the Eight Trigrams Locks and waved it at Luo Nian. With a gentle rub of its hands, it opened it. Its eyes revealed a provocative expression.
Luo Nian was speechless at this provocation.
The creatures in the fifth grid world all had immeasurable strength. At least, they were much stronger than Luo Nian. Any one of them could be easily killed. But now, they had to compete with these children's toys.

Chapter 3821
The little monster's movements were very smooth. It must have dissected the Eight Trigrams Lock many times.
It could quickly find the trick to the Eight Trigrams Lock. In less than half an hour, it had dissected them one by one. For the rest of the time, it was still staring at Luo Nian, as if it wanted to see what he was capable of.
Because of his previous two experiences, Luo Nian was now able to maintain his composure and was not as anxious as before.
He even observed the other monsters for a while before reaching for the Eight Trigrams Lock. He grabbed the side of the Eight Trigrams Lock with both hands and gently twisted it, easily dissecting it.
The second, the third, the fourth … After dissecting the six Eight Trigrams Locks, Luo Nian smiled at the little monster opposite him. He then used a few of them to piece together the lock.
The two largest Eight Trigrams Locks were joined with the two smallest Eight Trigrams Locks, and then the medium-sized Eight Trigrams Locks. Finally, a strange shape was formed.
These Eight Trigrams Locks were compatible with each other and had endless transformations. As Luo Nian's nimble hands worked, these Eight Trigrams Locks seemed to come alive, constantly changing their shapes.
When Luo Nian had combined all the Eight Trigrams Locks into a large structure, he placed it down and gently pressed on it. The structure collapsed with a bang and returned to its original state of small pieces.
The little monster watched Luo Nian's series of dazzling movements with a look of surprise in its eyes. This thing could be played with like this?
Luo Nian spread his hands at the little monster and leaned on the side of the cage.
When the time was up, the underground space began to clear out the losers again.
Pop pop pop … A series of sounds rang out, indicating that another batch of monsters had been cleared out. There were probably less than a hundred monsters left in the cage.
On the third day, a new round of tests continued. This time, it was a set of exquisite Hundred Chains.
Luo Nian had never seen a Hundred Chains before.
However, he was very smart, so he easily dissected them after studying them in his hands.
In contrast, the little monster opposite him was scratching its head and ears, as if it was difficult for it to solve this thing.
"Can't laugh anymore …" Luo Nian shrugged.
However, the little monster was not stupid. Although the Hundred-Chain Chain was complicated, it tried again and again. It spent more than an hour before it found the technique to solve the Hundred-Chain Chain Chain. Then, it opened the chains one by one.
When the last chain was dissected, the little monster sighed in relief. At the same time, it looked at Luo Nian deeply. It now regarded Luo Nian as a worthy opponent.
Pop pop pop pop pop … Time was up.
Another sound came from the underground space.
Originally, there were only a few monsters left. Now, the voices were sparse.
Then three "spiders" appeared at the entrance of the underground space. One of the "spiders" opened the cage, took Luo Nian out, and locked him on its back.
The little monster received the same treatment.
In addition to the two of them, there was also a black ape about ten feet tall in the underground space. It was also chained to the back of the "spider".
"Three spiders? Are we the only ones left?"
Sitting on Spider's back, Luo Nian realized that the underground space that used to be full was now very empty. Except for the three of them, everyone else was dead! Such an elimination rate was too terrifying! "Rustle, rustle, rustle …" The three spiders carried them deeper into the forest.
At the end of the underground space was a flight of stairs with no end in sight. The three spiders were climbing the stairs at a leisurely pace.
The black ape curled up in the cage and didn't make a sound. The little monster looked around and then locked its gaze on Luo Nian. It was very interested in Luo Nian.
Luo Nian was used to the little monster's strange behavior. He pretended not to see it and simply closed his eyes to meditate.
But at this moment, the little monster suddenly asked, "What … are you called?"
Hearing the little monster's words, Luo Nian opened his eyes. His pupils were filled with disbelief.
The little monster was speaking Sanskrit! The Sanskrit of the Four Spirit Gates! If it were Luo Zheng, Jiang Ziya and the others naturally wouldn't understand. Of course, Luo Zheng could also decipher it with the help of the Tianzhi Civilization.
Luo Nian didn't need to decipher it. He could read and hear Sanskrit … but Sanskrit was the language of the Four Spirit Gates. Why did the little monster in the deep space also understand it?
Could it be that the creatures in the deep space also used Sanskrit to communicate?
Impossible! Luo Nian had already fully understood.
Sanskrit was a culture created by the Four Spirit Gates. It wasn't passed down from the deep space.
"I …" Luo Nian pinched his throat and made a series of sounds.
The pronunciation of Sanskrit was very complicated. For example, in order to speak Sanskrit, Luo Zheng directly asked a group of people to "sing".
Although Luo Nian knew how to read Sanskrit, it was too difficult for him to pronounce it alone. It took him a long time to express what he wanted to say. "You … can speak Sanskrit?"
The little monster nodded and replied in the same half-baked Sanskrit, "You … can also …" "Isn't this nonsense?"
Luo Nian complained in his heart and asked again, "Who … taught you?"
The little monster pointed at Luo Nian and then made a gesture, "Human … spirit."
"Human spirit … surnamed Xue?"
Luo Nian's eyes suddenly widened.
In the four-hour communication between Ku Shu and Luo Nian, there were two things that worried Ku Shu the most. First, whether Elder Xue was really alive. Second, even if Elder Xue was alive, whether Luo Nian could find him.
From the moment the little monster spoke Sanskrit, hope had been ignited in Luo Nian's heart. The little monster's Sanskrit was most likely taught by Elder Xue. If so, he was too lucky to receive news of Elder Xue soon after entering the deep space! The little monster tried hard to understand Luo Nian's words and nodded after a long time. "Yes …" Elder Xue was still alive! Luo Nian was very excited and hurriedly asked, "Where … is he?
Can … can you take me there? "
"He is outside." The little monster pointed in a direction. "We can't get out."
"Can't get out?"
Luo Nian was slightly shocked again.
"Yes, we can never … get out." The little monster nodded.
This news was a blow to Luo Nian's head.
He had thought that he could finally get freedom after going through rounds of tests.
But from what the little monster said, he would be trapped here forever?
"Why?"
Luo Nian frowned, and his face was a little ugly.
"Only the wisest person here can … reverse. Are you … the wisest person?"
The little monster recited melodiously.
Only the wisest person can reverse?
Luo Nian chewed on this sentence.
Along the way, it was a test of wisdom. It was indeed like a selection of the wisest existence.
But the so-called "wisdom" actually had a limit. No matter how smart the brain was, it could not withstand the strongest power.
Luo Nian felt that he was smarter than the little monster and the black ape, but in terms of strength, he could not beat the other party.
What happened in Skywheel City? Why did they have to select the wisest person?

Chapter 3822
Luo Nian and the little monster had a rough understanding of the situation in the deep space after their brief exchange.
Today's deep space was no longer as glorious as it used to be. Although these newly born creatures were from the fifth grid, their strength could not be compared to the Dan and Yang tribes of the past.
Moreover, most of the creatures maintained a very primitive state, which Luo Nian had noticed in the rounds of tests.
But as long as civilization was given time, it could always be rebuilt from the ruins.
In the endless years, some races slowly gained wisdom, established tribes, and began to expand. The little monster came from one of the tribes called "Xiong."
The Hun Tribe was not considered a powerful tribe among the many tribes. In fact, they could even be considered weak. Over the long years, they had been on the brink of extinction twice. There were countless small tribes like the Hun Tribe. In the long river of history, these small and weak tribes were like tiny waves that could be wiped out if one did not pay attention.
When the Xiong tribe was at their most dangerous, they met a human spirit, the Elder Xue that Luo Nian spoke of.
Although Elder Xue was an elder of the Great Conjecture Institute and his strength was at the top level in the Xuanliang world, he was not enough in the deep space. After all, the level of power in the deep space was different.
But Elder Xue had extraordinary wisdom, which was what the Xiong tribe lacked.
He won the trust of the Xiong tribe and joined them as a member. He temporarily led the Xiong tribe to escape from their original homeland and slowly recuperated. At the same time, he taught them how to enter Skywheel City … For ten years, a hundred years, and a thousand years, he did so.
Today, the Xiong tribe was a race that could not be underestimated in the deep space.
This was why the little monster was interested in Luo Nian from the beginning. In appearance, he looked exactly the same as Elder Xue.
"Since … your Xiong tribe is so powerful, why were you captured?"
Luo Nian asked again.
"Captured?"
The little monster thought for a moment. "I … came in on purpose."
Luo Nian was inadvertently captured here and thought that other intelligent creatures were the same, but the actual situation was very different.
The little monster pointed to the black ape not far away and said, "It, and I, came in on purpose …" "Why?"
Luo Nian asked.
"Skywheel City … has the most powerful power. Whoever controls it can dominate … the deep space," the little monster replied.
Currently, there were six powerful races like the Hun Clan in the deep sky. These six races were constantly killing each other, but they couldn't determine a winner. Therefore, they turned their attention to Skywheel City.
This was a legacy left behind by the most powerful 'Yang Clan' in the past. They believed that it contained the power to rebuild the deep space.
But since the destruction of the Yang Clan, the relatively intact Skywheel City has opened a unique mode. This city will kill any trespassers. The only legal way to enter Skywheel City is to be caught by the "Spider".
The creatures caught in Skywheel City would undergo rounds of wisdom tests step by step.
"There's no end to these tests of wisdom?"
Luo Nian's voice was trembling, and his Sanskrit was even less accurate.
"No one has found the end point yet …" the little monster replied.
"Then … most of them are dead," the little monster said.
Its pair of black eyes revealed a determined gaze.
"Most of them will die?
Only a small portion of them could survive?
Why? "
Luo Nian asked again.
According to Luo Nian's understanding, Skywheel City should be a screening method. Those who couldn't pass the wisdom test were bound to die. Those who passed the wisdom test could live. So far, no one had passed the final test, so the creatures who entered Skywheel City were bound to die.
The little monster shook its head. "You'll know soon …" Luo Nian nodded and immediately fell into silence.
Soon after entering the deep space, he could learn so much information from a different race creature. He even inquired about the whereabouts of Elder Xue. This was already the blessing of the God of Luck.
The only flaw was that he fell directly into a desperate situation! "I want to live!"
"I want to get out of Skywheel City alive!"
"Or return to the chaotic world …" He secretly said in his heart, his mood fluctuating.
"Rustle …" The three "Spiders" climbed the stairs patiently. Luo Nian had already found that these "Spiders" were not like living creatures, but like puppets. They were simply giving orders on their own.
After climbing this endless staircase for more than two hours, a wide exit finally appeared at the top.
The exit was a huge square with a circular depression. In the center of the square was a thick chimney building.
"Swish!"
The teeth on the backs of the three "Spiders" retracted. Luo Nian, the little monster, and the black ape were all free.
"Hoo!"
Luo Nian turned over and landed firmly on the ground. The little monster and the black ape followed closely behind.
After landing, Luo Nian found that there was a huge square frame on the ground. This square frame was outlined by red lines as thick as an arm.
Just as Luo Nian was about to take a step forward, the black ape beside him cracked a strange smile. Meanwhile, the little monster flew up and grabbed Luo Nian. "Don't … Don't go …" Seeing the little monster so anxious, Luo Nian asked curiously, "What's wrong?"
"On the ground … lines … go over, die!"
The little monster said.
At this time, Luo Nian was only one step away from the edge of the red square frame. When he heard this, he was shocked and couldn't help but take a few steps back. "So dangerous!"
"Wait, follow me," the little monster said.
After the three "Spiders" withdrew, the red square frame on the ground began to flicker. Two green lines emerged from the edge of the red square frame.
The little monster hooked its finger at Luo Zheng and walked along the green lines. Seeing this, Luo Nian followed closely behind.
As for the black ape, it stayed where it was for a while. Above the red square frame, other green lines appeared one after another. These green lines clearly led in other directions. The black ape chose one of them to enter.
The huge square was filled with these green lines. Luo Nian followed the little monster and after a few turns, they arrived at the left edge of the square.
There was an oval-shaped building here. At the entrance of this building were three Hun tribe members.
These three Hun tribe members were probably waiting for the little monster. When they saw Luo Nian behind the little monster, a strange look appeared in their eyes. One of the Hun tribe members mumbled something and asked the little monster about Luo Nian's identity.
The little monster also mumbled an explanation. The three Hun tribe members looked at Luo Nian with bewilderment. Thus, they opened the door of the oval-shaped building and welcomed Luo Nian in.
There were seven or eight other Hun tribe members in the oval-shaped building. After Luo Nian entered, the small building became extremely noisy. They all used their own language to communicate. After a while, the Hun tribe members stood on both sides, leaving only one Hun tribe member in the center.
This Hun tribe member looked at Luo Nian and spoke with a smile on his face. "I heard you were looking for me?"
He didn't use the incommunicable Hun language or the difficult Sanskrit language, but the human language.

Chapter 3823
Luo Nian had left Jixue space for a while and only used human language to communicate with Jiang Ziya.
Now that he suddenly heard a familiar voice, he didn't react immediately and asked reflexively, "What?
What did you just say? "
The Xiong person said with a smile, "I said, are you looking for me?"
"You're … you're Elder Xue?"
Luo Nian finally reacted.
The Xiong person nodded slightly, looking at Luo Nian with endless interest.
He never thought that someone from the main world would find this place … the Four Spirit Gates were the only ones who could enter deep space, but whether it was human or bird spirits, they couldn't return to the main world, let alone enter deep space.
Elder Xue was very clear that there were still humans in the chaotic world, but they couldn't even walk normally in the main world, let alone enter deep space.
So when he heard this, Elder Xue's blood boiled like boiling water, his excitement was no less than Luo Nian's.
"Elder Xue! Why did you become like this! "
Luo Nian cried out.
Elder Xue sighed and said deeply, "Everything is for survival …" As a creature of the fourth grid world, how difficult was it to survive in deep space?
To reach this step, Elder Xue had experienced many unimaginable setbacks and difficulties.
First, he had to face the obstacles between the four and five grid worlds. Everything he saw after entering deep space was a circle.
"I've been here for a long time and finally got a chance to occupy the body of a Xiong person. It's also because of this body that I have more possibilities …" Elder Xue suddenly thought of a key question and asked, "You're from the chaotic world?"
Luo Nian nodded.
"Then you've seen that person …" Among the humans in the chaotic world, the only one who knew of his existence was Taishang Laojun.
Not to mention how Luo Nian got here, he must have seen Taishang Laojun.
"I've seen Ku Shu." Luo Nian nodded.
"What!"
Elder Xue's voice trembled with excitement. "Lord Ku Shu, he's awake?"
"Yes." Luo Nian nodded.
Although Luo Nian knew Ku Shu's identity, he had only seen Ku Shu through the Nirvana Token and knew his identity. But he didn't think Ku Shu was very powerful, at least not as powerful as his father. So he didn't understand why Elder Xue was so excited.
"How did he wake up?"
Elder Xue asked.
"I think he was saved by my father." Luo Nian shrugged.
"Your father?
Who's your father? "
Elder Xue asked.
"My father's name is Luo Zheng," Luo Nian said proudly.
Elder Xue naturally wasn't interested in a human. He just waved his hand and said, "I've never heard of this person."
Now it was Luo Nian's turn to be unconvinced. "Ku Shu asked me to bring you a message. He said that my father fused with the Origin Divine Blood, but that sword was lost. Therefore, he wants you to find another object, an object that can control the power of deep space. Only my father can control it!
...
....... your father.... your father...............
.. e...
...... Luo....................................
Elder Xue stared at Luo Nian once more, lost in thought.
Naturally, he could understand the relationship between the two. After all, he was the one who taught Taishang Laojun the refining technique. After a while, he asked, "You can walk freely between the Main Realm and the deep space. Who taught you this ability?"
"I don't know. I was born with it." Luo Nian shrugged.
Luo Nian. Jiang Ziya. Luo Zheng. Luo Nian couldn't explain this. Naturally, Luo Nian thought it was his talent.
"Did your father give birth to you after practicing that body refining technique?"
Elder Xue asked again.
"Yes." Luo Nian could confirm this.
Elder Xue was relieved. He smiled and said, "Your father is very powerful, but it's all thanks to me that you can come here to see me."
"Thanks to you?
Why? "
Luo Nian said disdainfully. This Elder Xue was quite thick-skinned. He dared to claim credit even though they had never met.
Elder Xue didn't get angry. He was famous for his good temper in the Great Guessing Institute. "Because I was the one who projected your father's body refining technique from here. Your father's ability to fuse with the origin divine blood is also thanks to me. You can walk between different levels of space because of your father's special constitution. So I created all of this. Do you understand?"
This explanation stunned Luo Nian. "My father's body refining technique belongs to you …." "It doesn't belong to me, but I projected it into the chaos world and taught it to Taishang Laojun." Elder Xue corrected.
"So that's how it is!"
"What do you mean by that sword you just said was lost?"
Elder Xue asked again.
Ku Shu had told Luo Nian everything that had happened in the Chaos World. Although Luo Nian didn't quite understand, he remembered every word, and immediately told Ku Shu what he had told him.
When Elder Xue heard that the humanoid had obtained the sword, the corner of his eyes twitched.
He had stayed in the deep space for a long time. He knew all the secrets that the Four Spirits Sect couldn't decipher. He knew what this meant and what the consequences would be if the humanoid had obtained the sword.
"Ku Shu said that the most important thing was to obtain another object. Elder Xue, do you know what that other object is?"
Luo Nian asked.
Elder Xue walked a few steps, passed Luo Nian, and came to the door of the oval-shaped building. He pointed at the huge chimney in the distance and said, "The object that Lord Ku Shu asked you to find is there. It's also what I've been plotting."
"You still haven't obtained it after so long?"
Luo Nian curled his lips.
Elder Xue didn't know whether to laugh or cry. He shook his head helplessly. "Obtaining that object even has the possibility of reversing the deep space. How could it be so easy to obtain? Look over there, over there, over there …." He pointed at several oval-shaped buildings in the distance. "There are members of other deep space races in these buildings. They have come here for the same purpose as us, to obtain that object. For countless years, the six deep space races have sent countless people here. Countless people have died inside, but none of them succeeded!"
"Isn't it just a test of intelligence?
Send me in. I can succeed! "
Luo Nian said confidently.
Elder Xue glanced at Luo Nian indifferently. His heart moved slightly.
This kid was so arrogant …. He didn't realize the difficulty in front of him.
But Elder Xue suddenly realized that Luo Nian was special. He might really be able to do it! Was it the will of the heavens?
The hope that he had projected in the past had taken a different form and walked to him of its own accord?
"Maybe." Elder Xue's voice was calm and calm. "You can try. But if you fail, you will pay with your life."

Chapter 3824
Luo Nian had been very confident, but when he heard the words "the price of life," his face finally changed.
"What's the probability of death?"
Luo Nian asked.
Elder Xue tilted his head and thought for a moment. "Excluding those who sacrificed themselves before the end of the year, there were about two million people who entered the chimney after the end of the year. None of them succeeded. If you really want me to give you a probability, then the probability is zero."
"Will, will we die?"
Luo Nian asked, trembling.
"Almost. And these two million people are basically the elites selected by the six major clans. They are the smartest clansmen," Elder Xue said.
Luo Nian fell silent.
His experience was very different from Luo Zheng's. He had grown up in a good environment. Although he had experienced some small setbacks, he had never faced death directly.
When he heard the serious consequences, his heart was almost occupied by fear.
Elder Xue said this to observe Luo Nian's reaction. He continued, "Your father, Luo Zheng, lost the sword to that 'humanoid'. Do you know what we will face?"
"We will face an opponent that is difficult to defeat," Luo Nian said.
"It's not that we can't defeat him, but we will all pay with our lives. In other words, everyone will die," Elder Xue said seriously.
Luo Nian panicked even more. He asked, "What about my father?
What about my mother? "
"Everything will collapse. Everything will be degraded. Your father and mother will certainly not be able to escape," Elder Xue said again.
Luo Nian was even more panicked. "This, this … I want to go back! I want to go back to my parents! "
Since he knew there was not much time left, Luo Nian wanted to escape at once.
He had been away from them for a while. It would be a lie to say that he didn't miss them. After all, he came out with a playful attitude at the beginning. Suddenly realizing the seriousness of the matter, his mind naturally changed greatly.
In fact, when Ku Shu communicated with Luo Nian, he had made things very clear. At that time, Luo Nian also realized his importance.
However, Ku Shu didn't want to put too much pressure on Luo Nian. He just told Luo Nian that the task was very important, and didn't directly expose his life and death in front of Luo Nian.
Elder Xue revealed the result. Luo Nian couldn't bear it.
"You will die if you go back. Do you have the heart to see your father and mother die like this?"
Elder Xue asked again.
Elder Xue had lived for so many years, and he had long seen through people's hearts. Although he had only interacted with Luo Nian for a short period of time, he had already grasped Luo Nian's personality.
Arrogant, conceited, playful, irresponsible, but intelligent and possessed extraordinary talent. Elder Xue would definitely not let go of this talent, which was why he made the situation sound so serious.
"What else can we do?!"
Luo Nian lost his self-control and shouted.
Elder Xue stretched out his paw and placed it on Luo Nian's shoulder. "Of course there's another way!"
He pointed at the chimney with his other hand and said, "Your father has the Origin Divine Blood, but he lacks another item. Only if your father obtains this item can he change the situation. This is the only chance!"
"But if we fail, we'll die …" Luo Nian's voice was trembling, and he sounded like he was about to cry.
Luo Nian was like every other young man. He had a dream of saving the world, a dream of becoming a great hero. It was just that he had never figured out what it would take to realize this dream.
"Fail, die, succeed, live!"
Elder Xue said sternly.
To Elder Xue, as well as to the Shifters and Immortals who had lived in the primal chaos for countless years, they could immediately understand the cause and effect. Thus, they would sacrifice themselves without any hesitation.
Donghuang was like this, Nuwa was like this, Jiang Ziya was like this, and even Bird Lord and the other humans and spirits were like this.
But Luo Nian did not.
"I don't want to! I want to go back! Send me back, back to the primal chaos world. I want to see my parents! "
Luo Nian covered his face and said.
"You can't go back. Don't talk about going back to the main world. If you go back to the secondary world, you won't even be able to leave this plaza. Look at the line under your feet. If you take a single step, you'll definitely die!"
Elder Xue pointed at Luo Nian's feet.
The entire plaza was a forbidden area filled with killing intent. All living beings could only stay within a limited area.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Nian suddenly took two steps back and slammed into the wall. His eyes were filled with terror as he looked at the line, as though he was looking at the Grim Reaper.
"This room can only hold ten people. If there are more than eleven people, everyone will die. Thus, one person has to enter that chimney today. Are you willing to be that person?"
Elder Xue continued to press.
"I don't want to! I'm not going! "
Luo Nian shouted again.
In this place where one could die at any time, he was on the verge of breaking down.
"If you don't go, I won't go either. How about we all die together?"
Elder Xue asked again.
"I'm not going!"
Luo Nian resisted.
Seeing Luo Nian's ashen face, Elder Xue's lips curved into a smile. He ignored Luo Nian and turned to wave at the oval-shaped building.
These Hun people were all trained by Elder Xue. Like the little monster from before, they had no intention of leaving alive from the moment they entered this place. They were willing to sacrifice their lives for their own race.
One of the Hun people walked out and stood in front of the green line.
Elder Xue then said to Luo Nian, "I originally planned for ten people to stay here, but because of you, it has to die. Although it's a foreign race, it has parents and brothers. It's also infatuated with this world. However, for the sake of the entire Hun race, it has to sacrifice itself for that fleeting chance!"
Luo Nian watched helplessly as the Hun people left. They followed the two winding green lines to the center of the huge square and entered the huge chimney without hesitation.
A green light appeared on the side of the chimney. This green light slowly climbed up the chimney. One zhang, two zhang, three zhang …
When the green light reached a third of the way up the chimney, it disappeared, replaced by a red light.
Elder Xue closed his eyes and said regretfully, "It's dead."
Luo Nian stared blankly at this scene. After a long while, he said, "So fast?"
"What do you think?"
Elder Xue snorted.
The other Hun people stood up and placed their fists on their chests in silent mourning.
They had long since gotten used to this scene, but seeing one of their own die still filled their hearts with sorrow.
"Since it's so difficult to get that thing, why did we have to go?" Luo Nian was somewhat angry.
"Same reason as you. If we don't get it, the other races will get it, and the Hun race will perish." Elder Xue shrugged.
The gap between races could not be removed. The law of the jungle ruled everything here.
After Elder Xue finished speaking, he turned and entered the oval-shaped building.
From the moment he saw Luo Nian, Elder Xue knew that his chance had come.
He had waited here for so long, but the Hun race had not been able to produce an earth-shattering genius. However, the deep space had still delivered such a character to him.
He had deliberately put great pressure on Luo Nian, and at the same time, he wanted Luo Nian to be able to bear this pressure. Only under such circumstances could he maximize Luo Nian's sense of responsibility.

Chapter 3825
Luo Nian leaned against the wall and stared blankly at the distant 'chimney'.
This interesting adventure was over. Next, he would have to face the god of death.
He really wasn't prepared for this …
Ever since he was young, he had heard all sorts of legends about his father from his mother.
He had saved the Cloud Hall, saved the Central Domain, saved the Vast Thousand Worlds, saved the universe …
His father had always been the Child of Destiny. Everything he did was as easy as pushing a boat with the current, as if all these great feats were accomplished in the blink of an eye.
Although he had heard from Ning Yudie that his father had been trapped in death countless times, hovering on the brink of death, hearing about it and experiencing it for himself were two different experiences.
How could he be certain of success in a hopeless situation that no one had ever succeeded?
His father must have faced such a situation before. What was he thinking …?
He sat in boredom for several hours.
The sky gradually darkened.
The golden hoop at the top of the deep space seemed to be covered in a layer of scales, gradually turning from gold to inky black. Night fell, and the faint light shone on the chimney. From afar, it looked like a giant man-eating beast standing tall.
In the oval building, Elder Xue was communicating in the language of the Xiongnu.
The creatures of the deep space had a very unique voice when communicating. They weren't as rhythmic as the creatures of the Origin World. Instead, they emitted very soft sounds, like a group of crickets chirping softly.
Elder Xue was still teaching the Xiongnu. They needed to obtain enough knowledge to deal with the trials of the chimney.
These Xiongnu were aware of their own fate. In order to obtain that impossible probability, they could only desperately absorb any knowledge that could be used.
In the past, the Xiongnu had been very primitive. They couldn't even pass through the first checkpoints, let alone occupy a place in this huge plaza. When the 'spider' captured them and brought them underground, they were directly annihilated by the golden light.
As the night deepened, the black scales on the golden hoop grew thicker and thicker, and the surroundings became pitch-black.
Elder Xue stretched out his hand and lightly drew a line across the wall. A circular flame slowly appeared, illuminating the people inside and outside.
At this moment, Luo Nian suddenly stood up and walked in.
"Have you thought it through?"
Elder Xue asked casually.
"Why would a chimney like this appear here? What's their goal in selecting intelligent creatures?"
Luo Nian asked.
"Good question." Elder Xue had an expression of praise. "While the Yang Clan was destroyed, they had already set up this before they were destroyed. This is actually a magnificent feat."
Although the Yang Race had perished together with the Dan Race, they had left behind this precious inheritance before their extermination. Their purpose was to select an intelligent life form to restore the deep space.
This was a race of universal love. Even though they no longer existed, they still hoped to pass on the civilization of the deep space.
After hearing Elder Xue's words, Luo Nian spat out a mouthful of turbid air. "I've thought it through. I'll go! Can you tell me what to do? "
Elder Xue wasn't surprised by Luo Nian's decision because it was almost inevitable.
"Don't worry." Elder Xue patted the ground beside him and motioned for Luo Nian to sit down. Then he pointed to the "chimney" and said, "I've been confused all these years. Since the Yang family wants to select a successor, why did they set up such a difficult test that no one can pass?"
"Why?"
Luo Nian was also puzzled by this question.
"Because rebuilding the deep space isn't something that ordinary deep space creatures can do. Most of this world has collapsed, and it's filled with four-dimensional spaces." Elder Xue's eyes flashed with wisdom. "They need to select an existence that can control that thing. For example, the Hun Clan can only use the ability of 'condensing energy', which is very difficult to meet their requirements. The other deep space races are facing similar situations."
Luo Nian blinked and said, "But I don't have any ability …"
In terms of ability, Luo Nian only had the power of the Divine Path of the Chaotic World. Not to mention the fifth or fourth dimensional space, even the third dimensional space was not enough.
"But you have the ability to travel through worlds," Elder Xue said. "You can travel through worlds of different levels. This is very powerful!"
"Elder Xue, do you mean that the space in the chimney is a higher level world?"
Luo Nian's eyes flashed slightly.
"I don't know," Elder Xue replied.
"…"
Luo Nian did not know whether to laugh or cry. "Elder Xue, from what you've said, you've been in the deep space for a very long time, and you've also stayed here for a very long time. You say you don't know?"
"I've never really entered the chimney, and no creature has ever returned after entering it. I can only find information about the chimney from the information left behind by the Yang Clan. I can only be sure that it's a very special test, a test of wisdom, ability, and vision. But I don't know what it is specifically." Elder Xue shook his head.
"Then you don't plan to go in yourself?"
Luo Nian asked again.
Elder Xue played the role of a teacher in the Hun Clan, but as a teacher of the Hun Clan, it was not reasonable that he did not know what was in the 'chimney'.
"Of course I plan to," Elder Xue said frankly. "But it was a coincidence that I fused with this body of the Hun Clan back then. There was no chance for me to fuse with a second body. Once I die, there will be no more hope. So I've been hesitating, waiting …"
"I didn't expect to see you here." Elder Xue rejoiced at his own judgment. "So this time, I'll go in with you!"
"You still have a body outside. If I die, I'll really die." Luo Nian still felt that it was unfair.
Elder Xue smiled faintly. "I'll let the other body commit suicide now. How about it?"
He had long been indifferent to life and death. Now that he understood the situation of the Four Spirits Sect through Luo Nian, it was inevitable for him to put all his eggs in one basket.
When Luo Nian heard Elder Xue's understatement, he was shocked. He suddenly felt that he should not be so calculative …
"When do we go in?"
Luo Nian asked.
"Wait, wait for an opportunity," Elder Xue said.
In the next three days, Elder Xue imparted his experience to Luo Nian.
Although these experiences were only inferred by Elder Xue, they were after all tested and tested with the lives of the Hun Clan.
When he had led the Xiong tribe here, they could only climb up half a zhang in the chimney. Now, even the Xiong tribe members who performed poorly could climb up to a height of seven to eight zhang. The most outstanding Xiong tribe member could even climb up to a height of sixty zhang.
This meant that Elder Xue's experience was effective.
Luo Nian was also aware of the importance of this matter. These days, he listened to Elder Xue's teachings very seriously.
Three days later, some deep-space races appeared on the Giant Star Square. They followed the green lines in twos and threes, heading straight for the giant chimney.

Chapter 3826
When Luo Nian saw the movements of these deep-space races, he immediately asked, "Are we going as well?"
Elder Xue walked out of the oval-shaped building and looked up at the sky. "It's not time yet."
"But they're all going," Luo Nian said.
Elder Xue shook his head. "Today is Power Modeling Day. On this day, the talents of the deep-space races will increase explosively. It's a good day for them, but not for us."
A part of the deep-space races' power came from the ring-shaped sun above their heads. Today, the light released by the ring-shaped sun was indeed very dazzling. Luo Nian had more or less understood it over the past few days.
"What about them?" Luo Nian looked at the Xiongmen.
"They'll go as well," Elder Xue said.
Elder Xue then counted the four Xiongmen. These Xiongmen were already prepared and quickly gathered in front of the door.
After Elder Xue gave them a few instructions, they stepped onto the green line and headed straight for the chimney.
"Actually … actually, they don't have to go," Luo Nian said as he looked at their backs.
