"I didn't expect you to be so kind." Elder Xue smiled. "But I've taught them a new method. If they succeed, they'll be able to go even further on the chimney."
"But they'll die …"
Luo Nian muttered.
After staying here for a few days, he realized that these Xiongmen weren't as repulsive as they appeared.
They had learned the language of the four-grid world from Elder Xue and could even communicate with Luo Nian.
Luo Nian was very sad to see them throw away their lives.
Elder Xue ignored him and only stared at the chimney. He wanted to observe the movements of the other deep-space races.
Streaks of green light began to rise from the surface of the chimney.
Some streaks of green light reached thirty to forty feet before being extinguished. Some streaks of green light reached seventy to eighty feet before being extinguished. There were even some streaks of green light that reached a height of thirty feet. This was an excellent record, but it was created with their lives.
"The Hu Na Clan's performance this time isn't bad."
"The Icy Monkey Clan's performance is unexpectedly bad."
"It's our turn …"
Elder Xue recorded the results without blinking.
Every result was very important. Through thousands of trials and errors, Elder Xue had roughly figured out some tricks to the chimney.
After the four Xiongmen entered the chimney, four streaks of green light could be seen slowly climbing up.
The Hun people climbed very slowly, even stopping from time to time. However, they maintained a constant momentum.
The first green light was extinguished at a height of ten zhang. The corners of Elder Xue's eyes twitched slightly. His black claws were already clenched into fists.
The second green light was extinguished at a distance of thirteen feet. This time, Elder Xue's complexion was slightly better.
By the time the third green light reached a height of sixteen zhang, the Hun people in the oval-shaped building were already cheering.
This had already tied their previous record, and from the looks of it, they could continue to climb higher!
Elder Xue's face was also filled with excitement. His judgment was correct!
Sixteen, seventeen, eighteen zhang …
As the green light rose higher and higher, the cries of the Hun people became louder and louder.
They even began to look forward to the two Hun comrades directly ascending to the top of the chimney and obtaining the item that was used to feed their race, thus ending this nightmarish exploration.
But just as their expectations soared, the green light suddenly extinguished at a height of two hundred feet …
"It's extinguished …"
"We still have one more person!"
"It can still rise!"
The Hun people placed their hopes on the last ray of green light.
But the last ray of green light also stopped at a height of two hundred feet before extinguishing.
The Hun people all fell silent.
Even though they were already used to this process and had already expected the result, they still felt extremely disappointed.
Another failure.
Elder Xue stood in place like a statue, quietly gazing at the chimney.
After each failure, he would stand like this for a long time, pondering the reasons for his failure.
After a long time, Elder Xue suddenly turned around and announced to the remaining Hun people, "Next time, it will be my turn to go in with Luo Nian."
The four remaining Hun people instantly paled at these words.
"Elder Xue, how could you go in!"
"In our Hun tribe, you're even more important than the patriarch. You absolutely cannot go in!"
"The lives of our Hun people aren't worth anything. There are still many Hun people who can sacrifice themselves, but you only have one body. If you fail, it's all over!"
It wasn't an exaggeration to say that Elder Xue's status in the Hun tribe was much higher than the patriarch's.
Elder Xue wasn't very strong, and any Hun of similar strength could easily kill him.
But the Hun tribe had only reached this point because of Elder Xue. Even the talents of the Hun people had been unearthed by Elder Xue. One could imagine just how important he was to the Hun tribe.
"Was it that brat's request? Elder Xue, don't listen to him. I'll throw him out right now! "One of the Hun people glared viciously at Luo Nian.
Luo Nian couldn't understand the Hun language at all. Suddenly seeing them glaring at him, he was dumbfounded. Why are these Hun people suddenly glaring at me?
Elder Xue smiled faintly. "No."
"Then why! You were doing fine here, but now you're planning to go in personally as soon as this brat came. It must be because of him, "the Hun continued to shout.
"Because he brought an important message," Elder Xue explained. "If you don't get that thing quickly, the deep space will be completely degraded in a short time. Everything you have will disappear."
"What?"
"It can't be!"
"I don't believe it!"
The Hun people shook their heads furiously.
Although the deep space was currently in a shattered state, it was still stable without any further degradation.
Elder Xue was very patient. After all, educating these Hun people was no easy task. He had already tempered his temperament to the point of being unperturbed. Thus, he explained the reason for the collapse of the deep space, the current situation, and the four dimensions.
Inside the world of the three dimensions.
He roughly explained what had happened.
The Hun people were confused, but they still understood the general logic.
The Degenerate who had destroyed the deep space to such a state was now stirring up trouble in the world of the third grid. Once he succeeded, the fifth grid to the third grid would crumble, and the only way to save them was to obtain that item.
"Thus, I have no choice," Elder Xue said. "Five days from now, I will go with Luo Nian on Water Burst Day."
"What about us?" a Hun asked.
"You can come together, but it's best if one of you stays behind," Elder Xue said.
If one of them stayed behind, they could still take Elder Xue's place and continue fighting for that minuscule chance.
The Hun people no longer objected, but they continued to argue over who should stay.
They were a race that knew how to contribute. At this point, no one was willing to be the one to stay behind. After some discussion, the Hun who had come in with Luo Nian was left behind.
Five days later, Water Burst Day arrived.
The ring-shaped sun in the deep space began to emit rippling golden light, and the sunlight became peaceful and warm.

Chapter 3827
The green lines on the plaza meandered back and forth like a massive glyph array.
Luo Nian followed Elder Xue along this shoulder-width path.
Crossing this shoulder-width green line meant losing one's life, so Luo Nian naturally had to be extremely vigilant, taking each step with great care.
The chimney was located at the center of the plaza, only a few thousand zhang away in a straight line. However, their group actually covered a distance of several tens of thousands of zhang.
The other deep-space races had also sent out their clansmen, so there were ten-some people walking across the plaza. Sometimes, Luo Nian's group would get closer to the other races, and the gazes they shot at each other carried a hint of hostility.
Traces of hostility.
After all, the six races were hostile to each other, and they were also in a direct competition here. It was only because of the special nature of this place that they were not allowed to fight.
Two incense sticks later, Luo Nian's group finally arrived at the base of the chimney.
Elder Xue had been here for countless years, but this was his first time observing the chimney up close.
From a distance, the chimney looked like a black monster, but up close, it was a completely different feeling.
The chimney was constructed from a pure black metal, and one could see its shiny side in the tranquil sunlight.
Elder Xue walked up to the outer wall of the chimney and slowly stroked it.
He had spent the latter half of his life pondering the mysteries of this chimney, and at this moment, his mind was in turmoil.
At this moment, a soft chuckle came from behind Elder Xue.
Three black apes walked in from another path. The leading black ape laughed and said, "The Xiong race has always been cautious, but they've sent out five people at once. Several days ago, four of them were sacrificed, and only that Elder Xue is left to guard the base?"
"This time, there's an extra Human Spirit. Did that Elder Xue find a helper?"
"When Old Gan came last time, he saw this Human Spirit!"
These black apes were from the Icy Ape Race.
Of the six deep-space races, the Xiong race was the weakest. The Xiong race had only risen to prominence because Elder Xue had taken advantage of the other races' lack of intelligence. It was for this reason that the other five races had always looked down on them.
The Xiong race.
Several deep-space races had once sent assassins to assassinate Elder Xue, but the Xiong race had risked their lives to protect Elder Xue.
"Elder Xue is not the one staying behind. I am Elder Xue," Elder Xue replied.
The Three-headed Icy Apes were obviously stunned for a moment. The leading Icy Ape revealed a puzzled expression. "Everyone knows that you are the Hun Tribe's greatest ally. Today, you actually dared to risk your life. Have you found a way to break Levita's spell?"
Everyone knew how important Elder Xue was to the Xiong Tribe.
The Xiong Tribe would rather have their chief assassinated than protect Elder Xue's safety.
"No," Elder Xue answered honestly.
"Then why did you come?" Icy Monkey was puzzled.
Once they entered the huge plaza, there was no turning back. Elder Xue would definitely not be able to return.
Elder Xue only smiled and didn't pay much attention to the Icy Ape Clan.
At this moment, another deep-space race came in from the other side of the chimney. It was the Hu Na race.
The Icy Ape Tribe and the Hu Na Tribe were allies, and the two deep space races had always been on good terms. The Icy Ape Tribe immediately called out, "Kesh, look, Elder Xue of the Hun Tribe has personally come!"
This was indeed no small matter.
The Hun Clan had always been ahead of the other five clans because of Elder Xue.
The other five races had pressured the Hun Tribe and even offered them benefits in hopes that Elder Xue would help them. However, Elder Xue had refused.
The only time he had compromised was when the other five races joined forces to force the Hun Tribe. Elder Xue had no choice but to share part of his experience...
"Elder Xue?"
"Are you confident that you can pass Levita?"
"Entering Levita rashly is an act of suicide! Aren't you afraid of death? "
The Hu Na Tribe members were also stunned. They had the same thought as the Icy Ape Tribe. Since Elder Xue dared to come in person, he must have found a way to break the spell.
Two of the Hu Na Tribe members' eyes flashed with murderous intent. They definitely didn't want Elder Xue to climb to the top of Levita!
However, the entire plaza was protected by the Hun Tribe's powerful seal. The six major races didn't have the ability to resist this seal. They couldn't stop the Hun Tribe's decision at all.
Just as the members of the Hu Na Clan were feeling puzzled and worried, the members of the other three deep space races arrived one after another.
Every time there was a sudden flood, the six deep space races would fight to enter the chimney. However, they were not in a hurry today. They wanted to see what Elder Xue was planning.
Elder Xue ignored them and said to the Hun Tribe members, "Let's go in!"
The three Hun Tribe members took the lead and entered the chimney. Elder Xue and Luo Nian followed closely behind.
The door at the bottom of the chimney was only about the height of a person. When the five of them approached, the door automatically shrank to one side.
After Luo Nian entered the door, he looked around and saw an extremely wide metal hall.
The floor and walls of the hall were all made of black metal. The surface of the metal was engraved with golden rings. Some of these golden rings existed alone, while others were connected to each other. It looked like a mess.
They were tangled together.
"What are these?" Luo Nian pointed at the golden rings and asked.
"Ring patterns." Elder Xue looked as if he had the pearl of wisdom in his hands.
"It's exactly as I guessed. The bottom of the chimney records the ring patterns left behind by the Hun Tribe. We're not in a hurry to go up. Let me decipher these ring patterns!"
"Ring patterns" were the words of the Hun Tribe and the Dan Tribe.
The creatures of the Chaotic World had a strong interest in Sanskrit. Powerful experts like the Winged Monarch and the Primeval Lord of Heaven had spent their entire lives deciphering Sanskrit.
The Four Spirit Sects in the Main Realm worked tirelessly on the "Ring patterns". Ku Shu, Shou, and Sin all had deep attainments in the "Ring patterns".
Elder Xue's attainments in the "Ring patterns" used to be inferior to theirs. However, not only did he stay in the deep space for a long time, he also had the body of a Hun Tribe member. Now, his attainments in the "Ring patterns" far surpassed anyone in the Four Spirit Sects.
Human.
After Elder Xue finished speaking, he lay on the wall and began to decipher the "Ring patterns" word by word.
"This Levita is the foundation of the Hun Tribe. It is also... is..."
The structure of the "Ring patterns" was very simple. They were just golden rings. However, deciphering them was even more complicated. After all, the golden rings were connected and there was not much difference between them. It was even more difficult to read them.
However, deciphering them was still very difficult.
"It is also an opportunity. We were defeated by the Dan Tribe. We can only take it as the fate of Nirvana. Now, all those who enter the tower have come for this opportunity..." Luo Nian said after Elder Xue.
Elder Xue's face stiffened. He turned around and stared at Luo Nian in disbelief.

Chapter 3828
Luo Nian had no problem reading Sanskrit. It was as if he was born with the ability to decipher Sanskrit and understand the hidden messages within.
When he first saw these circles, he couldn't see the contents clearly. But after Elder Xue recited a few words according to the 'ring pattern', these patterns suddenly became amiable in Luo Nian's eyes.
It was as if he was born with this ability. He had used these words for many years and could recite them easily.
"Do you really recognize these ring patterns?"
Elder Xue asked.
Luo Nian's gaze wandered to the other ring patterns. After a few glances, he nodded. "Yes, I can understand …" "Strange, strange …" Elder Xue was speechless for a moment, but he was also very surprised. "It's a little difficult for me to decipher these ring patterns, and sometimes it's not accurate. Then I'll have to trouble you to decipher it!"
Luo Nian had done a lot of deciphering. He nodded and began to recite word by word from right to left.
"The Levita is five hundred feet tall. If you climb to the top and become the Levita Master, you will be qualified to obtain the Sky Ring Staff, the Yang Clan's sacred artifact.
"The Sky Ring Staff is the foundation of the Yang Clan. It can freely control all forms of energy." This item is important and cannot be given away by fate. It must be owned by the capable. Those who wish to obtain this staff must pass through the layers of the Levita's tests. "
Elder Xue's gaze flickered as Luo Nian recited. He knew that the Yang Clan had something comparable to that sword, but he didn't know what it was. Now, he learned from Luo Nian that it was precisely called the 'Sky Ring Staff'.
Elder Xue didn't interrupt Luo Nian and continued to listen. The three Xiong people on the side weren't in a hurry either. They obediently stood by the side.
The Xiong people and other deep space races couldn't recognize the ring patterns. Although they had no problem reading them, the Yang Clan and Dan Clan had been destroyed for many years. The six deep space races that were born later were still in a very primitive stage. They only had strength but hadn't created their own civilization. Their knowledge of the ring patterns was close to zero.
The five deep space races outside the Levita were even more confused.
Normally, after entering the Levita, they would rush up as fast as they could. No one would stay on the first floor.
When they were outside the tower, they saw the five green lights of the Hun tribe lingering on the first floor. They didn't know what they were up to.
"We, the Yang Clan, have received the inheritance of the Infinite God Realm and are the rightful masters of the Unthinking Limit Realm. Since the beginning of time, we have regarded the Dan Clan as an enemy that we must destroy. The first ten thousand Water Abrupt Suns, the Yang Clan slaughtered three thousand Dan Clan masters. The ninety thousand Blazing Suns, we broke through the Dan Clan's impregnable stronghold, the thirteenth …" The Yang Clan briefly introduced Levita before recording the history of the war between the Yang Clan and the Dan Clan.
Luo Nian's original intention was to find some clues to Levita in the first layer of the ring pattern, but the Yang Clan did not record the clues. Instead, it recorded some of the history of the Yang Clan's development. Luo Nian naturally felt rather bored. Halfway through the recitation, he asked, "Elder Xue, do you want to continue?"
"Read! Of course I have to read it! "
Elder Xue was extremely interested in the history of the Yang Clan.
As an elder of the Great Conjecture Academy, he was naturally interested in exploring the world. How could he miss such a rare opportunity?
Luo Nian rolled his eyes and had no choice but to continue reciting.
The timeline of the Yang Clan started from the ten thousand Water Abrupt Suns and continued until the three hundred millionth Water Abrupt Suns. The Yang Clan experienced prosperity, decline, rise, conquest, decline, and the process of being evenly matched with the Dan Clan. In the end, the two clans fought to the death.
After deciphering the last ring pattern, Elder Xue patted Luo Nian's shoulder with satisfaction. "Let's go up!"
The inner layer of Levita was a spiral structure. When they climbed up about ten feet along the spiral staircase, a red line suddenly appeared under their feet and blocked their path. Everyone suddenly stopped.
Without needing Elder Xue's explanation, Luo Nian knew that crossing the red line meant death.
Just as they approached the red line, they simultaneously felt a consciousness drilling into their minds.
"It's a soul test …" Elder Xue looked as if he had expected it.
In the long years, Elder Xue had experienced countless trials and errors. He had a rough estimation of the first twenty feet of Levita.
"Elder Xue, will I fail?"
Luo Nian was still a little nervous.
Although Luo Nian was already a True God, his soul was not considered strong. It was far inferior to an ordinary Nirvana Realm, let alone the Holy Soul Realm or the Immortal Realm.
Any creature in the deep space had a soul that was far stronger than Luo Nian. They only needed to release a little soul pressure and Luo Nian's soul would be extinguished like a candle in the wind.
"Don't worry, the test in Levita has nothing to do with strength. Otherwise, no one would have passed after so many years. Don't resist this consciousness," Elder Xue comforted.
It was not that the six deep space clans, including the Xiong Clan, did not have any powerhouses. However, the effect of sending powerhouses in was not good. They even performed worse than ordinary clansmen. After the six deep space clans lost some powerhouses in Levita, they stopped this practice.
After all, powerhouses were the foundation of the deep space clans. If they lost too many powerhouses, they would not be able to control the deep space. The other five clans would kill them first.
This consciousness was unobstructed in Luo Nian's mind. The moment it came into contact with Luo Nian's soul, a simple question surfaced in Luo Nian's mind.
"Who are you?"
A strange expression appeared on Luo Nian's face when he heard this question.
Elder Xue and the other three Xiong Clan members had the same expression. Elder Xue's reaction was calm. He reminded, "Relax, answer truthfully."
Answer truthfully?
Introduce himself?
"I am Luo Nian … from the Chaotic World …" No one knew what the correct answer was. Luo Nian was very straightforward at this moment and conveyed his thoughts truthfully to the consciousness.
After a round of self-introduction, a gap opened in the red line under Luo Nian's feet. Two green lines extended from the gap. This was the passage forward.
"Simple."
Luo Nian shrugged.
Elder Xue and the Xiong Clan members also finished introducing themselves and completing the answers given by the consciousness. A passage outlined by green lines also appeared under their feet.
Then the group continued to follow the green lines.
After they had walked for some distance, the wisp of consciousness raised a new question.
"What is your dream?"
"Dream?"
Luo Nian tilted his head and hesitated for a moment.
According to the logic of accepting the test of raising a clan, it should be something like saving the world.
But should this test cater to their tastes?
While Luo Nian was hesitating, Elder Xue reminded him again, "Answer truthfully!"

Chapter 3829
Dream …
Luo Nian blinked a few times and began to seriously consider this question.
He now knew that the entire world was in danger. What he wanted to do the most now was to save the world and see his parents reunite.
But was this his dream?
After thinking about it, Luo Nian quickly denied it. This was not a dream.
For as long as Luo Nian could remember, he was a child under control.
On one hand, his mother was the Hallmaster of the Cloud Hall. On the other hand, his father was a legend in various worlds.
His environment was generally stable, but this stable environment was like a prison. He could never be controlled and could not leave at will.
It was like this in the Cloud Hall, and it was the same in Immortal Mansion.
Ever since Luo Zheng went to the mother world, he had always stayed in Luo Zheng's inner world. He was used as a tool by his father. Only when it was time to decipher Sanskrit would he appear.
Although Luo Zheng had given him a certain amount of freedom in his inner world, and even occasionally created some difficulties and dangerous situations for him with his omnipotent ability, he was well aware that everything in his inner world was completely under his father's control. Dangers and whatnot did not exist …
It was also this experience that caused Luo Nian to have an indifferent attitude, because it was meaningless to care.
Carefully thinking back on the years he had been alive, the happiest time seemed to be after entering the Jixu space and the Xuanliang world.
It was very dangerous here …
And his parents had no way to control it. His life was in danger at any time!
But Luo Nian was indeed very happy!
Understanding this, Luo Nian immediately replied, "My dream is to be able to freely roam the world!"
After Luo Nian's answer, the consciousness still did not respond. He and Elder Xue continued to advance along the green line. There was no obstruction in front of them.
"Elder Xue … it seems too simple." Luo Nian shrugged.
"It's not that simple." Elder Xue shook his head. "They're just collecting information."
What was the use of collecting information …
While Luo Nian was puzzled, the green line under his feet came to an end. There was another red line on the ground in front of them.
At that moment, the consciousness suddenly became active in his mind. This time, it did not ask any questions. Instead, it directly drilled into Luo Nian's soul.
"Boom!"
Luo Nian felt as if he had been hit by an ancient beast. A white light flashed in his mind, and he appeared in a strange world.
Elder Xue disappeared, and so did Levita. In front of Luo Nian's eyes was a vast wasteland, with no end in sight. But not far away, Luo Nian saw a few shabby wooden houses.
Luo Nian walked towards the small wooden huts and immediately heard the sound of crying. Luo Nian searched for the source of the sound and found a skinny little girl with pale skin in one of the wooden huts.
"Hey."
Luo Nian shouted from afar.
He knew that the environment he was in was very strange, so he did not dare to approach the little girl.
"Hey, why are you crying!"
Luo Nian asked again.
The little girl slowly stopped crying and complained to Luo Nian, "It's all your fault. We're all going to die!"
Luo Nian was baffled. "Blame me for what?"
This should be an illusion created by Levita. The illusion itself was a test.
Seeing Luo Nian's expression, the little girl's resentment grew. "It's because of you that I was created. That's why I'm so miserable. There will be ghost crows coming soon. We can't run away, so we'll die together!"
Hearing the little girl's words, Luo Nian immediately reacted. This was a hint!
He looked up at the end of the wasteland. A black cloud had risen, and after careful observation, it was really a black crow.
"Since there are ghost crows, why don't you run!"
Luo Nian urged.
"I won't! We can't run away anyway! "
The little girl shook her head stubbornly.
"Then I'll run!"
Luo Nian turned and ran without hesitation. At the same time, he muttered in his heart, "What the hell is this illusion …"
After running for a dozen steps, he suddenly stopped. Seeing the little girl still sitting there crying, he frowned and helplessly turned back.
Since the little girl appeared in the illusion, it must be useful.
He squatted in front of the little girl and said, "You haven't even tried, and you're so sure we can't run away?"
"This wasteland is very big, and the ghost crows are very fast. Of course we can't run away!"
The little girl said.
"Are you familiar with this world?"
Luo Nian asked.
The little girl shook her head. "Anyway, we can't run away. We can only wait for death!"
"If we run, we die. If we don't run, we die. We have to try. If we give up without doing anything, we'll die with regret," Luo Nian said.
"Look at those ghost crows. Just one of them can swallow you and me. The nearest city is a hundred miles away. We'll definitely die. How can we have any regrets?"
The little girl said solemnly.
"Since you know that this world is imaginary, then you should know that this test is not unsolvable. As long as you run with me, there will be a way," Luo Nian said patiently. He had already figured out that the key to passing this illusion was this little girl.
"My existence is meaningless. Regardless of whether I run or not, I will disappear along with this world. Running and dying are meaningless to me," the little girl retorted.
"But it's meaningful to me." Luo Nian was a little annoyed. This little girl who had appeared out of nowhere was simply stubborn.
"I don't have to help you. I can't help you." She stopped crying, and there was even a hint of malice in her cute big eyes.
"Caw caw caw …"
The ghost crows on the horizon were getting closer and closer. Luo Nian could feel their brutal aura.
He had only climbed two or three feet on the Levita. Was he going to fail so soon?
He frowned and suddenly realized that something was wrong.
He had been focusing on persuading the little girl, but did he have to persuade her mother?
"I can't accept this …"
Luo Nian said in his heart. He glanced down at the little girl and raised his eyebrows slightly. Suddenly, he reached out and grabbed the little girl's arm and pulled her up.
"Whoosh …"
The little girl's body was weightless, as light as a paper kite. Luo Nian dragged her all the way forward and said, "Since you can't help me, I'll help you!"
The little girl was dragged all the way, with a light smile on her face. She waved her hand lightly towards the sky, and a fan-shaped flame rolled up in the sky.
In the blink of an eye, the ghost crows were burned to ashes.
The wilderness suddenly became quiet. Although it was an illusion, it was extremely realistic. Luo Nian could even smell the smell of barbecued meat.
"You've passed," the little girl replied.
Luo Nian threw the little girl aside and sighed. He was about to say something when the illusion disappeared.
He reappeared on the Levita. The red line in front of him disappeared, and there were two smooth green lines in front of him.
Elder Xue and the other three Xiongmen still had their eyes closed. They were obviously still trapped in the illusion.

Chapter 3830
Luo Nian was not in a hurry to go up. He stood to the side and waited.
Half an hour later, one of the Xiongmen opened his eyes. When he found that he was safe, he was excited.
He noticed that Luo Nian had already left the illusion. He mumbled a series of words, but Luo Nian did not understand a single word.
Soon, the second Xiongman also woke up. Only Elder Xue and the last Xiongman were left.
"They should be waking up soon," Luo Nian said confidently.
After all, he had seen the Xiongmen enter the Levita. The first half of them ascended very quickly. It should not take too long.
But as soon as Luo Nian finished speaking, he saw the two lines under the feet of the unconscious Xiongman begin to close.
When the two Xiongmen saw this, they cried out anxiously, but there was nothing they could do.
Everyone had their own path. The lines under their feet were like pools of lightning. They did not dare to cross them.
When the lines closed under the Xiongman's feet, the originally green lines turned red. Then, the Xiongman's legs were burned by an invisible flame. From bottom to top, he turned into a human-shaped charcoal. The charcoal gradually turned white.
White ash …
"Dead!" Luo Nian's eyes twitched.
Even though he was well aware that those who entered the Levita Tower had no chance of survival, including himself, but seeing this Xiong man die in front of him was still very unbearable!
At the same time, he was worried about Elder Xue's safety. He had not woken up for so long. Would Elder Xue also meet his end here?
Just as Luo Nian was worrying, Elder Xue's body shook slightly and he opened his eyes. Luo Nian breathed a sigh of relief.
When the two Xiongmen saw that Elder Xue had woken up, they pointed at the drifting white ash and mumbled. Elder Xue naturally understood what they meant. After a few words of consolation, he said to Luo Nian, "It must have lied when it asked the question before.
It will give you an impossible test. I have emphasized many times that no matter what the Levita asks, you must answer truthfully. You must remember! "
Elder Xue put all his thoughts on Luo Nian. He used the Xiongman's death as a negative example to educate Luo Nian.
"Yes, I will remember," Luo Nian nodded. When it came to life or death, he would follow orders.
Then, the remaining four people continued to walk up the green line.
"Those who enter the Levita Tower will stop at the height of twenty feet, and then go straight up to the height of fifty feet. I don't think there will be any tests at this distance," Elder Xue said as he walked.
Elder Xue had observed this phenomenon many times, and this visit was a general verification.
Just as he said, the four of them circled around Levita twice and climbed to a height of fifty feet without any obstacles. The consciousness in their minds was silent the whole time, but when they reached a height of fifty feet, a red line appeared in front of them again, blocking their way.
At the same time, the consciousness in his mind asked another question.
"Are you afraid of death?"
When Luo Nian heard this question, his heart trembled slightly. A bad premonition surged in his heart, but he still answered with certainty, "Yes!"
Boom!
The moment he answered the question, that wisp of consciousness once again crashed into Luo Nian's soul.
In the blink of an eye, he entered a new illusory realm.
In front of him was a two-foot-long Buddhist Commandment Blade. Not far away was a large lake. Beside the lake, Luo Nian saw the pale-faced little girl.
"We meet again," the little girl smiled sweetly.
Luo Nian ignored her and looked around vigilantly.
Since the problem this time was death, then the test he went through must be related to death …
The biggest difficulty of this illusory realm was that there were too few hints and almost no clues. If he wanted to pass the test, he had to rely on himself.
After walking a few steps, Luo Nian picked up the Buddhist Commandment Blade and held it in his hand. He asked, "Why did you appear again? Are you here to help me?"
"I won't help you," the little girl smiled maturely. "I want to watch you die. Those who are afraid of death will definitely die."
"Watch me die. Those who are afraid of death will definitely die …" Luo Nian pondered.
There was only this lake in front of him. Was he going to drown himself?
With Luo Nian's cultivation, drowning was impossible, but this was an illusory realm. Any way to die was possible.
When he realized this, Luo Nian noticed something moving quickly at the bottom of the lake. His eyes widened, and he immediately retreated.
"Phew …"
When Luo Nian retreated dozens of feet, a vine drilled out from the bottom of the lake and shot straight at him like an arrow.
The vine was so fast that it wrapped around Luo Nian's leg in the blink of an eye.
"Clang, clang, clang, clang …"
Luo Nian used the Buddhist Commandment Blade in his hand to slash at the vines. The vines were much harder than he had imagined. Although sparks flew, the surface of the vines was not damaged. Then the vines released an irresistible force and dragged him into the water.
Under the water.
"Gulp, gulp …"
As Luo Nian had expected, after entering the water, a strong sense of suffocation hit him. He could drown!
Although Luo Nian struggled wildly, the vine was as hard as an iron hoop and he could not break free.
The little girl standing by the lake bent over and looked at Luo Nian struggling at the bottom of the water. The smile on her face was innocent and terrible.
As the sense of suffocation grew stronger, Luo Nian's consciousness began to fade.
If he died, he would really die …
Thinking of the Xiong man who had been burned to ashes, he was afraid that everything would be over if he was like him.
"I … want to live!"
He clenched his teeth, holding on to the last trace of his obsession to survive.
The little girl couldn't help, and the vine couldn't be cut …
Then the Buddhist Commandment Blade …
Cut himself!
Luo Nian suddenly reacted and waved the Buddhist Commandment Blade at his leg.
He had never suffered much since he was a child, but at this moment, he was particularly ruthless.
"Crack!"
A handful of blood blossomed at the bottom of the lake. His right leg was cut off by a blade. He quickly drilled out of the water and climbed ashore just before he fainted, gasping for breath. Just as Luo Nian thought he had escaped disaster, another vine rolled up from the bottom of the lake.
This time, it was his left leg.
"Crack!"
Luo Nian became more decisive this time. Before he was pulled to the bottom of the lake, he cut off his left leg with the Buddhist Commandment Blade.
The illusion here was very detailed. It was simply a real world. The pain of the broken leg was also very clear.
"How much more do you want to cut? Just say it! As long as I can live! "Luo Nian roared fiercely at the little girl.
Being roared at by Luo Nian, the little girl seemed a little aggrieved. She lowered her head and thought for a moment, then showed a look of disinterest. "Boring. You're really afraid of death. Then let's end it …"
As soon as her voice fell, the entire illusion shook with the little girl and disappeared.
Luo Nian, who was in the Levita, was soaked in sweat.
Having successfully passed the test, Luo Nian felt like he had survived a disaster.
He looked at Elder Xue and the two Xiong men and was stunned. He didn't know what kind of torture they were going through. They were trembling all over.
"It was so dangerous even if I said I was afraid of death. Elder Xue and the others' Taoist hearts are stable. They must have said they weren't afraid of death. The illusion they faced was probably ten times more difficult …" Luo Nian said and shook his head.

Chapter 3831
The illusion that Elder Xue and the others faced was indeed much more difficult than Luo Nian's.
But in terms of life and death, both Elder Xue and the Hun tribe had more experience than Luo Nian.
It wasn't an exaggeration to say that those who were qualified to enter the Levita were basically indifferent to life and death, and the test of life and death was much easier for them.
Luo Nian didn't have to wait long before Elder Xue and the other two Hun tribe members safely left the illusion.
The four of them passed this time, and Elder Xue was still the first to confirm Luo Nian's safety. Seeing that Luo Nian was safe, he breathed a sigh of relief.
"That question, how do you answer?"
Elder Xue asked.
"Of course … I'm afraid of death," Luo Nian said.
"Afraid of death …" Elder Xue was slightly taken aback, but immediately understood.
Those who entered the Levita basically had the awareness to see through life and death, and Elder Xue and the other two Hun tribe members answered that they weren't afraid of death, and the test of life and death they experienced was much more severe.
Especially Elder Xue, who had just reincarnated between life and death hundreds of times in a short period of time. If it were Luo Nian, he would have already broken down.
Being weak had its advantages, and the test of the Levita was much easier. If they kept going at this pace, it really wouldn't be difficult to pass the Levita.
"Let's continue!"
When the red line disappeared, the four of them continued to circle the Levita's steps.
Sixty feet … Seventy feet … Eighty feet … At ninety feet, the consciousness asked another question.
"If faced with a dilemma, who would you save?"
Before Luo Nian could answer, he was led into the illusion, and the little girl appeared again. At the same time, she gave Luo Nian a chance to choose between his own life and the lives of ten thousand people. Either he sacrificed his own life, and the ten thousand people would live, or the ten thousand people would sacrifice themselves in exchange for his own life.
This time, Luo Nian's reaction was very fast, and he almost didn't think before choosing his own life. In Luo Nian's eyes, his own life was much more important than those irrelevant strangers! Elder Xue had previously emphasized that he would tell the truth, so he was facing the Levita with the most frank attitude, and he was very confident in his choice.
Soon, the innocent people were slowly drowned in the lava, and the little girl standing beside Luo Nian commented that he was "cold-blooded" before the illusion disappeared.
Elder Xue and the two Hun tribesmen also successfully passed.
After passing three soul tests in a row, Luo Nian's expression became more and more relaxed. "Elder Xue, these aren't soul tests. They don't involve the strength or depth of the soul. At most, they are tests of human nature …" "I've noticed that as well." Elder Xue nodded.
"It shouldn't be hard to pass this test," Luo Nian said.
It was hard for him to imagine that none of the six deep space races had passed the Levita test after so many years.
Elder Xue smiled. "Don't forget, no matter if it's the Hun Tribe, the Icy Ape Tribe, or the Hu Na Tribe, although they have strength, their civilization is still in a very barbaric stage. Humanity is what they lack the most."
In fact, after so many years, Elder Xue had figured out that he couldn't lie to Levita. However, he had never entered Levita before. No matter how smart he was, it was impossible for him to guess the content of Levita's test out of thin air.
Next, they were tested three times at 120 feet, 150 feet, and 180 feet respectively, falling into three illusions.
These illusions also repeated the test of human nature. As long as one's heart was strong, it wasn't difficult for Luo Nian and Elder Xue. However, one of the Hun tribesmen unexpectedly fell at the 180 feet mark.
The remaining three crossed the 180 feet mark and went straight for the 200 feet mark. 200 feet was already the limit. Only a few days ago, two Hun tribesmen had come here, but they also fell here.
Elder Xue and Luo Nian also wanted to know what was at the 200 feet mark.
When they climbed up the spiraling staircase, they found a transparent object about half a foot wide blocking their way at the 200 feet mark.
There seemed to be a transparent glass door blocking the stairs, and inside the transparent object, there were dozens of gray things floating.
Luo Nian walked forward and reached out to touch the transparent object. The moment his fingertips touched the object, ripples appeared on its surface. This large transparent glass-like object was actually in the form of water! Luo Nian's touch seemed to have activated the gray objects. One of the gray objects rapidly approached the 'water' in Luo Nian's direction, suddenly transforming into a large gray mouth that bit at Luo Nian.
However, this large gray mouth couldn't leave the 'water' and could only open up inside the body of water. After frightening Luo Nian into sitting on the ground, it quickly transformed back into the gray object and shrank back.
"What the hell is this …" Luo Nian's face turned green.
Elder Xue's brows immediately furrowed. This transparent object was blocking the only path. If they wanted to continue climbing, they could only swim through this layer of 'water' … The two Hun tribesmen from before should have died in this' water '. Looking at the situation, wouldn't they be doomed as well?
"Gurawalili …" The remaining Hun tribesman saw Elder Xue's frown in contemplation. After muttering a few words, it actually went straight for the 'water'. This Hun tribesman also knew that it was doomed, but all the Hun tribesmen were full of respect for Elder Xue. It was willing to sacrifice itself to help Elder Xue explore the path.
"Don't!"
Elder Xue wanted to stop it, but it was too late. As soon as the Hun tribesman dove into the water, the gray object immediately locked onto it.
"Whoosh!"
The gray object transformed into a large mouth and bit down on the Hun tribesman's head. Then, the object transformed into a gray fog and began to burrow into the Hun tribesman's mind. At first, the Hun tribesman was somewhat flustered.
After realizing that it was safe, it waved at Elder Xue and swam towards the other side of the 'water'. Just as it was about to pass through the 'water', it suddenly rolled its eyes and involuntarily hugged its head. Its facial features were twisted into a ball, as if it was suffering intense pain.
Elder Xue had taught these Hun tribesmen how to endure pain. Their willpower was actually stronger than that of ordinary creatures. For them to be in such pain, one could only imagine what kind of torture they were enduring.
"What happened …" Luo Nian cried out.
"Pop!"
A muffled sound came from the water. The Hun tribesman's head suddenly exploded, and the gray fog drilled out.
The headless Hun tribesman slowly floated in the water before finally landing in front of Luo Nian and Elder Xue. Because the headless corpse had crossed the green line that belonged to it, it was directly burned to ashes.
Luo Nian and Elder Xue stood in place in silence for a while. Luo Nian then asked, "There's no way to pass through this place. Can we still go back?"

Chapter 3832
Luo Nian was well aware that he couldn't go back, but he was scared out of his wits.
Elder Xue didn't say a word as he observed the grey things in the water.
He really didn't expect Levita to have something like this. At this moment, he was very calm as all kinds of hypotheses flashed through his mind. He was the one who came up with the maxim of the Great Conjecture Institute, "Make bold assumptions, verify carefully."
"Luo Nian, don't you think the aura of these things is familiar?"
Elder Xue asked.
"Familiar aura?"
Luo Nian shook his head.
This was the first time he had seen these things.
Elder Xue thought for a moment, then poked his head into the transparent water.
"Splash!"
When he dived into the transparent body of water, the greyish thing in the body of water quickly swam towards him and spread out into sharp teeth to bite him.
But Elder Xue only poked his head in, and before the thing could bite him, he had already retreated.
"Splash!"
The grey thing couldn't leave the water, and could only spread out on the water for a while before slowly retreating.
Elder Xue seemed to enjoy teasing this thing. When the grey thing had just swam away, he stuck his head into the transparent water again, attracting the grey thing back.
He played back and forth four or five times … "Elder Xue, why are you doing this?"
Luo Nian thought he was teasing the thing in the transparent water.
"I'm trying to sense their aura, but the water blocks most of their aura. It's very difficult to sense them, so I want to confirm it a few more times."
"What aura is it?"
Luo Nian couldn't figure it out.
Elder Xue smiled, "I already know." He pointed to his head, "The wisp of consciousness that connected to our minds before, its aura is very similar to theirs!"
Luo Nian's interest was immediately piqued, "The thing in the water is very similar to their consciousness …" "It's possible that this is the soul of a Yang clan member." Elder Xue's hypothesis was even bolder.
"The soul of a Yang clan member …" Luo Nian was surprised, but also somewhat doubtful, "But why would they put their soul here?
Just to snipe us? "
Elder Xue stared at the thing in the water for a while before saying, "I'm not sure, but it's very likely a reward."
"Reward?"
Luo Nian didn't know whether to laugh or cry.
That Hun had just died a miserable death right in front of them. How could this have anything to do with the reward?
Furthermore, a few days ago, the two Hun Clan members had also climbed to this height, but they also stopped here.
Elder Xue didn't care about Luo Nian's mocking expression. Instead, he continued to analyze, "It's impossible for the Yang race to set up an obstacle that cannot be passed."
"That's just because we haven't found the method. It can't be a reward …" Luo Nian insisted.
At this time, Elder Xue stared at Luo Nian and said, "Do you think the probability of finding a human who is similar to you or even weaker to enter Levita and pass the six tests just now is high?"
Luo Nian didn't know why Elder Xue would ask such a question. After thinking for a moment, he said, "The probability of passing is very high!"
"But over the countless years, more than two million people from the six deep space races have entered Levita, and yet only you, me, and three Huns have made it here. Don't you find it strange?"
Elder Xue asked again.
Indeed, Luo Nian didn't think so much. But now that Elder Xue asked, Luo Nian said, "Aren't these so-called six deep space races a little too stupid …" For humans, even if they were placed in the wilderness, it would take them at most tens of thousands of years to develop intelligence. After a few thousand years, they would develop civilization and rapidly prosper.
The six deep space races were born far earlier than that, and the Hun Clan had Elder Xue's teachings.
It was indeed a little unreasonable for them to still be so stupid.
"After the deep space shattered, it lost the conditions for a higher civilization. These six deep space races are like the earthworms and ants that live underground. They have been restricted since birth, so no matter how hard I try to teach them, it doesn't help. They are still stupid in many things …" Elder Xue then asked, "If you put a strand of your soul into an ant, what will happen to the earthworm's brain?"
"A headshot?"
Luo Nian asked tentatively. He had never done such a boring thing.
"Yes." Elder Xue pointed at the white ash on the ground. "From the perspective of the Hun Clan, the Hun Clan are the ants, earthworms, and insects of the underground. They can't bear the soul of a Hun Clan member, which is why their heads exploded."
"Then, Elder Xue, do you think … we won't explode?"
Luo Nian asked.
"We have no choice but to take the risk!"
Whoosh!
Elder Xue dived into the transparent body of water.
Although he had the physical body of a Hun, his soul was that of a human spirit. Even though he came from the Overworld, he was still confident that he was stronger than the Hun Clan.
"Whoosh …" That gray, blurry thing quickly grew out "teeth" and bit Elder Xue. Then, it kept drilling into his mind. This scene was extremely terrifying.
Elder Xue's eyes rolled back and his body spasmed … Luo Nian was so nervous that he didn't know where to put his hands. He was afraid that Elder Xue's head would explode in the next moment.
One breath, two breaths, three breaths … After about twenty breaths, Elder Xue finally stopped trembling. He calmly floated in the transparent body of water and looked at Luo Nian with a profound look in his eyes. At the same time, he hooked his finger at Luo Nian.
"He really took it!"
Luo Nian said excitedly. He wanted to dive into the transparent body of water, but he stopped as soon as he approached the head of the body of water.
He had forgotten that he was different from Elder Xue.
Elder Xue was a human spirit, and one of the strongest among them. Luo Nian was a human from the Overworld of Chaos and was only a True God. The difference was too great.
Elder Xue might be able to take it, but he might not be able to.
"Elder Xue, why don't I wait here for you? You can save me after you get the Skyring Staff," Luo Nian said with a grin.
Unexpectedly, Elder Xue swam out of the transparent body of water and reached out with one hand, grabbing Luo Nian by the neck and dragging him into the transparent body of water.
"Gurgle, gurgle …" At the same time that Luo Nian dived into the body of water, another gray blob came straight for him.
It was as if he saw a gray blooming flower. The petals wrapped around his head before drilling into Luo Nian's brain.
Whoosh!
The next moment, Luo Nian felt a swelling pain as a powerful soul force drilled into his brain.
This soul force was extremely pure and had no imprint. It was completely ownerless, so Luo Nian naturally absorbed it very smoothly.
At the same time, Luo Nian felt an intense swelling pain. This powerful soul force felt like it was about to burst his head open.

Chapter 3833
Luo Nian had never suffered much in his life, so how could he bear such pain?
At this moment, he didn't have any other thoughts except to faint. However, the soul force continued to strengthen Luo Nian's soul, and at the same time, it gave him even more pain! This was inhumane torture … He was in so much pain that he wanted to die. Just as he was about to open his mouth, water poured in.
It took ten breaths longer than Elder Xue, but Luo Nian felt like it was as long as a century.
After the infusion of his soul was complete, Luo Nian lay there in a daze. His powerful soul gave him a new perception of the world, and he was digesting this new perception … "My soul … has become so powerful!"
After a while, Luo Nian swam out of the transparent water. Just a glance at Elder Xue gave him an incomparable aura.
Of course, Elder Xue could sense the change in Luo Nian.
As a peak expert in the main world, Elder Xue's soul was already extremely powerful. Now that the soul force of the Yang clan had been injected into him, it was three to four times stronger. However, Luo Nian was different.
Luo Nian was only a true god in the chaotic world. After receiving the soul force of the Yang clan, his soul had probably grown several times stronger! "This feeling … is too wonderful!"
He had been in so much pain that he wanted to die, but now he was very happy, as if he had experienced a rebirth.
Elder Xue was excited too. To be able to strengthen his soul to this extent was indeed a great surprise, but now he had calmed down. "Why did they inject such powerful soul force into me?"
Levita was the hope left behind after the extinction of the Yang clan. They wouldn't do such meaningless things. There had to be a reason for their souls to be strengthened.
"We'll know when we go up," Luo Nian said confidently.
Elder Xue nodded and continued to walk up the stairs.
After passing through the transparent water, there were no more green lines in front of them to restrict their path.
They climbed up one level, which was two hundred feet, and saw a ball in the middle.
This ball hung in the air like an absolute circle. No matter which side you looked at it from, there was no flaw.
However, the moment their gazes came into contact with the sphere, they felt a sense of loneliness and desolation. This sphere seemed to exist for all eternity. It had no beginning, no end, and no meaning … "What's going on with this sphere?"
Luo Nian asked.
Elder Xue studied the sphere as he walked. He shook his head to indicate that he did not know either.
At this moment, a faint beam of light appeared in front of the two of them. The beam of light constantly changed and gradually took form. It was the little girl that Luo Nian had seen several times in the illusion.
"We meet again," the little girl said indifferently.
Luo Nian looked around and asked nervously, "This isn't an illusion, right …?" "No." The little girl shook her head.
Elder Xue could tell that the little girl herself was an illusion.
"Then … what are you going to do?"
Luo Nian asked.
He actually understood that the little girl represented Levita, but Levita needed an image to communicate with Luo Nian, so he created her.
"As your guide, I will guide you up," the little girl said expressionlessly.
"No need. We have our own legs." Luo Nian waved his hand.
The little girl pointed to the sphere and explained, "This is the Thoughtless World. It's actually an infinitely small and infinitely large point. It contains everything and is empty at the same time. It's a one-grid world."
Luo Nian did not take the little girl's words to heart, but Elder Xue's expression changed greatly. "This is a one-grid world?
A real one-grid world? "
"Of course not." The little girl stared at Elder Xue with a mocking look. "The Thoughtless World contains all matter. Not a single needle can escape. If the Thoughtless World really appeared, you would all become part of it. How could you stand here?"
"This Thoughtless World was made by the Yang clan?"
Elder Xue asked again.
"We didn't make it." The little girl shook her head. "This was created by 'From'."
"Who is' From '?"
Elder Xue asked again.
The little girl tilted her head and thought for a moment. "From is from. It owns everything and creates everything." The little girl continued after a simple explanation. "Next, you just need to climb up."
"What's so difficult about that …" Luo Nian shrugged.
The atmosphere in the Thoughtless World was indeed strange, but so what if it was strange?
At most, it would only affect his mood. Luo Nian didn't even look at it and climbed to the higher level.
The little girl looked at Luo Nian's indifferent look and just smiled without explaining.
After walking around the Levita Tower twice, they climbed to a height of two hundred and thirty feet. At this time, the ball disappeared, and the indescribable desolate atmosphere also disappeared.
But in the center of the tower, there was a huge wall. There were countless lines on the wall, and these lines were very complicated. All kinds of squares and circles were intertwined together, and many lines formed strange things.
"The Wall World, the second grid world," the little girl said.
For Luo Nian and Elder Xue, the Wall World was just a picture. But this picture was much better than the Thoughtless World. They could even feel the vitality of the Wall World. The lines inside formed a moving picture, which was a living creature.
"This is not a test at all. It's like looking at flowers on horseback." Luo Nian shrugged.
The little girl smiled coldly. "Because the Thoughtless World and the Wall World are lower than the level of the world you are in, you will not be affected by the low-level world. When you ascend to a higher level world, I hope you can still talk like this."
Hearing the little girl's words, Elder Xue was slightly stunned, and immediately understood why the Yang clan gave him and Luo Nian strong soul power.
Next, they would probably experience the baptism of the higher level world. The higher the level of the world, the more information it contained, and the higher the requirements for the soul … If it was only a six-grid world, could they bear it with their own ability?
If there were seven or even eight grids, then perhaps … Elder Xue was worried, and his eyes suddenly fell on Luo Nian.
This kid, as a three-grid world creature, could move freely in four-grid or five-grid worlds. Perhaps he could cross those higher level worlds?
Elder Xue thought so, but he didn't say it out loud. Instead, he climbed step by step with Luo Nian.
After climbing more than twenty feet, what appeared in front of them was the three-grid world, which was the world Luo Nian was most familiar with.
Luo Nian had been away from the chaotic world for a long time. Suddenly seeing everything familiar, he felt a little homesick.
Then it was the four-grid world. This time it was Elder Xue's turn to be nostalgic. After all, he stayed in the five-grid world for a long time.
When they climbed to a height of thirty feet, they entered the fifth grid world. This grid world naturally posed no difficulty for them.
But when Luo Nian saw the creatures in the fifth grid world, he couldn't help but laugh. "Yang clan, Yang clan people look like this?"
Elder Xue had never seen a Yang clan person, and he didn't expect them to look so charmingly naive. But he didn't lose his composure like Luo Nian. Instead, he winked at Luo Nian. Don't forget that Levita's guide was still here. She came from the Yang clan.

Chapter 3834
The little girl leading the way didn't mind Luo Nian's rudeness.
There was a huge gap between civilizations, she couldn't even understand why Luo Nian was smiling.
She led the two of them up another twenty feet and saw the sixth grid world.
Elder Xue stopped in front of the six grid world.
The stairs in front of him had disappeared. All he could see were pieces of square frames. These frames fused with each other to form a larger frame, and then quickly split apart. It looked incredible.
As a creature of the four grid world, Elder Xue already had a hard time understanding the deep space. It was only by chance that he obtained the body of the Hun that he barely adapted to it. It would be even harder for him to adapt to the six grid world.
"This is the No Limit Realm, you will have a hard time adapting," the little girl said calmly.
"Even if I can't adapt, can I still find my way through?"
Elder Xue suddenly asked.
Since they were already in Levita, Elder Xue wanted to take the chance to leave alive.
If he couldn't do it himself, he could pin his hopes on Luo Nian. If Luo Nian was still unimpeded, he could just drag him up.
"Of course not," the little girl was a bit confused by Elder Xue's words, "You must fully adapt to this world and see everything clearly in order to pass."
"Adapt?
See everything clearly? "
Elder Xue was excited, "It's almost impossible for creatures of a lower level world to do this, even the powerful beings of your clan can't do it …" "Why can't it be done?
Just accept the soul spark reincarnation, this way the creatures of the fifth grid world will be able to see the sixth grid world clearly. Why else do you think Levita gave you such powerful soul energy? It's so you can withstand the soul spark reincarnation … "the little girl explained.
After hearing this explanation, Elder Xue finally understood.
The Four Spirit Sects also had a similar method, and that was the famous "Crimson Jade Ritual". After the ritual, a creature of the three grid would be successfully transformed into a creature of the four grid. The Crimson Jade Ritual was researched by the Great Conjecture Institute.
Although the Crimson Jade Ritual could transform a creature of the three grid into a creature of the four grid, the process would also be painful. If the ritual failed, the creature of the three grid would also be destroyed.
"Then let's see how many times I can withstand the soul spark reincarnation!"
With that, Elder Xue took a step forward.
When he stepped into the Thoughtless Limit Realm, he felt that his body had also turned into countless square frames of various sizes. Everything in front of him changed drastically, and he could not adapt to this change at all.
Just as Elder Xue was panicking, he saw a bright red square frame appear. The frame enveloped him and Elder Xue felt his body being cut into pieces.
This sensation didn't come from his body but directly from his soul. It wasn't an exaggeration to call it an illusion. The problem was that Elder Xue couldn't tell at all. He only felt that his body and soul were being sliced into countless pieces. This pain was inescapable, and he couldn't even faint. He could only resist it with his willpower.
After the time it took for an incense stick to burn, the entire process finally stopped.
The boxes began to take their positions, lining up and combining with each other to form a unique pattern. Elder Xue also understood this pattern and the steps in the six boxes world.
"I … have completed the Soul Spark Reincarnation?"
Elder Xue said.
"Yes." The little girl stood by the side, completely unaffected by the six-grid world. "It's his turn now."
Of course, she was referring to Luo Nian.
Luo Nian had seen Elder Xue's pained expression just now, and now he was particularly nervous. "If … if I can understand the six-grid world, can I not undergo the Soul Spark Reincarnation?"
The little girl glanced at him coldly. "What nonsense are you spouting?"
Luo Nian helplessly took a step forward, then began to walk up the steps.
The rules of the two worlds were completely different. Those who hadn't undergone the Soul Spark Reincarnation would find it difficult to move around, but Luo Nian really was walking around mindlessly … This time, it was the little girl's turn to change her expression. "You … come from the six-grid world?
Or perhaps you've already undergone the Soul Spark Reincarnation? "
Luo Nian shrugged.
He truly had no problems traversing through different worlds.
As for the Soul Spark Reincarnation, how would he know?
The pale Elder Xue still hadn't recovered from his shock. When he saw Luo Nian so relaxed, a smile appeared on his face. Whether or not he succeeded, at least he had Luo Nian as a guarantee … As he climbed up the steps of the six-grid world, Luo Nian's gaze constantly turned to the center of the tower.
"Hey, what are those square stone steles erected there?"
Luo Nian asked.
"Those aren't stone steles. They're Shengming," the little girl replied.
"Are they tombstones erected for Shengming?"
Luo Nian asked again.
"Shengming is the strongest race in the Thoughtless Limit World. They're also the race that inherited our Yang clan!"
The little girl finally grew impatient. According to the rules, she shouldn't have any emotional fluctuations, but this guy in front of her was really … "What kind of creature looks like stone steles? Such an unlucky race. They're even funnier than the Yang clan." Luo Nian shrugged.
Elder Xue, who was walking at the front, was also speechless. He could only shake his head inwardly.
After climbing past the sixth grid world, they were already at a height of three hundred and sixty feet. When the little girl began her introduction, Elder Xue's legs were trembling slightly.
If he had the chance to experience the Soul Spark Reincarnation in the past, he would've been overjoyed.
However, after successfully enduring the Soul Spark Reincarnation, the pain was truly unbearable. Inferring from the little girl's words, the higher the level of the world, the more painful the Soul Spark Reincarnation would be! "The seventh grid world, God's Immeasurable. Please enter," the little girl said.
This time, the little girl focused her gaze on Luo Nian. She wanted to see Luo Nian endure the torture of the Soul Spark Reincarnation.
Unexpectedly, Luo Nian walked in casually and looked at the center of Levita. His eyes lit up as if he had discovered a new continent. He shouted, "It's bamboo. So many bamboos. Are these bamboos the natives of God's Immeasurable?"
"This guy …" The little girl looked at Luo Nian. On the one hand, she was furious. On the other hand, she was surprised.
He wasn't a creature of five or six grids. Was he a creature of seven grids?
Otherwise, why couldn't the Soul Spark Reincarnation be activated?
It couldn't be that something had happened to Levita and he couldn't perform the Soul Spark Reincarnation, right?
While she was puzzled, Elder Xue had already stepped into the seventh grid world and was tortured to death by the Soul Spark Reincarnation.
When the little girl looked at Luo Nian again, she finally realized that she had been waiting for a weirdo who couldn't be measured by common sense.
"Worlds of different levels don't affect you?"
The little girl couldn't help but ask.
"I don't know." Luo Nian really couldn't answer this question. He pointed at the stairs and said, "Won't you understand if we keep going up?"

Chapter 3835
Luo Nian also wanted to understand the problem with his body. This was a good opportunity.
Each layer was more complex than the last, more obscure than the last, but he could easily pass through each one.
He wanted to know what was his connection to these worlds … Elder Xue was tortured for a long time. The reincarnation of the seventh grid world was even more cruel, but he finally managed to survive. His face was very ugly.
If the complexion of the Hun people was poor, their faces would first turn white, but after a certain degree of whiteness, their faces would begin to wrinkle. Now, Elder Xue's face was wrinkled like a ball of paper. It was easy to imagine what he had just experienced.
"Go, go, keep going …" Elder Xue said weakly.
Luo Nian saw him like this and said worriedly, "Do you want to rest?"
In any case, Levita didn't set a time limit.
But the little girl said, "It's no use. After the reincarnation of the soul, he's been under great pressure. This pressure can't be removed."
Elder Xue nodded. The Four Spirit Sect's "Scarlet Jade Sacrifice" was the same. Although it could turn a three grid life form into a four grid life form, they had to bear great mental pressure forever.
Elder Xue was able to withstand the pressure of crossing a single grid world. However, Elder Xue was a four-grid living being. Now that he had crossed a seven-grid world, the pressure was too great.
"Then what do we do?
Go back? "
Luo Nian frowned.
The little girl glared at Luo Nian, "I don't mind ending his life now."
She had the authority to do so.
Luo Nian could only keep his mouth shut. He went up to support Elder Xue, who was struggling to walk, and slowly climbed up. Elder Xue said slowly, "It seems like my life will end in the eighth grid world. Cough cough … If you die, what will happen to me?"
Luo Nian said gloomily.
His personality was like this. When he didn't have anyone to rely on, he had a trace of ambition. Now that he finally found Elder Xue to communicate with, he immediately returned to his original state. He panicked that Elder Xue was going to die.
But as soon as Luo Nian finished speaking, Elder Xue used all his strength to grab Luo Nian's clothes. He didn't know where the strength came from, but he lifted Luo Nian up by one foot. "You have to know what to do! Even if you die, you have to get the Skyring Staff! "
"Even if I get it, I don't know how to go back," Luo Nian said in a low voice.
"I'm not really dead! I'll take you back to the Main Realm. Don't forget that the real me is still outside Skywheel City! "
Elder Xue recovered a little from his excitement, and the wrinkles on his face lessened.
"Alright, I'll try my best …" Luo Nian replied.
"Louder!"
Elder Xue said excitedly.
"I'll do my best!"
Luo Nian roared.
The pair of hands that gripped his collar slowly loosened, and Elder Xue continued to climb up with a hunched back.
With Luo Nian's speed, it would only take ten breaths of time to walk three laps around Levita from a height of thirty feet, but Elder Xue walked for a full three incense sticks worth of time.
After reaching the end of the seventh grid world, the eighth grid world appeared in front of them.
"Boundless Will."
It was a world full of colors.
In Elder Xue's eyes, there were countless dyes splashed together, entangled, intertwined, and mixed, but the dyes were incompatible with each other.
Even though he knew that he would die if he took that step, Elder Xue patted Luo Nian's chest and walked in without hesitation.
Elder Xue thought that he was very lucky to be able to enjoy the scenery of the eighth grid world, "Boundless Will".
But the moment he entered that world, Elder Xue felt himself being split into countless tiny things, and then slowly eroded away. He finally fell in front of the reincarnation of his soul.
After Luo Nian entered the eighth grid world, he still looked like a normal person.
The little girl on the side clicked her tongue in wonder, but Luo Nian's mood wasn't very good. Although he hadn't known Elder Xue for a long time, he was still the one who guided him into Levita. Moreover, Elder Xue was an important figure in the Four Spirit Gates, and his father Luo Zheng being able to reach this step was all thanks to this old man.
He wasn't in the mood to enjoy the scenery of the Boundless Realm, but instead strode upwards … The deep space races outside Levita had already exploded; they had witnessed the entire process from beginning to end.
Not long after the Xiong people entered, one of them died. The Icy Monkey clansmen were still ridiculing them; after all, the deep space races rarely died at a height of two to three feet. But after that, the Xiong people took their time and reached the height of two hundred feet.
No one had ever broken the height of two hundred feet. The deep space races all stared fixedly at this scene, but they didn't expect that not long after, there would be two dots of light that passed this stage, and then continued upwards … When one of the dots of light disappeared, the deep space races all cried out in alarm.
Some of the deep space races were happy, and some were regretful … most of them hoped that their race would be able to climb up Levita, but there were still some people who feared death. They were more willing to see someone end this "man-eating" tower, and now the Xiong people were their only hope.
"Four hundred feet …" "Is the last person Elder Xue?"
"It might be the new person who joined …" The deep space races all stared at the last dot of light with different thoughts, their hearts full of nervousness.
In the oval-shaped building at the edge of the square, the Xiong "little monster" left behind didn't even blink.
It had entered on the same day as Luo Nian. If the Levita Tower was completely destroyed, it would be responsible for this place in the future.
Seeing the green dots of light disappear one after another, its heart was gradually filled with fear, but it could do nothing except kneel down and mumble a series of prayers … Four hundred and ten feet … Four hundred and twenty feet … Four hundred and thirty feet … "Immeasurable Realm."
Four hundred and forty feet … Four hundred and fifty feet … Four hundred and sixty feet … "Immeasurable Realm."
Four hundred and seventy feet … Four hundred and eighty feet … Four hundred and ninety feet … "Immeasurable Realm."
Every time a world rose to a new level, the scene presented would change completely, and it would give people a very novel feeling.
With the personality of his father, Luo Zheng, he would imprint everything in his heart.
But Luo Nian had a look of boredom, and he felt a little tired after seeing so many dramatic changes of beautiful scenery.
"The Immeasurable Realm is a twelve-grid world, and it's also the world closest to perfection. Worlds one level higher than the Immeasurable Realm are called the Immeasurable Realm …" The little girl talked endlessly.
"Have you reached the top?"
Luo Nian interrupted her.
"The thirteen-grid Immeasurable Realm can't be restored, it can't even be 'imitated', so there are only twelve-grid small worlds in the Levita Tower …" The little girl explained stubbornly.
"I just want to know if you've reached the top!"
Luo Nian interrupted her again.
"You …" The little girl was speechless and said angrily, "It's four hundred and ninety feet now. The top is the top, go in and take it if you can!"

Chapter 3836
Luo Nian raised his head and looked up, then strode forward without hesitation.
He had already come this far, what was there to be afraid of?
When he reached the end of the Infinite Realm, he reached the top of the stairs. In front of Luo Nian was a normal stone door. The surface of the door was very rough, very inconsistent with the exquisite Levita.
Luo Nian didn't think too much and pushed the door open.
"Crack crack crack …" This door hadn't been opened for many years. The rough material didn't decay. With a sharp sound, the door was pushed open by Luo Nian.
The moment he opened the door, Luo Nian paused.
The space inside the door was also very rough.
It was as though someone had used small stones to pave the space. The surface was very rough.
It was worth mentioning that the space inside the door belonged to the fifth grid world. Levita had returned to normal here, but to Luo Nian, it didn't matter if it was normal or not.
"Where is the Skyring Staff?"
Luo Nian turned and asked the little girl.
The little girl stood at the door and didn't enter. She didn't have permission to enter the top floor.
"You can go in and find it yourself." The little girl was still very dissatisfied with Luo Nian.
Luo Nian nodded and entered the top floor space to look around.
He quickly found a purple crystal pillar at the center of the top floor space. There seemed to be something inside this purple crystal pillar. When he walked closer, he found that there was something like a piece of dead wood inside the purple crystal pillar.
He frowned and thought about what this thing was when he slapped his head. "A staff … this is the Skyring Staff!"
This staff seemed to have grown naturally. Its shape was so strange that Luo Nian couldn't react in time.
The only possibility left on Levita's top floor was the Skyring Staff! Thinking that he finally got the most important thing, Luo Nian was very excited.
He immediately took two steps back, one hand forming a palm, the other a fist, and punched at the purple crystal pillar.
"Dong …" A very light muffled sound was heard.
The purple crystal pillar didn't move.
Luo Nian's expression changed several times. He channeled a few more techniques and abilities.
Unfortunately, with his True God cultivation, if he could show off in the universe, it wasn't enough in the divine realm, much less the fifth grid world.
Even if he "worked hard" to squeeze himself to the limit, the purple crystal pillar still didn't move. It didn't even shake, much less leave a mark.
Luo Nian also knew his own limitations. He knew that he was too far behind.
"It's a pity that Elder Xue couldn't hold on any longer and perished. If he had come here with me, that Xiong person's body should have been able to smash through …" After this fruitless attempt, Luo Nian returned to the door to seek help from the little girl.
After all, she was the guide of the Leviathan, and it was not easy for him to reach the top of the tower. He could not let his efforts go to waste just because he could not break the stone pillars.
When the little girl heard Luo Zheng's words, she was also puzzled. "This is beyond my scope. I can't enter this door, and I can't understand the information inside. However … I think with Cong's infinite wisdom, he will definitely not leave you with a dead end. You have to search inside."
Luo Nian felt that this made sense and returned to the empty top floor.
"Other than the amethyst pillar and these rough stones, there's nothing else. Since I can't open the amethyst pillar, I can only find the answer in these rough stones?"
Luo Nian looked at the nearest rough stone. After a careful examination, he found that there were small protrusions on these rough stones, like fist-sized lumps.
"Is there something hidden inside?"
A thought flashed through Luo Nian's mind.
He reached out his hand and dug at the protrusions.
These rough stones were formed by sticking sand together, and the texture was soft. Luo Nian dug out countless grains of sand.
As Luo Nian dug more and more, the surface sand was finally removed, and an oval-shaped object was revealed. It felt soft to the touch, like a piece of meat embedded in the stone. Luo Nian could even feel the heat and energy from it.
"What is this …" Luo Nian did not think much, and grabbed the "meat" with both hands to pull it out.
But just as his hands grabbed it, the "meat" became very soft, and his ten fingers sank directly into it.
Luo Nian was a little surprised. He wanted to pull it out, but the meat stuck to his hands as he pulled it out. The whole "meat" was like melted sugar, completely integrated with his hands. He couldn't get rid of it even if he wanted to.
"This thing, it got into my hands …" Luo Nian looked surprised.
"Calm down! Calm down! "
Luo Nian shouted in his heart.
The only thing he could rely on now was himself, so he must not panic! Although the "meat" kept fusing into his hands, there was no pain in the whole process. Instead, there was a burst of warmth.
The "meat" itself became part of Luo Nian's body, spreading along the surface of his arms. It looked scary, but it was not harmful at all.
After about half an hour, the "meat" was completely fused. Luo Nian only felt that his hands had become a bit thicker. It looked a bit awkward, but he could still use it freely. What surprised Luo Nian even more was that his strength had increased by quite a bit! The "meat" itself contained an amazing amount of energy! "This is … a reward!"
Luo Nian suddenly realized.
When Levita was two hundred feet tall, he had given them souls as a reward. Now Luo Nian was rewarded with a physical body! "As long as I absorb enough meat, I can break that amethyst pillar!"
Luo Nian's mind became clearer.
Without saying a word, he went straight to the nearest rough stone and touched the protrusions on the surface of the stone. After removing a thin layer on the surface, he could see the "meat" with a strange color.
Luo Nian grabbed the "meat" with both hands and let it fuse into his body.
There were three to four hundred of such rough stones in the space above. Luo Nian did not seem to be annoyed at all. He dug them out one by one and fused them … As more and more "meat" was fused, Luo Nian felt a tremendous change in his body. Just his size alone had expanded by four times! "My soul is already comparable to the experts in deep space. Now that my physical body has reached this standard, if I return to the chaotic world, just the strength of my physical body alone will give Father a shock …" Luo Nian thought as he grabbed at the last lump of stone.
With his current strength, he only needed to grab lightly to pull out the "meat" hidden inside.
After swallowing the last "meat" into his body, Luo Nian went straight to the amethyst pillar.
"The Skyring Staff is mine!"
With that, Luo Nian clenched his hand into a fist and punched at the amethyst pillar.
"Crack!"
The top half of the indestructible amethyst pillar suddenly shattered, revealing the top half of the Skyring Staff.

Chapter 3837
