From the outside, there was nothing special about the Sky Ring Staff.
It was just a piece of withered wood that was thin at the bottom and thick at the top. However, the messy tree crown at the top looked bloated and complicated, giving it a mysterious feeling of delicacy.
Luo Nian reached out his hand that had expanded several times and grabbed the Sky Ring Staff.
He couldn't pull it out of the purple crystal.
Luo Nian was afraid that the staff would break, so he punched the bottom half of the purple crystal a few more times, smashing it completely before pulling the Sky Ring Staff out.
"Does this Sky Ring Staff really have the ability to turn the situation around?"
He looked left and right, then looked again, then injected a strand of consciousness into it. The strand of consciousness disappeared as soon as it entered.
"It's useless?"
Luo Nian paused and walked towards the door with the Sky Ring Staff. The little girl still stood there silently.
If the Sky Ring Staff was taken away, Levita would lose its meaning and she would disappear as well. Of course, it would take a long time.
"Hey, how do I use this Sky Ring Staff?"
Luo Nian asked.
The little girl shook her head as usual. "I don't know."
"You don't know anything …" Luo Nian's goal was to bring the Sky Ring Staff back to Luo Zheng, so he wasn't in a hurry to find an answer. "Then how do I leave this place?"
At this moment, a strong suction force came from the Sky Ring Staff and drew a small part of Luo Nian's power.
"Whoosh whoosh …"
A strand of purple light circled around the Sky Ring Staff and shot towards the ceiling of Levita's top floor.
The moment the purple light hit the ceiling, the entire top of Levita began to change!
The deep space races at the bottom of Levita saw the green light climb to the top. Just as they were getting anxious, the green light suddenly disappeared. Usually, when the green light went out, it meant that the living beings in the tower were dead.
Some of the deep space races cheered. They couldn't get it and didn't want the Hun Tribe to get it. However, a small group of deep space races were filled with regret. They didn't want to die in Levita and hoped that someone would end this cruel situation.
But after the green light was extinguished for a while, a purple light slowly appeared. Then, the top of the Levita Tower that looked like a "chimney" slowly opened like a blooming flower. It expanded in all directions, and a burly figure walked out from it.
"Hum …"
At this moment, all the lines on the huge square turned green.
Luo Nian glanced at the green lines. "Does this mean that the restrictions on this square have been lifted?"
Then he jumped down from the top of Levita Tower, and after floating in the air for a while, he landed accurately in front of the door of the oval-shaped building on the far right.
The "little monster" of the Hun Tribe stood at the entrance of the building and watched in a daze as Luo Nian landed in front of it. For a moment, it was unable to come back to its senses. By the time it came back to its senses, it was mumbling something in its mouth, then it lay on the ground and kowtowed to Luo Nian.
"Hey, there's no need to be so courteous …" Luo Nian was a little speechless.
These deep-space races were indeed simple-minded.
In fact, Elder Xue had taught them many principles, but they still believed in the primitive tribes. Elder Xue had no choice but to "disguise" himself as their god, because this was the only way to effectively command the Hun tribe.
When the deep-space races saw Luo Nian fly down with a staff, their eyes were bloodshot.
After the restriction on the giant square was lifted, they rushed toward Luo Nian without any communication.
By the time Luo Nian had helped the "little monster" up, he saw the strange races rushing towards him.
"Wu wu wu wu …"
"Guga, guga …"
"Wah, wah!"
The deep-space races let out a series of roars. Luo Nian could not understand a single word. In the entire deep space, Elder Xue was probably the only one who had learned the language of the six deep-space races.
He did not understand, but Luo Nian could roughly guess what they were saying.
It was nothing more than nonsense like "Hand over the Skyring Staff, or we'll kill you."
The little monster mumbled something on the side, then a trace of courage appeared on its face. It actually stood in front of Luo Nian to protect him, but as soon as it stood still, Luo Nian reached out a large hand and threw it to the side.
"I'm not like when I first entered the deep space. I don't need your protection …"
Luo Nian was overjoyed when he received the soul power reward. This was a step to the top. Unfortunately, his physical body was still so weak. Now, his physical body was terrifyingly strong. He could feel great power surging in his body with every movement, and he was just worried that he had no target to release this power to.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Nian swung the Skyring Staff like a wooden stick and pointed it at the deep-space races.
The three leading Icy Apes were the first to jump out, and immediately pounced on Luo Nian with their agile figures.
These deep-space races were not very smart, but they were still a race born in deep space. Their physical talents were far stronger than the beings of the Main World, and even more so than the beings of the Primal Chaos World.
But now, strictly speaking, Luo Nian was a member of the Nurturing Race, a top expert of the Nurturing Race!
The Icy Apes were unimaginably fast, but in Luo Nian's eyes, they were extremely slow.
He raised the Skyring Staff and charged forward. "Smash, smash, smash."
The three Icy Apes did not know what was going on. They only felt their vision blur and their backs hurt. An irresistible force came crashing down on them, and they were already lying on the ground.
The other deep-space races saw the same thing, but they didn't get hit by the attack.
Luo Nian was afraid of breaking the Skyring Staff, so he held back most of his power. Otherwise, how could these Icy Apes still be alive?
"I know you are stupid, but you don't have to come here to seek death."
Luo Nian glared at the other deep-space races.
When the deep-space races saw the Icy Apes in such a state, they no longer had any will to fight …
With this glare, they all took a few steps back, not daring to charge forward.
"Let's go!"
Luo Nian turned around and threw the "little monster" on his back. With a leap, he flew out of the huge plaza.
After passing the perimeter of the plaza, Luo Nian looked up and found that he was at the highest point in Skywheel City.
Those spiders had carried him up the stairs for a long time, and they had climbed up from underground.

After flying towards the southwest of the Skywheel City for some distance, Luo Nian saw a giant strata floating in the air. Each strata had a diameter of hundreds of miles. These giant strata should have once been a complete continent.
Following the little monster's directions, Luo Nian flew toward one of the huge pieces of ground.
Before he could get close, the little monster let out a series of excited cries. Luo Nian looked over and saw black pillars of smoke rising from the center of the huge piece of ground!

Chapter 3838
Below the black pillars was a large city. This was the settlement of the Xiong Tribe.
The settlements of these deep space races were very crude and maintained the form of primitive tribes. Elder Xue had brought some unique construction techniques to the Xiong Tribe and helped the Xiong Tribe build a beautiful city. However, this city was now destroyed.
Yu Yi.
The deep space races in the plaza had a way to send the news out.
In reality, when the Xiong Tribe climbed two hundred feet to Levita a few days ago, the news had spread to the other deep space races. On one hand, they hoped that Elder Xue could discover the secrets of Levita. On the other hand, they wanted to obtain complete information from Elder Xue.
Therefore, when to attack the Xiong Tribe was very important.

Therefore, before Luo Nian reached the top of Levita, the five deep space races had already set off for the Xiong Tribe.
In the middle of the settlement was a wooden, stacked loft. Beneath the wooden loft was a slender underground passageway. In the innermost layer of the passageway was an underground secret room where the white-haired Elder Xue sat.
"Bam bam bam …"
A series of muffled sounds came from above.
"What … is that sound?" Elder Xue asked.
Elder Xue's main body was still a creature with four boxes. Even though he had stayed in the deep space for a long time, he was still limited by his life's level. His recognition of the surrounding scenery was still very blurry. Although he heard the huge muffled sound, he couldn't tell what it was.
He could only hear the sound.
"Elder Xue … they are attacking the pavilion …" A red-robed Hun man said from the side.
Elder Xue did not move from his spot. He only murmured, "They came so quickly?"
In reality, when Elder Xue had planned to go to Levita with Luo Nian, he had already guessed that this day would come.

Elder Xue had built complex tunnels in the bottom level to make it easier for the Xiong Tribe to escape.
"Alright!"
Elder Xue nodded.

If he couldn't see Luo Nian, he wouldn't be satisfied even if he died.
The Hun man carried Elder Xue on his back, pushed open the door of the secret room, and walked to the side of the passageway. But just as he took two steps, a spear came flying at him.
"Pu!"

"My Icy Ape Tribe has always treated Elder Xue as an honored guest. We will definitely not do any worse than those trash Xiong Tribe members. Why must Elder Xue run away?" The golden-furred Icy Ape asked.
Elder Xue pretended not to understand as he fumbled around in an attempt to leave.
But the golden-furred Icy Ape sneered and lifted Elder Xue up. With a soft yell, a layer of black light enveloped its body. Then, it brought Elder Xue straight through the ground.
As it stood on the surface, it roared with Elder Xue in hand, "Everyone, stop!"
The Xiong Tribe members were still fighting with all their might. Even though they were being pushed back, their fighting spirit was still high. But when they saw Elder Xue being captured, they all froze.
When the other deep space races saw Elder Xue, their eyes were filled with greed.
The news of Luo Nian breaching Levita hadn't reached them yet. They only wanted to get information about Levita from Elder Xue.
"We don't have a blood feud with the Xiong Tribe. As long as Elder Xue is willing to tell us the secrets of Levita, we five deep space races will retreat. How about it?" The golden-furred Icy Ape said.
"The Woodna Tribe agrees!"
"We agree too …"
"I agree!"
The deep space races immediately echoed.
The golden-furred Icy Ape placed Elder Xue in front of him and said word by word, "I know you understand our language. Don't pretend. If you don't tell us, we'll kill every last one of the Xiong Tribe members!"
This threat was fatal. Elder Xue had built up the Xiong Tribe single-handedly. He still had deep feelings for them.
It wasn't that Elder Xue didn't want to share information about Levita. But according to Levita's settings, these barbarian races didn't have the right to climb above two hundred feet!
He hesitated for a moment. The golden-furred Icy Ape made a throat-slitting gesture. "Keep killing!"
The deep space races were about to start another massacre …
"Wait!"
Elder Xue shouted. Then, he said in jerky language, "Once you're two hundred feet in front of Levita, you only need to answer a few questions …"
He could only tell the truth.
Once he told them everything he knew, he would be killed just like the Xiong Tribe.
So as he spoke, he tried to think of a way to get out of this situation. He hadn't been in such a passive situation for countless years. After all, these deep space races had conflicts with each other. They restrained each other and wouldn't join forces to attack the Xiong Tribe.
Now, he really had no way to deal with this …
Just as Elder Xue was about to fall into despair, he suddenly felt a powerful ripple.
Even someone as slow as Elder Xue could feel it. These deep space races naturally felt it even more clearly. They felt a powerful ripple of energy come from above. When they saw it clearly, that thing was only a dozen or so feet away from the golden-furred Icy Ape.
Zhang.
The golden-furred Icy Ape felt something was wrong. By the time it reacted and wanted to run, it was already too late.
"Boom!"

In Elder Xue's vision, everything else was made of rings. Only Luo Nian was clear.

"But this place is too dangerous. You shouldn't have come," Elder Xue said.

Luo Nian said with a confident smile.

At the top of Levita Tower, Luo Nian had discovered this characteristic of the Skyring Staff.
This staff needed to absorb power to help him …
This time, the Skyring Staff had absorbed a little too much energy. Luo Nian felt his limbs go soft. However, the Skyring Staff suddenly released a great pressure that swept through everything. Whether it was the Hun tribe or the other tribesmen, they all felt their legs fill with lead. They were unable to get up from the ground.

Chapter 3839
Although Luo Nian had the body and soul of an expert, his abhijna was still limited to the Divine Dao of the Chaos World.

A strong saint, an even stronger god …

After they kowtowed for a while, Elder Xue said something to the Xiong people. The Xiong people charged murderously at the outsiders.
Because these outsiders had directly attacked Elder Xue, the Hun people were very angry. They did not hold back at all and executed the invaders. For a time, the smell of blood spread throughout the settlement.
Four hours later, in an exquisite wooden hall, Luo Nian and Elder Xue discussed the matter of returning.
Actually, Luo Nian knew the way back. He had come here to send the "little monster" home and bring Elder Xue back.

Elder Xue had a strange expression. "You don't know? After the great calamity that wiped out the Four Spirit Gates, no living being in the Main World can return. Returning means death … "
Not only that, but it was also the same in the deep space.
During the decisive battle between the Yang and Dan races, there were some fish that escaped the net from both races. Now, it was unknown whether these fish that escaped the net were still alive, but they would never be able to return to the deep space. This was due to the restrictions of the rules.
In the Chaotic World, when Ku Shu heard that Taishang Laojun had met Elder Xue, he had also determined that Elder Xue was living in the deep space based on this point. This was because it was very difficult for Elder Xue to break through this restriction. It was impossible for him to cross the main world and reach the Chaotic World.
"So I have no choice but to go back alone?" Luo Nian asked regretfully.

Elder Xue smiled. "You have the Skyring Staff, and can control the life and death of all beings in the deep space. How would they dare to take revenge on me?"
While Elder Xue's words were reasonable, Luo Nian was still worried. He suddenly said to the Skyring Staff, "I want some guards to protect them … …"
The Skyring Staff once again absorbed Luo Nian's power.
"Rumble … …"
As the ground trembled, human-shaped statues climbed out of the ground. There were more than a thousand of them.
Luo Nian finally relaxed when he saw the guards created by the Skyring Staff.
But the "miracle" Luo Nian created attracted the worship of the Hun Clan … …
After doing all this, Luo Nian stepped on the path back.
He left the shattered ground and returned to Skyring City. From the center of Skyring City, he headed north to the furthest point. After passing through the passageway, he successfully found the round secret room he had come from.
After activating the teleportation formation in the secret room, rays of light entered his body from top to bottom and "devoured" him bit by bit.
From a complete person to a broken body, and then completely gone … …
Soon, Luo Nian's body appeared in the rhombus formation above the Eternal Divine Court.
Luo Nian looked around and then jumped down from the Eternal Divine Court.
"Whoosh … …"
It had taken Luo Nian several months to climb from the bottom to the top of the holy pillar, but now that he could fly freely with the Skyring Staff, it was naturally much easier to climb up and down the holy pillar.
Elder Jiang Ziya had been down there for too long. Luo Nian felt that he had to meet him first.
"Whoosh!"
When Luo Nian landed on the ground ten feet away, his body suddenly floated in mid-air and then flew towards the nearest sub-world.
In the subworld … …
Jiang Ziya was piecing together pieces of the statue.
After such a long time, Luo Nian had not been able to return to the subworld. He was most likely dead.
Jiang Ziya was not a person who gave up easily.
Back when he was exiled in Jixu Space, he did not compromise. In the end, he not only succeeded in controlling Jixu Space, but also drilled a hole in the chaos world.
He did not dare to leave the subworld now. Without Luo Nian's help, he could not climb up the holy pillar, let alone return to Jixu Space … …
After trying many plans, he focused on this statue. The statues made by the Four Spirit Gates could move freely in the Xuan Amount World.
If he could control this thing, perhaps he could find his way out bit by bit?
However, the Four Spirit Gates' craftsmanship was very high. Jiang Ziya had been piecing together for so long and only managed to repair a small part of the broken statue. It would probably take decades to completely repair it.
However, he had survived so many chaos epochs in Jixu Space. What was a mere few decades to repair a statue?
"Whoosh … …"
Today, he was immersed in repairing the statue as usual. Suddenly, he heard a slight sound.
The moment he looked up, he saw a figure flying in. It was Luo Nian, who was carrying a wooden stake on his shoulder.
Jiang Ziya's face was obviously dull. He cursed, "You little brat!" and then laughed.
Even an old monster who had lived for thousands of chaos epochs could be so emotional.
"Senior, sorry to keep you waiting!" Luo Nian smiled and greeted him.
"Why so long? I was worried that you would die up there. Did you climb to the top of the holy pillar?" Jiang Ziya asked.
Because he was isolated here, Jiang Ziya knew nothing about what happened in the chaos world.
"I did reach the top of the holy pillar, but … …" Luo Nian said.
"Did you find anything?" Jiang Ziya was even more surprised. He knew how important the Nirvana token on the holy pillar was.
"I did, but … …" Luo Nian continued.
"But what!" Jiang Ziya hurriedly asked. "Things are different from what you think. Many things happened during this time," Luo Nian explained.

Chapter 3840

Luo Nian told him about his exploration of the Holy Pillar and his trip to the deep space to get the Skyring Staff.

After studying the Xuan Liang world for so long, Jiang Ziya had never seen this world properly.

Levita could release soul reincarnation, so the Skyring Staff might be able to do the same.

Luo Nian smiled. "You'll know soon …"
He could feel the Skyring Staff drawing energy from him.
Whoosh!
Then the Skyring Staff released an invisible force that enveloped Jiang Ziya.

Luo Nian was actually worried that Jiang Ziya couldn't take it. After all, there was a certain risk in soul reincarnation.
However, after thinking about it carefully, although Jiang Ziya was a creature from a three-lattice world, he was not comparable to those barbaric races in the deep space. It should not be difficult for Jiang Ziya to reincarnate a soul lattice that was one level higher.

"What is this … Why are you torturing me like this?" Jiang Ziya asked weakly.

Seeing Luo Nian walk out of the secondary world, he naturally picked up the rope on the ground. He was afraid that he would get lost in the World of Xuan Liang.
Just as he grabbed the rope and stepped into the World of Xuan Liang, those flickering light dots disappeared and were replaced by a vast, complete, and enormous world.
"This, this, the soul spark reincarnation you're talking about is …"
"It's to help you become a creature of the Xuan Liang world. If your soul is strong enough, you can even become a creature of the deep space …"
Jiang Ziya was as excited as he could be. After all, this was the world that he had studied for countless eras. Now that he could finally walk around in it, he was like a child who had just learned how to walk and ran around freely.
If there was enough time, Jiang Ziya was willing to stay in the World of Xuan Liang.
However, according to Luo Nian, the situation in the Chaotic World was very dangerous. They really couldn't waste time in the World of Xuan Liang.
…
Chaotic World.
Li Mountain.
In Jinwu, Phoenix was bored to death as she looked at the blood-red sky that was getting farther and farther away.
Ever since she led that fatty Bofan to occupy the Yu Sheng-tian world, she rarely left the Nirvana World.
Managing the Yu Sheng-tian world was very interesting. After all, she was an overlord in the Yu Sheng-tian world. No one dared to go against her. This made her feel like she was once the master of the Phoenix Nest.
She didn't expect a disaster to happen so suddenly. Those destructive energy balls could devour everything.
Phoenix could leave the Nirvana World to avoid the disaster, but Bofan couldn't. This huge four-grid world creature could only watch the energy balls fall on his head.
Not long after, Phoenix returned to the Yu Sheng-tian world. There was a huge hole in front of her. More than half of the Yu Sheng-tian world was devoured. Even the core of the Heaven's Executor was destroyed.
After that, Phoenix couldn't stay in the Nirvana World anymore. She could only return to Li Mountain and watch the Land of Beginnings expand and grow in boredom.
The speed of the expansion of the Land of Beginnings was almost beyond everyone's expectations. Even Luo Zheng didn't foresee it.
The larger the size of the Land of Beginnings, the faster it devoured those heads. The speed of expansion also increased exponentially.
The once huge Li Mountain was like a small boat in the sea compared to the Land of Beginnings.
On the Jade Platform, Luo Zheng sat cross-legged with his eyes closed. He was looking at the Chaotic World from the perspective of the Land of Beginnings.
"The Mother World didn't come close to us. It started to move away from us in the other direction," Luo Zheng said with his eyes closed.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Fuxi, Nuwa, and the Ancient Gods of Chaos were relieved.
A few days ago, the sudden appearance of the Mother World made everyone nervous.
They knew the character of the Evil Deities. They were too difficult to deal with. Once entangled with them, they would lose a layer of skin.
Although the Land of Beginnings was now bigger than the Mother World, now was definitely not the time to start a war.

"Flying boat?"
"Let me stop it …"
"We can't let the Evil Deities enter the Land of Beginnings. Otherwise, there will be more trouble!"
Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others volunteered. Even "Mo" wanted to join in the fun.
Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "There's no need for you. My inner incarnation can now freely leave the Land of Beginnings. Let me go."
No matter what the Evil Deities planned, Luo Zheng would at most lose an inner incarnation. Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others did not insist.
Then, with a thought from Luo Zheng, an identical version of himself appeared on Mount Li.
The next moment, this inner incarnation appeared outside the Land of Beginnings with the flying boat, flying in the empty Chaotic World.
The distance between the Mother World and the Land of Beginnings, these two behemoths, did not seem far, but in fact, the distance between them was very far. The two flying boats took two days to travel at high speed.
The two flying boats slowly approached … Luo Zheng stood on one side of the flying boat and looked at the opposite flying boat. After the enchantment on the top of the flying boat opened, a black figure shot out directly from it and floated in the void. It looked down at Luo Zheng below and let out a burst of unruly laughter. The owner of this laughter was the Evil Deities.

Chapter 3841
Luo Zheng did not say a word, quietly waiting for the shrill laughter of Evil Deities to stop.
"I'm very happy that you're not dead," Evil Deities looked at the huge Land of Origin in the distance. "And you have such a huge Land of Origin. Is this the world I used to live in?"
"Yes," Luo Zheng nodded.

"Yes, they are still half the masters of that continent," Luo Zheng replied.
Evil Deities had a strange smile on his face. "You can use them to threaten me?"

Luo Zheng would not do such a thing, and with the character of Evil Deities, even his beloved could not be threatened.

The Land of Origin devouring the heads was a snowball process.
Originally, the mother world had the most advantage because the mother world was the largest, and the efficiency of devouring was far higher than the other two competitors.
But in the beginning, Evil Deities did not understand the meaning of the Serpent Spirit King, and did not know how to deal with these heads. Instead, they were overwhelmed by the heads, and even the mother world was almost devoured.
By the time Evil Deities dealt with the mother world, Luo Zheng's Land of Origin and the pyramid had already caught up, and the mother world was now lagging behind.
"The giant pyramid …" Luo Zheng gazed at the void, his eyes flickering with worry.
"If we want to deal with it, it's best if we work together," Evil Deities suggested.
Previously, when Luo Zheng led the Monkey Spirit Sect to attack the Clear Jade Heavens, he had formed a temporary alliance with the Bird Spirit Sect. Now, it seemed to be only natural for him to join forces with the Mother World controlled by the Evil Deities.
However, Luo Zheng would rather believe Bird Lord than Evil Deities. He smiled and asked, "Join forces? How?"
「 Your birthplace has become one with my homeworld 」 Evil Deities replied.
Luo Zheng asked in confusion, "Can the Land of Origin be integrated with each other?"
"I'll just use my Mother World to devour the Birthplace," Evil Deities said matter-of-factly.
"On what basis?"
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes. Although he was not surprised by the answer, he still felt a little annoyed.
Evil Deities caught Luo Zheng's anger, and the smile on his face grew wider. "Why? On the basis that I'm stronger than you. "
"Why do you say that?" Luo Zheng's expression turned cold.
"We can try," Evil Deities smiled sinisterly, and suddenly bent a finger.
A ray of colorful light flickered on his fingertip.
It was Dao-Fusion energy.
As Evil Deities flicked his finger, the ray of light shot toward Luo Zheng's head.
Facing the sudden attack, Luo Zheng was not surprised at all.
Just as the finger light was about to hit him, his body became blurry, and then he disappeared ….
The colorful ray of light hit the flying boat, and immediately turned into a dazzling white energy ball. The entire flying boat was annihilated, and not even a piece of it escaped.
Evil Deities did not even need to observe with his eyes. He waved his hands, and a long whip made of Dao-Fusion energy spread out from his fingers, and lashed it toward the void.
Luo Zheng's figure flickered in the air. After dodging dozens of whips, he reached out and gently pinched the long whip.
"Explode," Evil Deities spat out a single word.
The long whip turned into a blazing white ball of light that enveloped Luo Zheng.
But as soon as the ball of light expanded, it miraculously shrank rapidly, and then dissipated...
Luo Zheng's fingers pinched the long whip that no longer existed, and he said indifferently, "Although Dao-Fusion energy is strong, it's still the power of a three-grid world. This is your natural limit."
"Is that so?"
Evil Deities grinned, and his figure flashed toward Luo Zheng again.
This time, Evil Deities raised his hand and waved a long whip. This time, however, the long whip was not made of Dao-Fusion energy. Instead, it was made of green, black, and red energy.
Luo Zheng grabbed the end of the long whip, and began to analyze the long whip's energy.
But the moment he analyzed it, the corners of Luo Zheng's eyes trembled slightly.
The energy that made up this long whip was...
Bloodline power...
This was the power of a four-grid world!
And there were as many as nineteen types?
Even nineteen types of bloodline power were a piece of cake for Luo Zheng. After analyzing it, traces of pale yellow energy emerged from Luo Zheng's fingers. This pale yellow energy wrapped around the three-colored long whip like a spider web, and the long whip began to rapidly disintegrate...
Evil Deities waved his hand confidently, and circles of brown ripples were released from his palm. When the brown ripples came into contact with the pale yellow energy, they actually devoured it completely.
Seeing the brown ripples, Luo Zheng was even more astonished.
Using several or even dozens of bloodline powers was not a big deal, but Evil Deities could clearly analyze the bloodline powers and find the energy to counter them, just like he could. Did this mean that Evil Deities had all of the bloodline powers?
"Buzz buzz buzz..."
When the brown ripples rolled towards Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng pointed his finger lightly, and small pieces of lightning disintegrated them again. These lightning also followed the long whip and went straight towards Evil Deities.
Both of them released various bloodline powers along the long whip, countering each other's attacks, and this continued for six or seven rounds.
Evil Deities felt a little bored, and said, "You're right, natural limitations are the scariest, so I've been studying how to break this limitation. 371 types of bloodline powers are very powerful, but it's still not enough..."
After saying this, his other hand drew a circle in the air, and a white hexagonal snowflake appeared in the circle.
Then Evil Deities lightly knocked on the hexagonal snowflake.
"Crack!"
The hexagonal snowflake shattered.
Disappeared...
Luo Zheng had a bad premonition, but he did not understand it immediately.
In the next moment, Evil Deities disappeared, but his voice slowly came.
"If I want, I can kill you a thousand times, ten thousand times..."
"In theory, I can make you have the illusion that time is frozen..."
"Unfortunately, you won't be able to feel the beauty of this state. You'll only feel confused!"
"Am I right?"
Evil Deities' voice was intertwined, as if he was speaking at the same time, but he could control the order of his voice at will.
By the time he finished speaking, Evil Deities had already appeared in front of Luo Zheng, behind him, left, and right. In the end, Evil Deities was one foot away from Luo Zheng, and said at the same time, "This is called Stasis Extreme State."

Chapter 3842
Under the extreme state of stagnation, Luo Zheng's thinking became very slow.
The words of Evil Deities entered his ears, but Luo Zheng could not understand them.
When Evil Deities stretched out his hand and drew a circle in the air, the hexagonal snowflake that had been broken and scattered reassembled in the circle, forming a complete snowflake. Only then did Luo Zheng return to normal.
The moment he returned to normal, the words of Evil Deities drilled into Luo Zheng's mind. At the same time, Luo Zheng discovered that Evil Deities had unknowingly approached him.
"Extreme state of stagnation?"
Luo Zheng's expression changed dramatically.
He had been wondering why the Origin Divine Blood could not be decomposed. He did not expect Evil Deities to use the power of deep space!
Evil Deities did not speak, only looking at Luo Zheng with a playful expression. He enjoyed this feeling very much.
When Luo Zheng told him that there was a wider world outside, he probably had the same expression, but now it was Luo Zheng's turn.
"How did you get this level of power?" Luo Zheng asked.
Evil Deities fiddled with the hexagonal snowflake. "Do you want to know?"
"Of course!"
"Join us, and I'll tell you," Evil Deities said with a smile.
"Impossible," Luo Zheng replied.
Evil Deities placed the hexagonal snowflake in front of Luo Zheng. "I heard that you have directly fought with the Downtrodden. You should know that the power he possesses is not something you can fight against, even if you rely on the Land of Origin. You will take everyone with you.
Go to hell, and I can save you! Save everyone around you! "
Evil Deities had a trace of fanaticism in his eyes, as if saving Luo Zheng was saving the world.
When did this guy ever care about the life and death of others? Luo Zheng shook his head. "I don't need you to save me."
After saying that, he turned around to leave. The flying boat was destroyed by the Evil Deities. Luo Zheng needed to use the Great Shift to return to the Land of Origin.
However, just as his body was about to enter the Space Passage, the Evil Deities grabbed the Space Passage and crushed it, blocking Luo Zheng's path. "Aren't you afraid of death?"
"Why should I be afraid?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Then tell me, what is the meaning of my existence?" Evil Deities asked again.
Luo Zheng's expression became a little strange, and he looked at him with a puzzled look.
"I have all of his memories," the Wretch God said. "I am also a true body. However, all of my memories and all of my talent are for the sake of serving him. That shouldn't be my purpose …."
Although Luo Zheng and Evil Deities were both doppelgangers, Luo Zheng's Inner Incarnation was absolutely connected to Luo Zheng. Evil Deities' doppelgangers were considered independent entities, and the original body did not have complete control over them.
"You have to ask Evil Deities this question." Luo Zheng shrugged helplessly.
"Then take me away! I don't want to go back! I can teach you how to transform into a higher level of power! "Evil Deities continued.
Luo Zheng looked over with a strange expression. He realized that the doppelgangers of Evil Deities seemed to be … unstable?
And he could use this to his advantage …
Just as Luo Zheng made his decision, an indescribable black spiral pierced through the Void.
The black spiral was actually a spear thousands of feet long, and its target was Evil Deities.
Evil Deities also sensed that something was wrong. He hurriedly lifted the hexagonal snowflake in his hand and pointed it gently. Just as the snowflake was about to collapse, the black spiral spear was one step faster.
"Pop!"
Luo Zheng only heard a muffled sound. Evil Deities' head had been pierced, and then Evil Deities' vitality was completely extracted by the black spear. In the blink of an eye, they withered into a shriveled corpse.
"Sigh, it's troublesome to manage these traitors …"
"Each of them has a rebellious heart. None of them are easy to deal with."
"Before dealing with the Downgraded, I won't do anything to your Land of Origin. I hope you won't do anything to the Mother World!"
Hearing these words, Luo Zheng understood that this was Evil Deities' original body.
Without waiting for Luo Zheng to reply, the dried corpse quickly shrank, and cracks appeared on its skin. It collapsed into countless powder that scattered into the Void. The thousand-feet long black spear also disappeared.
…
After returning to the Land of Origin, Luo Zheng told them what he had learned.
Everyone on the Emerald Terrace was stunned when they heard this.
"What? You mean someone comprehended the power of deep space? "Ku Shu, who had descended, also had a look of disbelief.
Luo Zheng nodded. "The power aura he displayed is very familiar. It's very similar to the energy in the sword, and it's so strong that I can't resist it!"
"Could it be that you're mistaken? Maybe it's some kind of fused bloodline power?" Ku Shu asked.
"But he still has the complete bloodline power, which is all the power of the Origin Divine Blood," Luo Zheng said again. "He can resolve and counteract any power I use with the Origin Divine Blood …"
"How can Evil Deities obtain so much bloodline power?"
"The Origin Divine Blood is not on him!"
"That guy only relies on the fusion of Dao energy …"
Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others looked as if they had seen a ghost.
When Ku Shu heard the words "fusion of Dao energy," he asked, "He fused all the Divine Daos by himself?" "Yes, he has an extraordinary talent in the fusion of energy," Luo Zheng answered, and suddenly realized why Ku Shu asked this question. Then he said, "The low-level world can't be restored to the high-level world. Can the energy of the low-level world be restored to the high-level world?
The energy of the Lower Realm? "
According to the information Luo Zheng knew, the 3,000 Divine Daos were the result of the descent of the bloodline power of the Origin Divine Blood.
The Evil Deities did not have the means to obtain the Origin Divine Blood, so the only possibility was to restore it upward!
"The Great Conjecture Institute tried to do this and failed. But this hypothesis is indeed possible …" Ku Shu said with a sigh.
The Great Conjecture Institute had invested a lot of energy in this area, but they had no clue and had to give up in the end.
If a human from the Chaotic World could be successfully restored, not only would Ku Shu be depressed, but the elders of the Great Conjecture Institute would also be in a bad mood.
"Although the Evil Deities do not intend to attack us, we will still have to face them. Whether it is the Evil Deities or the Degraders, they all have the power of the Deep Space, and we …" Nuwa said worriedly.
"We can only pin our hopes on Nian 'er," Luo Zheng said with a bitter smile.
Hua Tianming was sitting on one side of the Emerald Terrace.
After Luo Nian left, Hua Tianming was arranged to sit on the Emerald Terrace. If anything happened to Gujian, Hua Tianming would report it immediately.
Now that Luo Nian had left for some time, there was no news of him so far. Every day that passed, everyone's hearts grew heavier, and their depression deepened.
Was there any possibility of Luo Nian returning?
At this moment, Hua Tianming's shoulders trembled slightly, and he turned his face. "Gujian, Gujian seems to be trembling …"
All the people on the Emerald Platform had been waiting for this sentence day and night. Including Ku Shu, all of them gathered in front of Hua Tianming at the first moment.

Chapter 3843
Earlier, Ku Shu had used "Elder Xue" to calm everyone's hearts, including those in the Courtyard of Great Guessing and those in the Land of Beginning.
However, these people were all intelligent people. Putting aside whether or not Elder Xue was really alive, the possibility of Luo Nian entering deep space to find Elder Xue was very small, let alone obtaining another object. This was as ethereal as the moon in the water.
No matter how small the hope was, it was still hope. During this period of time, people rarely mentioned Luo Nian, but everyone was worried about him.
So when Hua Tianming had the slightest reaction, they immediately surrounded him.
Hua Tianming clearly felt the Lonely Sword tremble a second time, then a third time, a fourth time … He followed the rhythm and interpreted the words.
"I, am, back," Hua Tianming said.
Everyone on the Jade Stage looked at each other, their faces full of joy.
Luo Zheng directly asked Hua Tianming, "Nian 'er, did you bring Elder Xue back?"
Everyone kept quiet, not affecting Hua Tianming from receiving the signal.
After a while, Hua Tianming said, "No."
The people beside him looked disappointed. Luo Zheng frowned and said nothing.
Ku Shu changed his question. "You didn't see Elder Xue?
Can you obtain that object? "
Although everyone no longer held any hope, they still stared at Hua Tianming.
Seeing everyone staring at him so seriously, Hua Tianming felt rather unnatural. But when he received Luo Nian's words, he no longer cared about this. He said cheerfully, "He saw Elder Xue and obtained that object …"
"Hua!"
Everyone on the Jade Stage became excited.
The three great Chaos Ancient Gods were no exception. The lairs that contained their huge bodies also swayed.
The people living in Li Mountain even felt the mountain shaking …
"Shh, he's still talking," Hua Tianming said.
Just a moment ago, the atmosphere was still a little heated. Now, everyone was quiet and did not say a word, but they were very excited.
"I have already obtained the Skyring Staff. Elder Xue cannot enter the main world and can only stay in the deep space. I will now return to the chaos world with Jiang Ziya. Yeah …" Hua Tianming continued to pass on Luo Nian's message.
Hearing the last "yeah", Luo Zheng and the others didn't know whether to laugh or cry.
…
After a short communication on the Holy Pillar, Luo Nian and Jiang Ziya set off on their way back.
The Chaotic World was still quite a distance away from the Saint Pillar. At that time, Luo Nian dragged Jiang Ziya all the way and bumped into a lot of things.
Now that Jiang Ziya had completely adapted to the Main World, and Luo Nian's strength had increased greatly, their return speed was naturally much faster. The two of them directly jumped down from the top of the Saint Pillar and glided down like two agile swallows.
"Whoosh, whoosh …"
Several hours later, the two of them returned to the original grassland.
After passing through the grassland and crossing a river, they would enter a deep mountain. The entrance to Ji Deleted Space was in that deep mountain.
Just as the two of them were flying along the grassland, a strange movement came from below.
Swish swish swish swish …
Luo Nian and Jiang Ziya sensed it almost at the same time. There seemed to be many slender things flying across the grassland.
"What is it?
Snakes? "
Jiang Ziya frowned.
Although he had undergone the Soul Lattice Reincarnation, he needed to resist the pressure of the Mysterious World, so he didn't release his Divine Sense.
"No, it's those tentacles," Luo Nian replied.
"That's good." Jiang Ziya nodded.
Jiang Ziya had killed those tentacle-like creatures in Ji Deleted Space, and they weren't very powerful.
Swish swish swish …
After running in the grassland for a while, the tentacles flew out and swept toward Luo Nian and Jiang Ziya.
When these tentacles all moved out, Jiang Ziya's expression changed abruptly. There were probably hundreds of thousands of tentacles, and they swept over like a black cloud slowly rising …
"Ha!"
Jiang Ziya took out the Holy Whip and whipped it down fiercely.
Swish swish swish swish swish …
Countless whip shadows spread out as if they were blooming with golden light, whipping those tentacles into pieces.
Although the power of the God Beating Whip was not bad, it could still maintain its vitality even after being smashed into pieces or even powder. Jiang Ziya had experienced this amazing vitality in the Ji Deleted Space.
Looking at Jiang Ziya's busy appearance, Luo Nian raised the Skyring Staff lightly and said, "Destroy them."
As part of Luo Nian's power was drawn out, two huge mirrors appeared between heaven and earth. One pressed down from Luo Nian's feet, and the other rose up from the ground.
After a few breaths, the two mirrors stuck together and formed a thin line. The thin line quickly shortened and finally disappeared …
Of course, everything between the mirrors disappeared.
Jiang Ziya saw this and swallowed his saliva. "This ability is too domineering …"
Jiang Ziya had seen all kinds of heaven-defying methods in the Chaotic World, but compared to Luo Nian's Skyring Staff, they were nothing. The Skyring Staff was the rule above the rule.
After dealing with the tentacles, they continued their journey. They returned along the river and went all the way into the mountains.
Although they had left for a while, Luo Nian remembered the way very clearly. They climbed down a path from the mountainside and walked for hundreds of feet before stopping in front of a mountain wall.
"We're here," Luo Nian said.
Although Jiang Ziya was the one who dug the hole in Ji Deleted Space, Jiang Ziya did not have a soul lattice reincarnated, so he did not know the exact location. He had to use ropes to enter and exit.
Jiang Ziya followed Luo Nian's directions, but he did not know that it was a cold mountain wall. "Nian 'er, did you remember wrongly? The entrance is not here …"
"How is that possible?"
Luo Nian was puzzled.
He went forward to search, and his expression became more and more strange. "Impossible, there's a stone staircase here. You can enter the cave by stepping on it. The staircase is still there, but the cave is gone?"
Seeing Luo Nian so confident, Jiang Ziya realized that something was wrong.
The two went around the mountain wall and searched from top to bottom, but they still could not find the entrance!
"The entrance disappeared," Luo Nian said as he looked at the mountain wall.
"I've dug this entrance for a long time and it has never disappeared. Did we make a mistake?"
Jiang Ziya was still suspicious. Maybe they found the wrong mountain.
"Impossible!"
Luo Nian said solemnly. Then he jumped lightly and faced the mountain wall. "Since the Chaotic World is inside this mountain, I'll just blast open an entrance!"
"Swoosh!"
"Bang!"
"Rumble …"
Luo Nian now had the power of a top expert of the Yang race.
Although the punch was not at full strength, its power was still terrifying. The entire mountain range shook, and a huge crack appeared on the mountain wall in front of him. A huge hole appeared in the middle of the wall, but behind the hole was still mountain rocks.
The passage Luo Nian hoped to see was nowhere to be seen.

Chapter 3844
"What's going on …"
Luo Nian was very annoyed that he couldn't go back to his home.
Jiang Ziya went into the big hole and looked around. He broke off a handful of gravel at the edge of the crack.
After coming out of the hole, he handed the gravel to Luo Nian and said, "Use your Divine Sense to check if there's anything special about this stone …"
Luo Nian used his Divine Sense to check the gravel and shook his head. Then he used his Divine Sense to enter the big hole. Within the range of his Divine Sense, he still sensed stones, cracks, plants, and some insects hiding in the hole.
"I don't believe that such a big Chaotic World can run away!"
Luo Nian jumped up with his legs, and his body rose high into the sky.
"Whoosh …"
His Divine Sense spread out like invisible clouds, immersing in the forest, rocks, and water …
One mile, ten miles, a hundred miles …
Everything that his Divine Sense touched, no matter how big or small, was captured by him.
Soon after, Luo Nian opened his eyes and slowly descended. He said with worry, "I found seven Secondary Worlds within a hundred miles. One of them is the Poisonous Fog World that we entered."
"Then let's check them one by one," Jiang Ziya said.
Luo Nian and Jiang Ziya went back and forth in the forest. Because this place was located at the edge of the Main World, the seven Secondary Worlds were all developed and distributed widely.
The first Secondary World they entered was pitch-black. After traveling for a short while, they came out.
The second sub-world was filled with countless fog …
The third Secondary World …
After a few hours, they had checked six Secondary Worlds, and there was only one left.
After crossing a long and narrow mountain range, Luo Nian and Jiang Ziya went into a cave again.
The moment they stepped into the cave, a gust of cold air hit them in the face. The last Secondary World was a world of ice and snow.
"This is not a Chaotic World. Let's go," Luo Nian said.
But just as Jiang Ziya and Luo Nian retreated, there was a "peeling" sound from the entrance of the cave. It sounded like ice water being frozen at a high speed. They saw that the way in was blocked by a thick layer of ice, and there were shadows moving in the ice.
"A trap?"
Jiang Ziya was a little surprised. He had already grasped the Holy Whip in his hand.
"Pa pa pa pa …"
Countless golden whip shadows lashed out at the layer of ice, and crystal clear ice shards flew in all directions.
Jiang Ziya was very confident in his strength. After all, he was one of the top existences in the three-grid world. How could a mere layer of ice seal him?
However, the speed at which the ice was condensing was beyond Jiang Ziya's imagination. Under the lashing of the God-beating Whip, the ice became thicker and thicker.
Outside the ice layer.
Two gigantic figures stood at the entrance of the cave. These were two giants. They belonged to the Pang Hao Clan just like Bofan.
However, unlike Bofan, the two giants had purple rhombus crystals inserted into their backs. These purple rhombus crystals extended from the back of their heads all the way to their waists.
Both giants had a huge bag hanging on their waists, from which they took out dark blue ice talismans and slapped them onto the ice. Every time they slapped a talisman, a visible ripple would be produced, and the ice would thicken rapidly.
"Two rats still want to struggle out after entering the rat trap. Hehe, master was right," one of the giants said with a smile as he stuck to the wall.
"Their strength is not bad. What's more, they can move freely in the four-grid world. Luckily … master has loyal servants like us. We won't lose to them!"
The other giant was also happily sticking to the wall.
Jiang Ziya, who was in the secondary world, naturally did not know what was happening outside. Golden thorns began to appear on the surface of the God-beating Whip in his hand.
"Thorny Whip!"
"Ka ka ka …"
Jiang Ziya was using his full strength now.
Previously, he had relied on Luo Nian to deal with the tentacles. If he still needed Luo Nian's tentacles now, he would be embarrassed.
However, even though Jiang Ziya had squeezed out all his potential, the ice layer did not become thinner. Instead, it became thicker and thicker.
The Xuanliang World was filled with low-level creatures. Those small tribes did not have the ability to create such a sturdy ice layer. This did not make sense …
While Jiang Ziya was vexed, Luo Nian was already standing on the other side of the ice layer.
"Let me, senior …"
Luo Nian stretched out his huge hand and slammed it against the ice.
He did not use any technique. He just used all his strength.
The huge layer of ice shook, and the blue translucent layer of ice turned snow-white. That was the color of countless cracks. Then, with a boom, thousands of ice shards sprayed out, like a flood rushing out after a dam broke.
The two giants standing outside did not expect that they would be swept away by the ice chips while they were still concentrating on sticking the ice talisman.
The giants struggled to get up. They looked up and saw Luo Nian floating above their heads.
"Who sent you?
Tell me! "
Luo Nian asked coldly.
Luo Nian was very angry because he could not find the Chaotic World. The appearance of these two giants became his breakthrough.
The two giants looked at each other secretly.
The purple crystals on their backs flashed, and endless power poured into their bodies. The two giants, one on the left and one on the right, approached Luo Nian at an unimaginable speed.
One of the giants grabbed Luo Nian — even though Luo Nian's body had expanded by four times, he was still like a child in the giant's hands.
The other giant clenched his fists and attacked Luo Nian.
"Die, die, die, die!"
"Bam, bam, bam … …"
Under the stimulation of the purple caltrop crystal, the giant attacked Luo Nian as though he had lost his mind. The giant's body could not withstand the power bestowed by the purple caltrop crystal. After fifty to sixty punches, the giant's arms broke with a pop before he stopped attacking.
When they looked again, Luo Nian's body was unharmed and unharmed.
"Why is this guy … …"
"Is he a being from a three box world?"
Just as the two giants were puzzled, Luo Nian slowly raised the Skyring Staff. "Give them unbearable torture."
When he finished, the Skyring Staff took away a portion of Luo Nian's power and then gave off a strange string sound.
This string sound could easily reach the depths of the soul and then release the most terrifying illusion of pain … …
The two giants managed to hold on for a while and then started to roll on the ground and wail. Their heart-wrenching wails almost broke their throats. It could be seen how much pain the two giants were enduring.
Only when the two giants madly begged for mercy did Luo Nian wave the Skyring Staff and stop the string sound.
"I will give you one more chance. Who sent you?"
Luo Nian asked.
The two giants did not dare to hide anything. They kowtowed and answered at the same time, "It is Master. Master divined that you would come and had us set up rat traps here to wait for you to come!"
"Who is your master?"
Luo Nian asked again.
"Master is master. Who else can it be?"
One of the giants asked Luo Nian in puzzlement.
